guntner.dasm
      
                    include "nesdefs.dasm"
                    include "zp_vars.asm"
      
                    ;;;;; RAM NOTES
                    ; $000 zero page
                    ; $100 6502 stack
                    ; $200 PPU OAM sprite data
                    ; $300 entity data
                    ; $400-7ff sine scale tables
      
      01 50         phase_spawn_table	= $0150
      01 70         spare_stack		= $170
      01 e0         top_of_stack		= $1e0
      08 00         end_of_ram 		= $800
      
                    sprite_0_y	EQM	$b6 ; 182
                    sprite_0_y_diff	EQM	$4a ; 74
      
      
                    ;;;;; NES CARTRIDGE HEADER
      
00c5                NES_HEADER 0,1,1,NES_MIRR_VERT ; mapper 0, 1 PRGs, 1 CHR
                    seg Header
                    org $Bff0
                .NES_MAPPER SET 0
                .NES_PRG_BANKS SET 1
                .NES_CHR_BANKS SET 1
                .NES_MIRRORING SET NES_MIRR_VERT
bff0  byte          $4e,$45,$53,$1a
bff4  byte          .NES_PRG_BANKS
bff5  byte          .NES_CHR_BANKS
bff6  byte          .NES_MIRRORING|(.NES_MAPPER<<4)
bff7  byte          .NES_MAPPER&$f0
bff8  byte          0,0,0,0,0,0,0,0
                    seg Code
                    org $C000
      
      
                    ;;;;; START OF CODE
      
                cart_start: subroutine
                    NES_INIT ; set up stack pointer, turn off PPU
c000  78            sei
c001  d8            cld
c002  a2 ff         ldx #$ff
c004  9a            txs
c005  e8            inx
c006  8e 01 20      stx PPU_MASK
c009  8e 10 40      stx DMC_FREQ
c00c  8e 00 20      stx PPU_CTRL
c00f  2c 02 20      bit PPU_STATUS
c012  2c 15 40      bit APU_CHAN_CTRL
c015  a9 40         lda #$40
c017  8d 17 40      sta APU_FRAME
c01a  a9 0f         lda #$0F
c01c  8d 15 40      sta APU_CHAN_CTRL
      
                    ; CLEAR CPU RAM
c01f  20 15 c1      jsr ClearRAM ; clear RAM
c022  20 f5 fd      jsr WHOOPS
      
                    ; MY SETUP
                    ; setup rng
c025  a9 01         lda #$01
c027  85 26         sta rng0
c029  85 27         sta rng1
c02b  a9 ff         lda #$ff
c02d  85 28         sta rng2
c02f  85 43         sta dashboard_message
      
c031  a9 e8         lda #232
c033  85 03         sta scroll_y ; just seems right
      
c035  20 84 d4      jsr sine_init
      
c038  a9 ff         lda #>arctang_velocity_tables
c03a  85 85         sta arctang_velocity_hi
      
c03c  a9 c0         lda #$c0
c03e  85 30         sta scroll_to_counter
      
c040  a9 01         lda #1
c042  85 23         sta game_difficulty
c044  a9 01         lda #$01
c046  85 1a         sta player_speed
      
                    ; set default player colors
c048  a9 14         lda #$14
c04a  85 a9         sta player_color0
c04c  a9 21         lda #$21
c04e  85 aa         sta player_color1
c050  a9 37         lda #$37
c052  85 ab         sta player_color2
c054  20 48 f2      jsr player_update_colors
      
                    ; bookbinder colors
c057  a2 51         ldx #81
c059  a0 06         ldy #6
c05b  20 cf d1      jsr palette_load
      
      
                    ;============================== M O D E   I N I T   H E R E
c05e  a9 0f         lda #$0f
c060  85 90         sta pal_uni_bg
      
                    ;jsr game_init
c062  20 54 c6      jsr menu_screens_init
                    ;jsr attract_init
                    ;jsr cut_scene_intro_init
                    ;jsr cut_scene_outro_init
      
                    ;jsr options_screen_init
                    ;jsr sandbox_init
                    ;jsr sandbox2_init
      
      
                .endless
c065  4c 65 c0      jmp .endless ; endless loop
      
      
      
      
      
      
      
                    ;;;;; INTERRUPT HANDLERS
      
                nmi_handler: subroutine
      
                    ; ~2250 cycles for PPU access (PAL is 7450 cycles)
                    ; "On NTSC, count on being able to copy 160 bytes
                    ; to nametables or the palette using a moderately
                    ; unrolled loop"
                    ; write 64 tiles?
                    ; write all palettes
                    ; oam dma
      
                    SAVE_REGS ; 13 cycles
c068  48            pha
c069  8a            txa
c06a  48            pha
c06b  98            tya
c06c  48            pha
      
                    ; enable NMI lockout
c06d  a5 00         lda nmi_lockout
c06f  c9 00         cmp #$00
c071  f0 03         beq .no_lock
c073  4c 0f c1      jmp .nmi_end
                .no_lock
c076  e6 00         inc nmi_lockout
      
                    ; PPU vBLANK STUFF
                    ; RENDER va cycles
c078  a5 55         lda state_render_addr
c07a  4c f1 c4      jmp jump_to_subroutine
                state_render_done:
                    ; OAM DMA 513 cycles
c07d  a9 02         lda #$02
c07f  8d 14 40      sta PPU_OAM_DMA
                    ; PALETTE 236 cycles
c082  20 df d2      jsr palette_render
                    ; SCROLL POS 17 cycles
c085  2c 02 20      bit PPU_STATUS
c088  a5 01         lda scroll_x_hi
c08a  8d 05 20      sta PPU_SCROLL
c08d  a5 03         lda scroll_y
c08f  8d 05 20      sta PPU_SCROLL
                    ; NAMETABLE++ 9 cycles
c092  a5 04         lda scroll_page
c094  29 01         and #$01
c096  09 90         ora #CTRL_NMI|CTRL_BG_1000
c098  8d 00 20      sta PPU_CTRL
c09b  a5 09         lda ppu_mask_emph
c09d  49 18         eor #MASK_BG|MASK_SPR
c09f  8d 01 20      sta PPU_MASK
      
                    ; hope everything above was under
                    ; ~2250 cycles!
      
                    ; main state logic
c0a2  a5 56         lda state_update_addr
c0a4  4c f1 c4      jmp jump_to_subroutine
                state_update_done:
      
                sprite0_wait:
c0a7  a5 52         lda state_sprite0
c0a9  f0 30         beq sprite0_off
      
                    ; wait for Sprite 0; SPRITE 0 WAIT TIME!!!
c0ab  2c 02 20      .wait0 bit PPU_STATUS
c0ae  70 fb         bvs .wait0
c0b0  a9 c0         lda #$c0
c0b2  2c 02 20      .wait1 bit PPU_STATUS
c0b5  f0 fb         beq .wait1
      
                    ; HUD POSITIONING
                    ; Horizontal Scroll Solution
c0b7  2c 02 20      bit PPU_STATUS
c0ba  a9 00         lda #$00
c0bc  8d 05 20      sta PPU_SCROLL
c0bf  8d 05 20      sta PPU_SCROLL
                    ; Vertical Scroll Solution
                    ;lda #$26
                    ;sta PPU_ADDR
                    ;lda #$c0
                    ;sta PPU_ADDR
                    ;lda #$00
                    ;sta PPU_SCROLL
                    ;sta PPU_SCROLL
      
                    ; set bg pos to page 2 for dashboard
c0c2  a9 91         lda #$01|#CTRL_NMI|CTRL_BG_1000
c0c4  8d 00 20      sta PPU_CTRL
c0c7  a5 09         lda ppu_mask_emph
c0c9  49 08         eor #MASK_BG
c0cb  8d 01 20      sta PPU_MASK
      
      
      
                    ; must be game mode
c0ce  20 fc f3      jsr player_bullets_update
c0d1  20 96 d5      jsr starfield_update
      
                    ; HUD prepare for next draw
                    ; stop updating HUD if game over
c0d4  a5 65         lda player_death_flag
c0d6  d0 03         bne skip_dash_update
c0d8  20 c2 d0      jsr dashboard_update
                skip_dash_update:
                sprite0_off:
      
      
                    ; debug in lifebar space
      
                    ;lda phase_spawn_counter
                    ;jsr get_char_hi
                    ;sta $109
                    ;lda phase_spawn_counter
                    ;jsr get_char_lo
                    ;sta $10a
      
                    ;lda phase_kill_counter
                    ;jsr get_char_hi
                    ;sta $10c
                    ;lda phase_kill_counter
                    ;jsr get_char_lo
                    ;sta $10d
      
      
c0db  20 3e fb      jsr song_update
c0de  20 9d f7      jsr apu_update
      
c0e1  20 a6 f4      jsr player_controls_read
c0e4  20 3d d4      jsr powerup_bomb_update
c0e7  20 0e d4      jsr powerup_mushroom_update
c0ea  20 00 d2      jsr palette_update
      
      
c0ed  e6 24         inc wtf ; frame counter lo
c0ef  d0 02         bne .skip_ftw
c0f1  e6 25         inc ftw ; frame counter hi
                .skip_ftw
c0f3  20 44 d6      jsr timer_update ; update global time
c0f6  a5 26         lda rng0 ; rng updates
c0f8  20 5a c1      jsr NextRandom
c0fb  85 26         sta rng0
c0fd  a5 27         lda rng1
c0ff  20 60 c1      jsr PrevRandom
c102  85 27         sta rng1
c104  a5 28         lda rng2
c106  20 5a c1      jsr NextRandom
c109  85 28         sta rng2
      
                    ; disable NMI lockout
c10b  a9 00         lda #$00
c10d  85 00         sta nmi_lockout
      
                .nmi_end
                    RESTORE_REGS ; 16 cycles
c10f  68            pla
c110  a8            tay
c111  68            pla
c112  aa            tax
c113  68            pla
c114  40            rti
      
      
                    ; all includes
      
c115                include "ppu_lib_and_tables.asm"
                    ;nop ; how much prg space we using?
      
c621                include "state__handler.asm"

                    ;nop ; how much prg space we using?
ccc4                include "state_game.asm"
                    ;nop ; how much prg space we using?
      
                sandbox_init
                sandbox_update
                    ; include "state_sandbox.asm"
                    ;nop ; how much prg space we using?
                    ;include "state_sandbox2.asm"
                    ;nop ; how much prg space we using?

fffa                ;;;;; CPU VECTORS
                    seg Vectors ; segment "Vectors"
                    org $fffa ; start at address $fffa
fffa  word          nmi_handler ; $fffa vblank nmi
fffc  word          cart_start ; $fffc reset
fffe  word          nmi_handler ; $fffe irq / brk

nesdefs.dasm
      
                    processor 6502
      
                    ;;;;; CONSTANTS
      
      
      20 00         PPU_CTRL	= $2000
      20 01         PPU_MASK	= $2001
      20 02         PPU_STATUS	= $2002
      20 03         OAM_ADDR	= $2003
      20 04         OAM_DATA	= $2004
      20 05         PPU_SCROLL	= $2005
      20 06         PPU_ADDR	= $2006
      20 07         PPU_DATA	= $2007
      
      40 14         PPU_OAM_DMA	= $4014
      
      40 16         JOYPAD1		= $4016
      40 17         JOYPAD2		= $4017
      00 80         BUTTON_A      	= 1 << 7
      00 40         BUTTON_B   	= 1 << 6
      00 20         BUTTON_SELECT 	= 1 << 5
      00 10         BUTTON_START  	= 1 << 4
      00 08         BUTTON_UP     	= 1 << 3
      00 04         BUTTON_DOWN   	= 1 << 2
      00 02         BUTTON_LEFT   	= 1 << 1
      00 01         BUTTON_RIGHT  	= 1 << 0
      
                    ; NOTE: I've put this outside of the PPU & APU, because it is a feature
                    ; of the APU that is primarily of use to the PPU.
      40 14         OAM_DMA         = $4014
                    ; OAM local RAM copy goes from $0200-$02FF:
      02 00         OAM_RAM         = $0200
      
                    ; PPU_CTRL flags
                    CTRL_NMI	EQM %10000000	; Execute Non-Maskable Interrupt on VBlank
                    CTRL_8x8	EQM %00000000 	; Use 8x8 Sprites
                    CTRL_8x16	EQM %00100000 	; Use 8x16 Sprites
                    CTRL_BG_0000	EQM %00000000 	; Background Pattern Table at $0000 in VRAM
                    CTRL_BG_1000	EQM %00010000 	; Background Pattern Table at $1000 in VRAM
                    CTRL_SPR_0000	EQM %00000000 	; Sprite Pattern Table at $0000 in VRAM
                    CTRL_SPR_1000	EQM %00001000 	; Sprite Pattern Table at $1000 in VRAM
                    CTRL_INC_1	EQM %00000000 	; Increment PPU Address by 1 (Horizontal rendering)
                    CTRL_INC_32	EQM %00000100 	; Increment PPU Address by 32 (Vertical rendering)
                    CTRL_NT_2000	EQM %00000000 	; Name Table Address at $2000
                    CTRL_NT_2400	EQM %00000001 	; Name Table Address at $2400
                    CTRL_NT_2800	EQM %00000010 	; Name Table Address at $2800
                    CTRL_NT_2C00	EQM %00000011 	; Name Table Address at $2C00
      
                    ; PPU_MASK flags
                    MASK_TINT_RED	EQM %00100000	; Red Background
                    MASK_TINT_BLUE	EQM %01000000	; Blue Background
                    MASK_TINT_GREEN	EQM %10000000	; Green Background
                    MASK_SPR	EQM %00010000 	; Sprites Visible
                    MASK_BG		EQM %00001000 	; Backgrounds Visible
                    MASK_SPR_CLIP	EQM %00000100 	; Sprites clipped on left column
                    MASK_BG_CLIP	EQM %00000010 	; Background clipped on left column
                    MASK_COLOR	EQM %00000000 	; Display in Color
                    MASK_MONO	EQM %00000001 	; Display in Monochrome
      
                    ; read flags
                    F_BLANK		EQM %10000000 	; VBlank Active
                    F_SPRITE0	EQM %01000000 	; VBlank hit Sprite 0
                    F_SCAN8		EQM %00100000 	; More than 8 sprites on current scanline
                    F_WIGNORE	EQM %00010000 	; VRAM Writes currently ignored.
      
      
                    ;;;;; CARTRIDGE FILE HEADER
      
                    NES_MIRR_HORIZ	EQM 0
                    NES_MIRR_VERT	EQM 1
                    NES_MIRR_QUAD	EQM 8
      
                    MAC nes_header
                    seg Header
                    org $Bff0
                    .NES_MAPPER	SET {1}	;mapper number
                    .NES_PRG_BANKS	SET {2}	;number of 16K PRG banks, change to 2 for NROM256
                    .NES_CHR_BANKS	SET {3}	;number of 8K CHR banks (0 = RAM)
                    .NES_MIRRORING	SET {4}	;0 horizontal, 1 vertical, 8 four screen
                    byte $4e,$45,$53,$1a ; header
                    byte .NES_PRG_BANKS
                    byte .NES_CHR_BANKS
                    byte .NES_MIRRORING|(.NES_MAPPER<<4)
                    byte .NES_MAPPER&$f0
                    byte 0,0,0,0,0,0,0,0 ; reserved, set to zero
                    seg Code
                    org $C000
                ENDM
      
                    ;;;;; NES_INIT SETUP MACRO (place at start)
      
                    MAC nes_init
                    sei ;disable IRQs
                    cld ;decimal mode not supported
                    ldx #$ff
                    txs ;set up stack pointer
                    inx ;increment X to 0
                    stx PPU_MASK ;disable rendering
                    stx DMC_FREQ ;disable DMC interrupts
                    stx PPU_CTRL ;disable NMI interrupts
                    bit PPU_STATUS ;clear VBL flag
                    bit APU_CHAN_CTRL ;ack DMC IRQ bit 7
                    lda #$40
                    sta APU_FRAME ;disable APU Frame IRQ
                    lda #$0F
                    sta APU_CHAN_CTRL ;disable DMC, enable/init other channels.
                ENDM
      
                    ;;;;; NES_VECTORS - CPU vectors at end of address space
      
                    MAC nes_vectors
                    seg Vectors ; segment "Vectors"
                    org $fffa ; start at address $fffa
                    .word NMIHandler ; $fffa vblank nmi
                    .word Start ; $fffc reset
                    .word NMIHandler ; $fffe irq / brk
                ENDM
      
                    ;;;;; PPU_SETADDR <address> - set 16-bit PPU address
      
                    MAC ppu_setaddr
                    lda #>{1} ; upper byte
                    sta PPU_ADDR
                    lda #<{1} ; lower byte
                    sta PPU_ADDR
                ENDM
      
                    ;;;;; PPU_SETVALUE <value> - feed 8-bit value to PPU
      
                    MAC ppu_setvalue
                    lda #{1}
                    sta PPU_DATA
                ENDM
      
                    ;;;;; SAVE_REGS - save A/X/Y registers
      
                    MAC save_regs
                pha
                txa
                pha
                tya
                pha
                ENDM
      
                    ;;;;; RESTORE_REGS - restore Y/X/A registers
      
                    MAC restore_regs
                pla
                tay
                pla
                tax
                pla
                ENDM
      
      

zp_vars.asm

ppu_lib_and_tables.asm
      
                    ;;;;; SUBROUTINES
      
                ClearRAM: subroutine
c115  a9 00         lda #0 ; A = 0
c117  aa            tax ; X = 0
                .clearRAM
c118  95 00         sta $0,x ; clear $0-$ff
c11a  e0 fe         cpx #$fe ; last 2 bytes of stack?
c11c  b0 03         bcs .skipStack ; don't clear it
c11e  9d 00 01      sta $100,x ; clear $100-$1fd
                .skipStack
c121  9d 00 02      sta $200,x ; clear $200-$2ff
c124  9d 00 03      sta $300,x ; clear $300-$3ff
c127  9d 00 04      sta $400,x ; clear $400-$4ff
c12a  9d 00 05      sta $500,x ; clear $500-$5ff
c12d  9d 00 06      sta $600,x ; clear $600-$6ff
c130  9d 00 07      sta $700,x ; clear $700-$7ff
c133  e8            inx ; X = X + 1
c134  d0 e2         bne .clearRAM ; loop 256 times
c136  60            rts
      
      
                do_nothing: subroutine
c137  60            rts
      
                    ; disable PPU drawing and NMI
                render_enable:
c138  a9 18         lda #MASK_BG|MASK_SPR
c13a  8d 01 20      sta PPU_MASK
c13d  a9 90         lda #CTRL_NMI|CTRL_BG_1000
c13f  8d 00 20      sta PPU_CTRL
c142  60            rts
      
                render_disable:
c143  a9 00         lda #$00
c145  8d 01 20      sta PPU_MASK
c148  8d 00 20      sta PPU_CTRL
c14b  60            rts
      
                    ; wait for VSYNC to start
                WaitSync:
c14c  2c 02 20      bit PPU_STATUS
c14f  10 fb         bpl WaitSync
c151  60            rts
      
      
                    ;;;;; RANDOM NUMBERS
      
                get_next_random: subroutine
c152  a5 26         lda rng0
c154  20 5a c1      jsr NextRandom
c157  85 26         sta rng0
c159  60            rts
      
                NextRandom subroutine
c15a  4a            lsr
c15b  90 02         bcc .NoEor
c15d  49 d4         eor #$d4
                .NoEor:
c15f  60            rts
                    ; Get previous random value
                PrevRandom subroutine
c160  0a            asl
c161  90 02         bcc .NoEor
c163  49 a9         eor #$a9
                .NoEor:
c165  60            rts
      
      
                shake_8: subroutine
                    ; returns rng in -4..3 range ready for addition
c166  20 52 c1      jsr get_next_random
c169  4a            lsr
c16a  29 07         and #$07
c16c  38            sec
c16d  e9 04         sbc #$04
c16f  18            clc
c170  60            rts
      
      
      
                    tile_empty 		EQM $03
      
                nametables_clear:
                    ; tiles
c171  a2 07         ldx #$07
c173  a0 00         ldy #$00
                    PPU_SETADDR $2000
c175  a9 20         lda #>$2000
c177  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
c17a  a9 00         lda #<$2000
c17c  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
c17f  a9 03         lda #tile_empty
                .page_loop
                .byte_loop
c181  8d 07 20      sta PPU_DATA
c184  c8            iny
c185  d0 fa         bne .byte_loop
c187  ca            dex
c188  10 f7         bpl .page_loop
                    ; attributes
c18a  a0 00         ldy #0
                    PPU_SETADDR $23c0
c18c  a9 23         lda #>$23c0
c18e  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
c191  a9 c0         lda #<$23c0
c193  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
c196  a2 c0         ldx #$c0
                .nm1_attr_loop
c198  8c 07 20      sty PPU_DATA
c19b  e8            inx
c19c  d0 fa         bne .nm1_attr_loop
                    PPU_SETADDR $27c0
c19e  a9 27         lda #>$27c0
c1a0  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
c1a3  a9 c0         lda #<$27c0
c1a5  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
c1a8  a2 c0         ldx #$c0
                .nm2_attr_loop
c1aa  8c 07 20      sty PPU_DATA
c1ad  e8            inx
c1ae  d0 fa         bne .nm2_attr_loop
c1b0  60            rts
      
      
                sprite_clear:
c1b1  a9 ff         lda #$ff
c1b3  a2 00         ldx #$00
                .clear_sprite_ram
c1b5  9d 00 02      sta oam_ram_y,x ; PPU OAM sprite data
c1b8  e8            inx
c1b9  d0 fa         bne .clear_sprite_ram
c1bb  60            rts
      
                ppu_mess_emph: subroutine
c1bc  a5 28         lda rng2
c1be  29 e0         and #%11100000
c1c0  85 09         sta ppu_mask_emph
c1c2  60            rts
      
                    ; HEX views
                get_char_hi: subroutine
                    ; a = value in / hex char out
c1c3  4a            lsr
c1c4  4a            lsr
c1c5  4a            lsr
c1c6  4a            lsr
c1c7  18            clc
c1c8  69 60         adc #char_set_offset
c1ca  60            rts
                get_char_lo: subroutine
                    ; a = value in / hex char out
c1cb  29 0f         and #$0f
c1cd  18            clc
c1ce  69 60         adc #char_set_offset
c1d0  60            rts
      
      
                    ;;;;; namtetable_tile_planter
                    ;;;;; data set address <address> - set 16-bit PPU address
      
                    MAC nmtp_setaddr
                    lda #>{1} ; upper byte
                    sta nametable_tile_planter_addr_hi
                    lda #<{1} ; lower byte
                    sta nametable_tile_planter_addr_lo
                ENDM
      
                nametable_tile_planter:
c1d1  a0 00         ldy #$00
c1d3  b1 57         lda (nametable_tile_planter_addr_lo),y ; upper byte
                .tileset_loop
c1d5  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
c1d8  c8            iny
c1d9  b1 57         lda (nametable_tile_planter_addr_lo),y ; lower byte
c1db  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
c1de  c8            iny
                .string_loop
c1df  b1 57         lda (nametable_tile_planter_addr_lo),y ; read string
c1e1  c8            iny
c1e2  c9 00         cmp #$00
c1e4  f0 05         beq .terminate_string
c1e6  8d 07 20      sta PPU_DATA
c1e9  d0 f4         bne .string_loop
                .terminate_string
c1eb  b1 57         lda (nametable_tile_planter_addr_lo),y ; look for ff
c1ed  c9 ff         cmp #$ff
c1ef  d0 e4         bne .tileset_loop
c1f1  60            rts

                jump_to_subroutine: subroutine
                    ; a = jump table offset
                    ; caches x and leaves y alone

state__handler.asm
      
                    ; === STATE MACHINE ===
      
      
      
                    ; nmi order of operations :
      
                    ; jmp() to state renderer
                    ; most likely starfield mode
                    ; jmp to dashboard
                    ; jmp back
                    ; OAM DMA
                    ; palette transfer
                    ; set scroll pos
      
                    ; jmp() to state logic
                    ; jmp back
      
                    ; check flag sprite0 and if set :
                    ; wait for sprite 0
                    ; set scroll pos
                    ; (palette hackery?)
      
                    ; apu manage
                    ; starfield manage jmp()?
                    ; palette manage
      
      
      
                    ; init functions order of operations :
      
                    ; disable rendering and nmi
                    ; (halt audio?)
                    ; update vram
                    ; wait for vsync/vblank
                    ; reenable rendering and nmi
      
      
                state_clear:
c621  a2 07         ldx #$07
c623  a9 00         lda #$00
                .loop
c625  95 4a         sta state_v0,x
c627  ca            dex
c628  10 fb         bpl .loop
c62a  60            rts
      
      
      
                state_render_do_nothing:
c62b  4c 7d c0      jmp state_render_done
      
                state_update_do_nothing:
c62e  4c a7 c0      jmp state_update_done
      
      
      
                state_sprite0_enable:
c631  a9 01         lda #$01
c633  8d 03 02      sta oam_ram_x
c636  a9 b6         lda #sprite_0_y
c638  8d 00 02      sta oam_ram_y
c63b  a9 ff         lda #$ff
c63d  8d 01 02      sta oam_ram_spr
c640  a9 20         lda #$20
c642  8d 02 02      sta oam_ram_att
c645  a9 ff         lda #$ff
c647  85 52         sta state_sprite0
c649  60            rts
      
                state_sprite0_disable:
c64a  a9 ff         lda #$ff
c64c  8d 00 02      sta oam_ram_y
c64f  a9 00         lda #$00
c651  85 52         sta state_sprite0
c653  60            rts

state_menu_screens.asm
                    ; zp vars
      
                    ;title_rudy_pos  EQU $181
      
                    ;options_rudy_pos EQU $182
                    ;options_music_on EQU $183
                    ;options_song_id  EQU $184
                    ;options_sound_id EQU $185
                    ;options_rudy_color1 EQU $186
                    ;options_rudy_color2 EQU $187
      
                    ;scroll_to_counter EQU $188
      
      
                menu_screens_init: subroutine
      
c654  20 43 c1      jsr render_disable
c657  20 71 c1      jsr nametables_clear
c65a  20 b1 c1      jsr sprite_clear
c65d  20 4a c6      jsr state_sprite0_disable
      
c660  a9 82         lda #state_render_jump_table_offset+1
c662  85 55         sta state_render_addr
c664  a9 87         lda #state_update_jump_table_offset+1
c666  85 56         sta state_update_addr
c668  20 21 c6      jsr state_clear ; a = 0
c66b  85 01         sta scroll_x_hi
c66d  85 04         sta scroll_page
c66f  85 22         sta player_damage_flash ; no flash on fade-in
c671  85 09         sta ppu_mask_emph ; reset mask
c673  20 95 d6      jsr timer_reset
c676  20 d5 c7      jsr title_screen_set_rudy_y
                    ; setup colors
c679  a2 12         ldx #18
c67b  a0 00         ldy #0
c67d  20 cf d1      jsr palette_load
c680  a9 0f         lda #$0f
c682  85 90         sta pal_uni_bg
                    ; setup everything else
c684  20 17 f2      jsr player_game_reset
c687  20 7c fb      jsr song_stop
c68a  20 65 cf      jsr dashboard_init
c68d  20 c2 d0      jsr dashboard_update
c690  20 75 d0      jsr dashboard_render
      
                    ; copylines pal att
                    PPU_SETADDR $23c0
c693  a9 23         lda #>$23c0
c695  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
c698  a9 c0         lda #<$23c0
c69a  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
c69d  a2 37         ldx #$37
                .copylines_pal_loop
c69f  8a            txa
c6a0  c9 30         cmp #$30
c6a2  b0 05         bcs .bookbinder_gray
c6a4  8a            txa
c6a5  29 30         and #$30
c6a7  d0 04         bne .on8
                .bookbinder_gray
c6a9  a9 0a         lda #%00001010
c6ab  d0 02         bne .plot_pal
                .on8
c6ad  a9 00         lda #$00000000
                .plot_pal
c6af  8d 07 20      sta PPU_DATA
c6b2  ca            dex
c6b3  10 ea         bpl .copylines_pal_loop
      
                    ; G u n T n e R
      
                    ; BIG TITLE
                    PPU_SETADDR $2060
c6b5  a9 20         lda #>$2060
c6b7  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
c6ba  a9 60         lda #<$2060
c6bc  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
c6bf  a0 00         ldy #$00
                big_title_loop:
c6c1  b9 f1 c7      lda guntner_title_name_table,y
c6c4  8d 07 20      sta PPU_DATA
c6c7  c8            iny
c6c8  d0 f7         bne big_title_loop
      
                    ; pinline on title screen
                    PPU_SETADDR $22c0
c6ca  a9 22         lda #>$22c0
c6cc  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
c6cf  a9 c0         lda #<$22c0
c6d1  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
c6d4  a9 b1         lda #$b1
c6d6  a0 20         ldy #$20
                .pinline_footer
c6d8  8d 07 20      sta PPU_DATA
c6db  88            dey
c6dc  d0 fa         bne .pinline_footer
      
                    ; pinline on options screen
                    PPU_SETADDR $2420
c6de  a9 24         lda #>$2420
c6e0  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
c6e3  a9 20         lda #<$2420
c6e5  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
c6e8  a9 b1         lda #$b1
c6ea  a0 20         ldy #$20
                .pinline_header
c6ec  8d 07 20      sta PPU_DATA
c6ef  88            dey
c6f0  d0 fa         bne .pinline_header
      
                    ; various stuff on screen
                    NMTP_SETADDR menu_screen_tile_data
c6f2  a9 c2         lda #>menu_screen_tile_data
c6f4  85 58         sta nametable_tile_planter_addr_hi
c6f6  a9 7a         lda #<menu_screen_tile_data
c6f8  85 57         sta nametable_tile_planter_addr_lo
c6fa  20 d1 c1      jsr nametable_tile_planter
      
                    ; set rudy stuff
c6fd  20 af c7      ` jsr menus_position_rudy
c700  20 98 f2      jsr set_player_sprite
      
c703  20 4e d2      jsr palette_fade_in_init
c706  20 76 fa      jsr sfx_rng_chord
      
                    ; turn ppu back on
c709  20 38 c1      jsr render_enable
c70c  60            rts
      
      
      
                    ; vBLANK RENDER HANDLER
      
                menu_screens_render:
                    ; pretty much just for options screen
                    ; show song id
                    PPU_SETADDR $2514
c70d  a9 25         lda #>$2514
c70f  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
c712  a9 14         lda #<$2514
c714  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
c717  a9 60         lda #char_set_offset
c719  8d 07 20      sta PPU_DATA
c71c  a5 2c         lda options_song_id
c71e  20 cb c1      jsr get_char_lo
c721  8d 07 20      sta PPU_DATA
                    ; show sound id
                    PPU_SETADDR $2554
c724  a9 25         lda #>$2554
c726  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
c729  a9 54         lda #<$2554
c72b  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
c72e  a5 2d         lda options_sound_id
c730  20 c3 c1      jsr get_char_hi
c733  8d 07 20      sta PPU_DATA

                game_update_generic: subroutine
cd18  a5 0b         lda player_health
cd1a  d0 7d         bne .player_not_dead
                .player_dead_anim
cd1c  a5 65         lda player_death_flag
cd1e  d0 39         bne .death_already_set
cd20  e6 65         inc player_death_flag
cd22  20 7c fb      jsr song_stop
cd25  a5 06         lda scroll_speed_hi
cd27  85 08         sta scroll_cache_hi
cd29  a5 05         lda scroll_speed_lo
cd2b  85 07         sta scroll_cache_lo
cd2d  c6 0c         dec player_lives
cd2f  d0 1d         bne .youdead
                    ; attract mode has different behaviour
cd31  a5 0e         lda attract_true
cd33  c9 ff         cmp #$ff
cd35  f0 17         beq .youdead
                    ; set to grayscale
cd37  a9 01         lda #%00000001
cd39  85 09         sta ppu_mask_emph
                    ; cinematics
cd3b  85 06         sta scroll_speed_hi
cd3d  a9 05         lda #song_game_over
cd3f  20 4b fb      jsr song_start
cd42  a9 79         lda #121
cd44  85 67         sta you_dead_counter
                    ; "GAME OVER"
cd46  a0 10         ldy #$10
cd48  20 66 d0      jsr dashboard_message_set
cd4b  4c b8 cd      jmp .done
                .youdead
cd4e  20 85 f8      jsr sfx_player_death
                    ; "YOU DEAD"
cd51  a0 00         ldy #$00
cd53  20 66 d0      jsr dashboard_message_set
cd56  4c b8 cd      jmp .done
                .death_already_set
cd59  20 c5 d5      jsr death_scroll_speed
cd5c  e6 67         inc you_dead_counter
cd5e  a5 67         lda you_dead_counter
cd60  c9 50         cmp #80
cd62  f0 0a         beq .trigger_fadeout
cd64  c9 78         cmp #120
cd66  d0 50         bne .done
                    ; next life
cd68  a5 0c         lda player_lives
cd6a  d0 14         bne .next_life
                    ; no lives left game over
                    ; GO BACK TO TITLE SCREEN AFTER DEATH SEQUENCE
cd6c  f0 4a         beq .done
                .trigger_fadeout
                    ; demo mode has different behaviour
cd6e  a5 0e         lda attract_true
cd70  c9 ff         cmp #$ff
cd72  f0 04         beq .return_to_title_screen
                    ; do not return to title if game is not over
cd74  a5 0c         lda player_lives
cd76  d0 40         bne .done
                .return_to_title_screen
                    ; GO BACK TO TITLE SCREEN
                    ; AFTER DEATH SEQUENCE
cd78  a9 7c         lda #state_init_jump_table_offset+0
cd7a  20 82 d2      jsr palette_fade_out_init
cd7d  4c b8 cd      jmp .done
                .next_life
cd80  20 81 fb      jsr song_unstop
                    ; reset health
cd83  a9 ff         lda #$ff
cd85  85 0b         sta player_health
                    ; turn on iframes
cd87  a9 78         lda #120
cd89  85 53         sta state_iframes
                    ; reset death sequence timers
cd8b  a9 00         lda #$00
cd8d  85 65         sta player_death_flag
cd8f  85 67         sta you_dead_counter
                    ; revert scroll speed
cd91  a5 08         lda scroll_cache_hi
cd93  85 06         sta scroll_speed_hi
cd95  a5 07         lda scroll_cache_lo
cd97  85 05         sta scroll_speed_lo
      
                .player_not_dead
      
                .healing_with_time
cd99  a5 0b         lda player_health
cd9b  c9 ff         cmp #$ff
cd9d  f0 0b         beq .no_heal
cd9f  a5 0d         lda player_heal_c
cda1  18            clc
cda2  69 23         adc #$23 ; speed of heal counter increment
cda4  85 0d         sta player_heal_c
cda6  90 02         bcc .no_heal
                .reset_heal_counter
cda8  e6 0b         inc player_health
                .no_heal
      
                player_change_speed:
cdaa  a5 1d         lda player_controls_debounced
cdac  29 20         and #BUTTON_SELECT
cdae  f0 08         beq .read_select_done
                    ; update speed
cdb0  e6 1a         inc player_speed
cdb2  a5 1a         lda player_speed
cdb4  29 03         and #$03
cdb6  85 1a         sta player_speed
                .read_select_done
      
      
                .done
                    ; player collision update
cdb8  a6 13         ldx player_x_hi
cdba  e8            inx
cdbb  e8            inx
cdbc  86 17         stx player_coll_x
cdbe  a6 15         ldx player_y_hi
cdc0  e8            inx
cdc1  e8            inx
cdc2  86 18         stx player_coll_y
      
cdc4  20 56 db      jsr enemies_update_all
cdc7  20 98 f2      jsr set_player_sprite
cdca  60            rts
      
      
      
      
      
                game_update: subroutine
                    ; check for (un)pausings
cdcb  a5 63         lda phase_end_game
cdcd  c9 01         cmp #$01
cdcf  f0 28         beq .read_start_done
cdd1  a5 1d         lda player_controls_debounced
cdd3  29 10         and #BUTTON_START
cdd5  f0 22         beq .read_start_done
cdd7  a5 1b         lda player_paused
cdd9  c9 00         cmp #$00
cddb  f0 0e         beq .pause
                .unpause
cddd  a9 00         lda #$00
cddf  85 1b         sta player_paused
cde1  a9 ff         lda #$ff
cde3  85 43         sta dashboard_message
cde5  20 81 fb      jsr song_unstop
cde8  4c f9 cd      jmp .read_start_done
                .pause
cdeb  a9 ff         lda #$ff
cded  85 1b         sta player_paused
cdef  a9 30         lda #$30
cdf1  85 43         sta dashboard_message
cdf3  20 7c fb      jsr song_stop
cdf6  20 76 fa      jsr sfx_rng_chord
                .read_start_done
                    ; test for paused
cdf9  a5 1b         lda player_paused
cdfb  d0 12         bne .done_and_paused
      
cdfd  20 a4 d6      jsr phase_handler
ce00  20 18 cd      jsr game_update_generic
      
ce03  a5 0b         lda player_health

ce07  f0 06         beq .done_and_paused
      
ce09  20 be f4      jsr player_move_position
ce0c  20 b8 f3      jsr player_bullets_check_controls
                .done_and_paused
ce0f  4c a7 c0      jmp state_update_done
      

arctang.asm
                    ; quadrants
                    ;
                    ;  0 | 1
                    ;  --+--
                    ;  2 | 3
      
                    ; 24 angular regions total (0-23)
                    ; 6 regions per quadrant
                    ;  direction
                    ; 12 o'clock  6
                    ;  3 o'clock  0
                    ;  6 o'clock  18
                    ;  9 o'clock  12
      
                enemy_get_direction_of_player:
                    ; references these zp vars
                    ; collision_0_x
                    ; collision_0_y
                    ; player_x_hi
                    ; player_y_hi
                    ; uses these zp vars
                    ; collision_0_w = x delta
                    ; collision_0_h = y delta
                    ; collision_1_x = "small"
                    ; collision_1_y = "large"
                    ; collision_1_h = "half"
                    ; temp00   = quadrant
                    ; temp01   = region
                    ; temp02  = x register stash
                    ; temp03  = y register stash
                    ; returns a = direction (or region)
ce12  86 88         stx temp02
ce14  84 89         sty temp03
                    ; find quadrant
ce16  a9 00         lda #0
ce18  85 86         sta temp00
                    ; y quadrants
ce1a  a5 15         lda player_y_hi
ce1c  38            sec
ce1d  e5 3c         sbc collision_0_y
ce1f  90 07         bcc .top_quadrant
                .bottom_quadrant
ce21  e6 86         inc temp00
ce23  e6 86         inc temp00
ce25  4c 2d ce      jmp .y_quadrant_done
                .top_quadrant
ce28  a5 3c         lda collision_0_y
ce2a  38            sec
ce2b  e5 15         sbc player_y_hi
                .y_quadrant_done
ce2d  85 3e         sta collision_0_h
ce2f  a8            tay
                    ; x quadrants
ce30  a5 13         lda player_x_hi
ce32  38            sec
ce33  e5 3b         sbc collision_0_x
ce35  90 05         bcc .left_quadrant
                .right_quadrant
ce37  e6 86         inc temp00
ce39  4c 41 ce      jmp .x_quadrant_done
                .left_quadrant
ce3c  a5 3b         lda collision_0_x
ce3e  38            sec
ce3f  e5 13         sbc player_x_hi
                .x_quadrant_done
ce41  85 3d         sta collision_0_w
ce43  aa            tax
                    ; quadrant is now in temp00
                    ; start finding that region
ce44  e4 3e         cpx collision_0_h
ce46  b0 0a         bcs .x_greater_or_equal_y
                .x_less_than_y
ce48  a9 10         lda #16
ce4a  85 87         sta temp01
ce4c  86 3f         stx collision_1_x
ce4e  84 40         sty collision_1_y
ce50  d0 08         bne .determine_region
                .x_greater_or_equal_y
ce52  a9 00         lda #0
ce54  85 87         sta temp01
ce56  86 40         stx collision_1_y
ce58  84 3f         sty collision_1_x
                .determine_region
ce5a  a5 3f         lda collision_1_x
ce5c  4a            lsr
ce5d  85 42         sta collision_1_h
ce5f  a5 3f         lda collision_1_x
ce61  0a            asl
ce62  b0 09         bcs .q_smaller
ce64  18            clc
ce65  65 42         adc collision_1_h
ce67  b0 04         bcs .q_smaller
ce69  c5 40         cmp collision_1_y
ce6b  90 0d         bcc .q_larger
                .q_smaller ; S * 2.5 > L
ce6d  46 42         lsr collision_1_h
ce6f  a5 3f         lda collision_1_x
ce71  18            clc
ce72  65 42         adc collision_1_h
ce74  c5 40         cmp collision_1_y
ce76  90 18         bcc .region1
ce78  b0 11         bcs .region0
                .q_larger ; S * 2.5 < L
ce7a  a5 3f         lda collision_1_x
ce7c  0a            asl
ce7d  0a            asl
ce7e  0a            asl
ce7f  b0 16         bcs .region2
ce81  38            sec
ce82  e5 42         sbc collision_1_h
ce84  c5 40         cmp collision_1_y
ce86  90 16         bcc .region3
ce88  4c 97 ce      jmp .region2
                .region0 ; L / S < 1.25 ; d = 3,9,15,21
ce8b  a5 87         lda temp01
ce8d  18            clc
ce8e  30 13         bmi .result_lookup
                .region1 ; 1.25 < L / S < 2.5 ; d = 2,4,8,10,14,16,20,22
ce90  a5 87         lda temp01
ce92  18            clc
ce93  69 04         adc #4
ce95  10 0c         bpl .result_lookup
                .region2 ; 2.5 < L / S < 7.5 ; d = 1,5,7,11,13,17,19,23
ce97  a5 87         lda temp01
ce99  18            clc
ce9a  69 08         adc #8
ce9c  10 05         bpl .result_lookup
                .region3 ; 7.5 < L / S ; d = 0,6,12,18
ce9e  a5 87         lda temp01
cea0  18            clc
cea1  69 0c         adc #12
                .result_lookup
                    ; temp01 should be ready in the accumulator
cea3  65 86         adc temp00
cea5  aa            tax
cea6  bd 00 ff      lda ARCTANG_TRANSLATION_LOOKUP_TABLE,x
                    ; restore enemy_ram_offset
cea9  a6 88         ldx temp02
ceab  a4 89         ldy temp03
cead  60            rts
      
      
      
      
                arctang_bound_dir: subroutine
                    ; a = value to be bounded
                    ; returns value 0..23 in a
ceae  10 04         bpl .check_above_24
                .below_0
ceb0  18            clc
ceb1  69 18         adc #24
ceb3  60            rts
                .check_above_24
ceb4  c9 18         cmp #24
ceb6  90 03         bcc .bounded
ceb8  38            sec
ceb9  e9 18         sbc #24
                .bounded
cebb  60            rts
      
      
      
      
                    ; arctang movement is 16-bit
                    ; oam ram x,y = high byte
                    ; enemy ram x,y = low byte
      
                arctang_update_x: subroutine
                    ; temp00 = hi
                    ; temp01 = lo
                    ; temp02 = region
cebc  a6 88         ldx temp02
cebe  bd 20 ff      lda ARCTANG_REGION_TO_X_VELOCITY_TABLE,x
cec1  0a            asl
cec2  a8            tay
cec3  bd 50 ff      lda ARCTANG_REGION_X_PLUS_OR_MINUS_TABLE,x
cec6  10 0c         bpl arctang_16bit_maths
      
                arctang_update_y: subroutine
                    ; temp00 = hi
                    ; temp01 = lo
                    ; temp02 = region
cec8  a6 88         ldx temp02
ceca  bd 38 ff      lda ARCTANG_REGION_TO_Y_VELOCITY_TABLE,x
cecd  0a            asl
cece  a8            tay
cecf  bd 68 ff      lda ARCTANG_REGION_Y_PLUS_OR_MINUS_TABLE,x
ced2  10 00         bpl arctang_16bit_maths
      
                arctang_16bit_maths: subroutine
                    ; a = plus or minus
                    ; y = velocity table offset
ced4  d0 11         bne .velocity_add
                .velocity_sub
                    ; lo byte
ced6  a5 87         lda temp01
ced8  38            sec
ced9  f1 84         sbc (arctang_velocity_lo),y
cedb  85 87         sta temp01
                    ; hi byte
cedd  a5 86         lda temp00
cedf  c8            iny
cee0  f1 84         sbc (arctang_velocity_lo),y
cee2  85 86         sta temp00
cee4  4c f5 ce      jmp .done_x
                .velocity_add
                    ; lo byte
cee7  a5 87         lda temp01
cee9  18            clc
ceea  71 84         adc (arctang_velocity_lo),y
ceec  85 87         sta temp01
                    ; hi byte
ceee  a5 86         lda temp00
cef0  c8            iny
cef1  71 84         adc (arctang_velocity_lo),y
cef3  85 86         sta temp00
                .done_x
cef5  60            rts
      
      
      
      
                arctang_enemy_update:
                    ; updates both x and y position given:
                    ; - enemy_ram_ex holds region/direction
                    ; - enemy_ram_x/y holds lo bytes
                    ; - oam_ram_x/y holds hi bytes
      
                    ; set region for arctang
cef6  bd 07 03      lda enemy_ram_ex,x
cef9  85 88         sta temp02 ; region
      
                    ; set x byte for arctang
cefb  b9 03 02      lda oam_ram_x,y
cefe  85 86         sta temp00 ; hi byte
cf00  bd 02 03      lda enemy_ram_x,x
cf03  85 87         sta temp01 ; lo byte
cf05  20 bc ce      jsr arctang_update_x
cf08  a4 77         ldy enemy_oam_offset
cf0a  a5 86         lda temp00
cf0c  99 03 02      sta oam_ram_x,y
cf0f  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
cf11  a5 87         lda temp01
cf13  9d 02 03      sta enemy_ram_x,x
      
                    ; set y byte for arctang
cf16  b9 00 02      lda oam_ram_y,y
cf19  85 86         sta temp00 ; hi byte
cf1b  bd 03 03      lda enemy_ram_y,x
cf1e  85 87         sta temp01 ; lo byte
cf20  20 c8 ce      jsr arctang_update_y
cf23  a4 77         ldy enemy_oam_offset
cf25  a5 86         lda temp00
cf27  20 ff da      jsr enemy_fix_y_visible
cf2a  99 00 02      sta oam_ram_y,y
cf2d  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
cf2f  a5 87         lda temp01
cf31  9d 03 03      sta enemy_ram_y,x
      
cf34  60            rts

collision_detection.dasm
      
                    ;;;; COLLISION DETECTOR
                    ;
                    ; works by putting object data into the following 0 page variables
                    ;
                    ; collision_0_x byte
                    ; collision_0_y byte
                    ; collision_0_w byte
                    ; collision_0_h byte
                    ; collision_1_x byte
                    ; collision_1_y byte
                    ; collision_1_w byte
                    ; collision_1_h byte
                    ;
                    ; returns ff (true) or 00 (false) in accumulator
      
                    ; the detect_collision subroutine was moved to player_bullets.asm
                    ; saving over 12 cycles per usage
      
      
      
                player_collision_detect: subroutine
                    ; returns true/false in a
cf35  a5 53         lda state_iframes
cf37  d0 29         bne .no_collision
cf39  18            clc
cf3a  a5 3b         lda collision_0_x
cf3c  65 3d         adc collision_0_w
cf3e  b0 22         bcs .no_collision ; make sure x+w is not less than x
cf40  c5 13         cmp player_x_hi
cf42  90 1e         bcc .no_collision
cf44  18            clc
cf45  a5 17         lda player_coll_x
cf47  69 0b         adc #$0b ; player width
cf49  c5 3b         cmp collision_0_x
cf4b  90 15         bcc .no_collision
cf4d  18            clc
cf4e  a5 3c         lda collision_0_y
cf50  65 3e         adc collision_0_h
cf52  c5 18         cmp player_coll_y
cf54  90 0c         bcc .no_collision
cf56  18            clc
cf57  a5 18         lda player_coll_y
cf59  69 03         adc #$03 ; player height
cf5b  c5 3c         cmp collision_0_y
cf5d  90 03         bcc .no_collision
                .collision
cf5f  a9 ff         lda #$ff
cf61  60            rts
                .no_collision
cf62  a9 00         lda #$00
cf64  60            rts

dashboard.asm
                    ;;; constants
      
      22 c0         dash_page1_top_bar	= $22c0
      26 c0         dash_page2_top_bar	= $26c0
      
      27 06         speed_addr		= $2706
      27 08         life_bar_addr		= $2708
      27 1c         lives_addr		= $271c
      27 48         phase_addr		= $2748
      27 4c         score_addr		= $274c
      27 56         timer_addr		= $2756
      
      01 00         dash_cache		= $0100
      
                    dash_top_bar_tile 	EQM $ad
      
      
                dashboard_init: subroutine
      
cf65  a9 ff         lda #$ff
cf67  85 43         sta dashboard_message
      
                    ; fill nametable 2 with HUB bg color
                    PPU_SETADDR $27c0
cf69  a9 27         lda #>$27c0
cf6b  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
cf6e  a9 c0         lda #<$27c0
cf70  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
cf73  a9 ff         lda #%11111111
cf75  a2 c0         ldx #$c0
                .23f0_loop
cf77  8d 07 20      sta PPU_DATA
cf7a  e8            inx
cf7b  d0 fa         bne .23f0_loop
      
                    ; COLOR SPLIT
                    ; set split color attr for top row
                    PPU_SETADDR $27e8
cf7d  a9 27         lda #>$27e8
cf7f  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
cf82  a9 e8         lda #<$27e8
cf84  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
cf87  a9 f1         lda #%11110001
cf89  a2 f8         ldx #$f8
                .27e8_loop
cf8b  8d 07 20      sta PPU_DATA
cf8e  e8            inx
cf8f  d0 fa         bne .27e8_loop
      
      
      
                    ; fill page 2 dashboard top row
                    PPU_SETADDR dash_page2_top_bar
cf91  a9 26         lda #>dash_page2_top_bar
cf93  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
cf96  a9 c0         lda #<dash_page2_top_bar
cf98  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
cf9b  a9 ad         lda #dash_top_bar_tile
cf9d  a0 20         ldy #$20
                .set_top_bar
cf9f  8d 07 20      sta PPU_DATA
cfa2  88            dey
cfa3  d0 fa         bne .set_top_bar
      
                    ; fill page 2 dashboard tiles
cfa5  a2 00         ldx #$00
                .dashboard_fill_tiles
cfa7  bd c6 cf      lda dashboard_bg_tiles,x
cfaa  8d 07 20      sta PPU_DATA
cfad  e8            inx
cfae  e0 a0         cpx #$a0
cfb0  d0 f5         bne .dashboard_fill_tiles
cfb2  a2 00         ldx #$00
                .dash_cache_fill
cfb4  bd e6 cf      lda dashboard_bg_tiles+$20,x
cfb7  9d 00 01      sta dash_cache,x
cfba  bd 26 d0      lda dashboard_bg_tiles+$60,x
cfbd  9d 20 01      sta dash_cache+$20,x
cfc0  e8            inx
cfc1  e0 20         cpx #$20
cfc3  d0 ef         bne .dash_cache_fill
cfc5  60            rts
      
      
      
                dashboard_bg_tiles:
                    ; row 0 is top bar
                    ; row 1 : top hud frames
cfc6  hex           b4b4bcbcbcbcbcbe
cfce  hex           bcbcbcbcbcbcbcbc
cfd6  hex           bcbcbcbcbcbcbcbc
cfde  hex           bebcbcbcbcbcbdb4
                    ; row 2 : health, meter, lives
cfe6  hex           b4b0
cfe8  byte          #char_set_S
cfe9  hex           a8a9aa
cfec  hex           ffb3
cfee  hex           ffffffffffffffff
cff6  hex           ffffffffffffffff
cffe  hex           b3aeaf
d001  byte          #char_set_x
d002  hex           30b1b2b6
                    ; row 3 : middle hud frames
d006  hex           b4b8b9b9b9b9b9b7
d00e  hex           b9b9bfbbb9b9b9b9
d016  hex           b9b9b9b9bfbbb9b9
d01e  hex           b7b9b9b9b9b9bab4
                    ; row 4 : wave, score, time
d026  hex           b4b0
d028  byte          #char_set_P,#char_set_H,#char_set_A
d02b  byte          #char_set_S,#char_set_E,#tile_empty
d02e  hex           ffffb2b6ffffffff
d036  hex           ffffffffb2b0ffff
d03e  hex           ffffffffffffb2b4
                    ; row 5 : bottom hud frames
d046  hex           b4b4b5b5b5b5b5b5
d04e  hex           b5b5b6b4b5b5b5b5
d056  hex           b5b5b5b5b6b4b5b5
d05e  hex           b5b5b5b5b5b5b6b6
      
      
      
      
                    dashboard_message_set: subroutine
                    ; set y for message start offset
d066  a2 00         ldx #$00
                .loop
d068  b9 f2 c1      lda dashboard_messages,y
d06b  9d 08 01      sta dash_cache+$08,x
d06e  c8            iny
d06f  e8            inx
d070  e0 10         cpx #$10
d072  d0 f4         bne .loop
d074  60            rts
      
      
      
      
                    ; RENDER DASH STATS TO SCREEN
                dashboard_render: subroutine
                    ; top hud info row
                    PPU_SETADDR $2706 ; PPU_SETADDR is 12 cycles
d075  a9 27         lda #>$2706
d077  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
d07a  a9 06         lda #<$2706
d07c  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
d07f  a2 06         ldx #$06
                .top_row_loop
d081  bd 00 01      lda dash_cache,x
d084  8d 07 20      sta PPU_DATA
d087  e8            inx
d088  e0 1e         cpx #$1e
d08a  d0 f5         bne .top_row_loop
      
                .lives
                    PPU_SETADDR lives_addr
d08c  a9 27         lda #>lives_addr
d08e  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
d091  a9 1c         lda #<lives_addr
d093  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
d096  a5 0c         lda player_lives
d098  20 c3 c1      jsr get_char_hi
d09b  8d 07 20      sta PPU_DATA
d09e  a5 0c         lda player_lives
d0a0  20 cb c1      jsr get_char_lo
d0a3  8d 07 20      sta PPU_DATA
      
                    ; bottom hud info row
                    PPU_SETADDR $2748
d0a6  a9 27         lda #>$2748
d0a8  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
d0ab  a9 48         lda #<$2748
d0ad  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
d0b0  a2 08         ldx #$08
                .bottom_row_loop
d0b2  bd 20 01      lda dash_cache+$20,x
d0b5  8d 07 20      sta PPU_DATA
d0b8  e8            inx
d0b9  e0 1e         cpx #$1e
d0bb  d0 f5         bne .bottom_row_loop
d0bd  60            rts
      
      
      
                dashboard_gauges_table:
d0be  byte          phase_kill_counter
d0bf  byte          player_frag_counter
d0c0  byte          player_x_hi
d0c1  byte          player_y_hi
      
      
                    ; GET DASH UPDATES READY TO RENDER
                dashboard_update: subroutine
      
                    ; player speed
d0c2  a5 1a         lda player_speed
d0c4  18            clc
d0c5  69 61         adc #char_set_offset+1
d0c7  8d 06 01      sta $0100+6
      
                    ; LIFEBARF
d0ca  a4 43         ldy dashboard_message
d0cc  30 06         bmi .lifebarf_is_live
d0ce  20 66 d0      jsr dashboard_message_set
d0d1  4c 0c d1      jmp .no_lifebar
      
                .lifebarf_is_live
                    ; 64 health lines on screen
                    ; 16 tiles across
                    ; 256 possible values
                    ; displaying 1/4th the range
      
d0d4  a0 00         ldy #$00
      
d0d6  a9 3c         lda #$3c ; #60 (4 less than max)
d0d8  85 86         sta temp00 ; life value floor for tile
      
d0da  a5 0b         lda player_health
d0dc  4a            lsr
d0dd  4a            lsr
d0de  85 87         sta temp01 ; life / 4 aka 0..63
                .find_top_tile
d0e0  a5 87         lda temp01
d0e2  c5 86         cmp temp00
d0e4  b0 0f         bcs .top_tile_found ; life > tile floor value
d0e6  a5 86         lda temp00
d0e8  38            sec
d0e9  e9 04         sbc #$04
d0eb  85 86         sta temp00
d0ed  a9 03         lda #tile_empty
d0ef  99 08 01      sta dash_cache+$08,y
d0f2  c8            iny
d0f3  d0 eb         bne .find_top_tile
                .top_tile_found
d0f5  a5 87         lda temp01
d0f7  38            sec
d0f8  e5 86         sbc temp00
d0fa  18            clc
d0fb  69 a4         adc #$a4
d0fd  99 08 01      sta dash_cache+$08,y
                .fill_remaining_tiles
d100  c8            iny
d101  c0 10         cpy #$10
d103  f0 07         beq .no_lifebar
d105  a9 a7         lda #$a7 ; full tile
d107  99 08 01      sta dash_cache+$08,y
d10a  d0 f4         bne .fill_remaining_tiles
                .no_lifebar
      
      
                    ; PHASE
d10c  a5 5a         lda phase_current
d10e  20 c3 c1      jsr get_char_hi
d111  8d 28 01      sta dash_cache+$28
d114  a5 5a         lda phase_current
d116  20 cb c1      jsr get_char_lo
d119  8d 29 01      sta dash_cache+$29
      
      
                    ; GAUGES
                    ; used to be scoreboard
      
                    ; phase_kill_counter
                    ; player_frag_counter
                    ; player_x_hi
                    ; player_y_hi
      
d11c  a2 07         ldx #$07
d11e  a0 03         ldy #$03
                .guages_display_loop
d120  86 86         stx temp00
d122  be be d0      ldx dashboard_gauges_table,y
d125  b5 00         lda $0000,x
d127  85 87         sta temp01
d129  a6 86         ldx temp00
d12b  20 cb c1      jsr get_char_lo
d12e  9d 2c 01      sta dash_cache+$2c,x
d131  a5 87         lda temp01
d133  20 c3 c1      jsr get_char_hi
d136  ca            dex
d137  9d 2c 01      sta dash_cache+$2c,x
d13a  ca            dex
d13b  88            dey
d13c  10 e2         bpl .guages_display_loop
      
      
                    ; TIMER
                .timer
                    ; make sure player has lives / not game over
d13e  a5 0c         lda player_lives
d140  c9 00         cmp #$00
d142  f0 2e         beq .timer_done
                    ; make sure player hasn't finished the game
d144  a5 63         lda phase_end_game
d146  c9 01         cmp #$01
d148  f0 28         beq .timer_done
                    ; we good to update the visual timer
d14a  a5 49         lda timer_minutes_10s
d14c  8d 36 01      sta dash_cache+$36
d14f  a5 48         lda timer_minutes_1s
d151  8d 37 01      sta dash_cache+$37
                    ; colon
d154  a9 ac         lda #char_set_colon
d156  8d 38 01      sta dash_cache+$38
d159  a5 47         lda timer_seconds_10s
d15b  8d 39 01      sta dash_cache+$39
d15e  a5 46         lda timer_seconds_1s
d160  8d 3a 01      sta dash_cache+$3a
                    ; period
d163  a9 99         lda #char_set_period
d165  8d 3b 01      sta dash_cache+$3b
d168  a5 45         lda timer_frames_10s
d16a  8d 3c 01      sta dash_cache+$3c
d16d  a5 44         lda timer_frames_1s
d16f  8d 3d 01      sta dash_cache+$3d
                .timer_done
      
d172  60            rts
      

palette.asm
      
      00 e7         palette_cache EQU $e7
      
      
                    ;Palette00:  ; 25 bytes
      
                    ; hex 0f  ;screen color
                    ; hex 022830 ;background 0
                    ;   hex 071624 ;background 1
                    ;   hex 02113c ;background 2
                    ;   hex 0b1a3b ;background 3
                    ;   hex 192939 ;sprite 0 ; rudy
                    ;   hex 132130 ;sprite 1; old birb palete
                    ;  ;hex 051637 ; chomps
                    ;hex 122130 ; vamp eyes & bats
                    ;hex 071727 ;sprite 2
                    ;   hex 150037 ; vamp head/face
                    ;   hex 013530 ;sprite 3 ; starglasses ; crossbones
      
      
                    ; bg 0 - stars bg_tiles 1 / title screen
                    ; bg 1 - stars bg_tiles 2
                    ; bg 2 - n/a  (bg enemies haha)
                    ; bg 3 - dashboard / options screen
                    ; spr 0 - player
                    ; spr 1 - enemy / darts
                    ; spr 2 - enemy / stars sprites 1
                    ; spr 3 - enemy / powerups / stars sprite 2
      
      
                    ; level palettes
                    ; spr 1, 2 and 3
                    ; boss palettes
                    ; spr 1 and 2
      
      
                palette_table:
                    ; #00 end bad
d173  byte          #$0f, #$30, #$27
                    ; #03 end ok
d176  byte          #$0f, #$10, #$30
                    ; #06 end good
d179  byte          #$0a, #$37, #$27
                    ; #09 end alien
d17c  byte          #$3c, #$1c, #$24
                    ; #12 intro bg
d17f  byte          #$0c, #$30, #$3c
                    ; #15 intro alien
d182  byte          #$04, #$1b, #$37
                    ; #18 title screen
d185  byte          #$02, #$27, #$34
                    ; #21 level 0 palettes (and attract mode)
d188  hex           13 21 30
d18b  hex           07 17 27
d18e  hex           01 35 30
                    ; #30 level 1 palettes
d191  hex           06 16 38
d194  hex           0b 18 39
d197  hex           08 32 20
                    ; #39 level 2 palettes
d19a  hex           0c 2d 32
d19d  hex           13 25 37
d1a0  hex           09 27 10
                    ; #48 level 3 palettes
d1a3  hex           2d 10 30
d1a6  hex           12 23 37
d1a9  hex           07 17 27
                    ; #57 boss
                    ; moufs palettes
d1ac  hex           04 16 20
d1af  hex           02 07 38
                    ; #63 boss
                    ; vamp+bats palettes
d1b2  hex           15 2d 37
d1b5  hex           02 11 30
                    ; #69 boss
                    ; scarab palettes

                    ; #75 boss
                    ; swordtner palettes

                    ; #81 bookbinder
