                palette_level_offset_table:
d1c7  byte          #21, #30, #39, #48
                palette_boss_offset_table:
d1cb  byte          #57, #63, #69, #75
      
                palette_load:
                    ; x = palette table offset
                    ; y = pal ram offset
                    ; loads 1 set of 3 colors
                    ; not used to write to main bg color
d1cf  a9 02         lda #$02
d1d1  85 86         sta temp00
                .loader_loop
d1d3  bd 73 d1      lda palette_table,x
d1d6  99 91 00      sta pal_uni_bg+1,y
d1d9  e8            inx
d1da  c8            iny
d1db  c6 86         dec temp00
d1dd  10 f4         bpl .loader_loop
d1df  60            rts
      
      
      
      
      
      
                palette_next_rainbow_color: subroutine
                    ; a = current and return color
d1e0  85 86         sta temp00
d1e2  e6 86         inc temp00
d1e4  a5 86         lda temp00
d1e6  29 0f         and #$0f
d1e8  c9 0d         cmp #$0d
d1ea  10 03         bpl .wrap
d1ec  a5 86         lda temp00
d1ee  60            rts
                .wrap
d1ef  a5 86         lda temp00
d1f1  38            sec
d1f2  e9 0c         sbc #$0c
d1f4  60            rts
      
      
                bomb_bg_animation_table:
d1f5  byte          $0f,$07,$06,$05,$16,$26,$27,$28,$37,$38,$30
      
      
                palette_update: subroutine
                    ; shroom
d200  a5 6c         lda shroom_counter
d202  f0 14         beq .no_shroom_effect
d204  a5 24         lda wtf
d206  29 03         and #$03
d208  d0 0e         bne .no_shroom_effect
d20a  a0 0e         ldy #$0e
                .shroom_loop
d20c  b9 9a 00      lda pal_bg_3_1,y
d20f  20 e0 d1      jsr palette_next_rainbow_color
d212  99 9a 00      sta pal_bg_3_1,y
d215  88            dey
d216  10 f4         bpl .shroom_loop
                .no_shroom_effect
                    ; fade in
d218  a5 8d         lda state_fade_in
d21a  f0 03         beq .dont_fade_in
d21c  4c 5b d2      jmp palette_fade_in_update
                .dont_fade_in
                    ; fade out
d21f  a5 8e         lda state_fade_out
d221  f0 03         beq .dont_fade_out
d223  4c 91 d2      jmp palette_fade_out_update
                .dont_fade_out
                    ; fade from white
d226  a5 8f         lda state_from_white
d228  f0 03         beq .dont_fade_from_white
d22a  4c bb d2      jmp palette_fade_from_white_update
                .dont_fade_from_white
                    ; bomb
d22d  a5 6a         lda bomb_counter
d22f  f0 08         beq .normal_bg
d231  4a            lsr
d232  4a            lsr
d233  aa            tax
d234  bd f5 d1      lda bomb_bg_animation_table,x
d237  85 90         sta pal_uni_bg
                .normal_bg
                    ; do normal
d239  a0 19         ldy #25
                .loop
d23b  b9 90 00      lda pal_uni_bg,y
d23e  99 e7 00      sta palette_cache,y
d241  88            dey
d242  10 f7         bpl .loop
d244  60            rts
      
      
                palette_state_reset:
d245  a9 00         lda #$00
d247  85 8d         sta state_fade_in
d249  85 8e         sta state_fade_out
d24b  85 8f         sta state_from_white
d24d  60            rts
      
      
                palette_fade_in_init: subroutine
                    ; make sure we're not already fading out
d24e  a5 8e         lda state_fade_out
d250  d0 08         bne .init_skip
                .init_fade
d252  a9 00         lda #$00
d254  85 8a         sta pal_fade_c ; frame counter
d256  a9 ff         lda #$ff
d258  85 8d         sta state_fade_in
                .init_skip
d25a  60            rts
      
                palette_fade_in_update: subroutine
d25b  a5 8a         lda pal_fade_c
d25d  29 30         and #%00110000
d25f  49 30         eor #%00110000
d261  85 8b         sta pal_fade_offset
d263  a2 17         ldx #23
                .loop
d265  b5 90         lda pal_uni_bg,x
d267  38            sec
d268  e5 8b         sbc pal_fade_offset
d26a  10 02         bpl .dont_set_black
d26c  a9 0f         lda #$0f
                .dont_set_black
d26e  95 e7         sta palette_cache,x
d270  ca            dex
d271  10 f2         bpl .loop
d273  a5 8a         lda pal_fade_c
d275  18            clc
d276  69 03         adc #$03
d278  c9 40         cmp #$40
d27a  90 03         bcc .fade_mode_not_done
d27c  20 45 d2      jsr palette_state_reset
                .fade_mode_not_done
d27f  85 8a         sta pal_fade_c
d281  60            rts
      
      
                    ; fade out leads to forced tansition and new state
                palette_fade_out_init: subroutine
                    ; a = init callback index
d282  85 8c         sta pal_fade_target
                    ; make sure we're not already fading out
d284  a5 8e         lda state_fade_out
d286  d0 08         bne .init_skip
                .init_fade
d288  a9 10         lda #$10
d28a  85 8a         sta pal_fade_c ; frame counter
d28c  a9 ff         lda #$ff
d28e  85 8e         sta state_fade_out
                .init_skip
d290  60            rts
      
                palette_fade_out_update: subroutine
d291  a5 8a         lda pal_fade_c
d293  29 f0         and #%11110000
d295  85 8b         sta pal_fade_offset
d297  a2 19         ldx #25
                .loop
d299  b5 90         lda pal_uni_bg,x
d29b  38            sec
d29c  e5 8b         sbc pal_fade_offset
d29e  b0 02         bcs .no_reset
d2a0  a9 0f         lda #$0f
                .no_reset
d2a2  95 e7         sta palette_cache,x
d2a4  ca            dex
d2a5  10 f2         bpl .loop
d2a7  a5 8a         lda pal_fade_c
d2a9  18            clc
d2aa  69 03         adc #$03
d2ac  c9 60         cmp #$60
d2ae  90 08         bcc .fade_mode_not_done
d2b0  20 45 d2      jsr palette_state_reset
      
d2b3  a5 8c         lda pal_fade_target
d2b5  4c f1 c4      jmp jump_to_subroutine
                .fade_mode_not_done
d2b8  85 8a         sta pal_fade_c
d2ba  60            rts
      
      
      
                palette_fade_from_white_update: subroutine
d2bb  a5 8a         lda pal_fade_c
d2bd  29 f0         and #%11110000
d2bf  85 8b         sta pal_fade_offset
d2c1  a2 17         ldx #23
                .loop
d2c3  b5 90         lda pal_uni_bg,x
d2c5  18            clc
d2c6  65 8b         adc pal_fade_offset
d2c8  c9 3f         cmp #$3f
d2ca  90 02         bcc .dont_set_white
d2cc  a9 30         lda #$30
                .dont_set_white
d2ce  95 e7         sta palette_cache,x
d2d0  ca            dex
d2d1  10 f0         bpl .loop
d2d3  c6 8a         dec pal_fade_c
d2d5  c6 8a         dec pal_fade_c
d2d7  c6 8a         dec pal_fade_c
d2d9  10 03         bpl .fade_mode_not_done
d2db  20 45 d2      jsr palette_state_reset
                .fade_mode_not_done
d2de  60            rts
      
      
      
                    ; 12 + 32 x 7 = 236 cycles
                palette_render: subroutine
                    PPU_SETADDR $3f00
d2df  a9 3f         lda #>$3f00
d2e1  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
d2e4  a9 00         lda #<$3f00
d2e6  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
d2e9  a2 00         ldx #0
d2eb  a0 08         ldy #8
                .loop
d2ed  a5 e7         lda palette_cache
d2ef  8d 07 20      sta PPU_DATA
d2f2  e8            inx
d2f3  b5 e7         lda palette_cache,x
d2f5  8d 07 20      sta PPU_DATA
d2f8  e8            inx
d2f9  b5 e7         lda palette_cache,x
d2fb  8d 07 20      sta PPU_DATA
d2fe  e8            inx
d2ff  b5 e7         lda palette_cache,x
d301  8d 07 20      sta PPU_DATA
d304  88            dey
d305  d0 e6         bne .loop
d307  60            rts

powerups.asm
                    ; powerups always spawn from starglasses
      
                    ; powerup juggling brought to you by sine waves
                    ; x pos is point of origin
                    ; only need to use rise and fall arc
                    ; must capture max fall velocity (4px/frame)
                    ; rise and fall needs full 8bit range
      
                    ; palette sprites start at $24
                    ; 0 - skull shield
                    ; 1 - mushroom
                    ; 2 - plus one
                    ; 3 - bomb
                    ; 4 - r bag
                    ; 5 - health 25%
                    ; 6 - health 50%
                    ; 7 - health full
      
                powerup_drop_table:
d308  hex           04 05 00 03 06 02 01 07
d310  hex           00 03 06 04 01 00 07 02
      
      
                powerup_from_starglasses:
d318  a9 02         lda #powerups_id
d31a  9d 00 03      sta enemy_ram_type,x
d31d  b9 03 02      lda oam_ram_x,y
d320  18            clc
d321  69 04         adc #$04
d323  9d 02 03      sta enemy_ram_x,x
d326  b9 00 02      lda oam_ram_y,y
d329  9d 03 03      sta enemy_ram_y,x
d32c  a9 40         lda #$40
d32e  9d 04 03      sta enemy_ram_pc,x
d331  a9 01         lda #$01
d333  9d 01 03      sta enemy_ram_hp,x
                    ; rng powerup type
d336  a5 68         lda powerup_counter
d338  29 07         and #$07
d33a  d0 07         bne .dont_reset_powerup_offset
d33c  20 52 c1      jsr get_next_random
d33f  29 0f         and #$0f
d341  85 69         sta powerup_offset
                .dont_reset_powerup_offset
d343  a5 69         lda powerup_offset
d345  29 0f         and #$0f
d347  aa            tax
d348  bd 08 d3      lda powerup_drop_table,x
d34b  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
d34d  9d 07 03      sta enemy_ram_ex,x
d350  e6 69         inc powerup_offset
d352  e6 68         inc powerup_counter
d354  60            rts
      
      
                powerups_cycle: subroutine
                .process_this_frame
d355  b9 03 02      lda oam_ram_x,y
d358  e9 03         sbc #3
d35a  85 3b         sta collision_0_x
d35c  bd 03 03      lda enemy_ram_y,x
d35f  99 00 02      sta oam_ram_y,y
d362  e9 03         sbc #3
d364  85 3c         sta collision_0_y
d366  a9 0d         lda #$0d
d368  85 3d         sta collision_0_w
d36a  85 3e         sta collision_0_h
d36c  a9 00         lda #$00
d36e  85 79         sta enemy_dmg_accumulator
d370  20 04 f3      jsr player_bullet_collision_handler
d373  a5 79         lda enemy_dmg_accumulator
d375  d0 14         bne .reset_velocity
d377  20 35 cf      jsr player_collision_detect
d37a  f0 25         beq .frame
                .despawn_powerup
                .player_picksup_powerup
d37c  bd 07 03      lda enemy_ram_ex,x
d37f  18            clc
d380  69 3f         adc #powerup_pickup_jump_table_offset
d382  20 f1 c4      jsr jump_to_subroutine
d385  20 47 dd      jsr enemy_clear
d388  4c 92 db      jmp update_enemies_handler_next
                .reset_velocity
d38b  a9 01         lda #$01
d38d  9d 04 03      sta enemy_ram_pc,x
d390  b9 03 02      lda oam_ram_x,y
d393  9d 02 03      sta enemy_ram_x,x
                    ; will wrap to the left so add hp
d396  fe 01 03      inc enemy_ram_hp,x
d399  10 03         bpl .dont_max_hp
d39b  de 01 03      dec enemy_ram_hp,x
                .dont_max_hp
                    ; give it sfx
d39e  20 32 f9      jsr sfx_powerup_hit
                .frame
d3a1  bd 04 03      lda enemy_ram_pc,x
d3a4  c9 80         cmp #$80
d3a6  f0 2f         beq .max_velocity
                .sine_arc
d3a8  bc 04 03      ldy enemy_ram_pc,x
d3ab  c8            iny
d3ac  b9 00 fe      lda sine_table,y
d3af  85 86         sta temp00
d3b1  88            dey
d3b2  b9 00 fe      lda sine_table,y
d3b5  38            sec
d3b6  e5 86         sbc temp00
d3b8  85 86         sta temp00
d3ba  9d 05 03      sta enemy_ram_ac,x
d3bd  a4 77         ldy enemy_oam_offset
d3bf  bd 02 03      lda enemy_ram_x,x
d3c2  18            clc
d3c3  65 86         adc temp00
d3c5  9d 02 03      sta enemy_ram_x,x
d3c8  99 03 02      sta oam_ram_x,y
d3cb  a5 24         lda wtf
d3cd  29 01         and #$01
d3cf  f0 14         beq .move_done
d3d1  fe 04 03      inc enemy_ram_pc,x
d3d4  4c e5 d3      jmp .move_done
                .max_velocity
d3d7  b9 03 02      lda oam_ram_x,y
d3da  18            clc
d3db  69 04         adc #$04
d3dd  99 03 02      sta oam_ram_x,y
d3e0  90 03         bcc .move_done
                    ; will fall off the right so minus hp
d3e2  de 01 03      dec enemy_ram_hp,x
                .move_done
                    ; sprite
d3e5  bd 07 03      lda enemy_ram_ex,x
d3e8  18            clc
d3e9  69 24         adc #$24
d3eb  99 01 02      sta oam_ram_spr,y
                    ; palette
d3ee  a9 03         lda #$03
d3f0  99 02 02      sta oam_ram_att,y
                .despawn_check
d3f3  bd 01 03      lda enemy_ram_hp,x
d3f6  c9 00         cmp #$00
d3f8  d0 03         bne .done
                    ;; despawns to the right
d3fa  20 47 dd      jsr enemy_clear
                .done
d3fd  4c 92 db      jmp update_enemies_handler_next
      
      
      
      
      
      
                powerup_pickup_mask: subroutine
d400  e6 6e         inc orbit_shield_speed
d402  e6 6e         inc orbit_shield_speed
d404  e6 6e         inc orbit_shield_speed
d406  20 86 f9      jsr sfx_powerup_mask
d409  60            rts
      
      
                powerup_pickup_mushroom: subroutine
                    ; shroom_counter set inside sfx call
d40a  20 62 f9      jsr sfx_powerup_mushroom
d40d  60            rts
                powerup_mushroom_update: subroutine
d40e  a5 6c         lda shroom_counter
d410  f0 20         beq .shroom_done
d412  a5 24         lda wtf
d414  29 03         and #$03
d416  d0 1a         bne .shroom_done
d418  c6 6c         dec shroom_counter
d41a  d0 0b         bne .audio_bend_up_or_down
                .end_of_shroom
d41c  20 39 d7      jsr phase_palette_load
d41f  20 48 f2      jsr player_update_colors
d422  a9 00         lda #$00
d424  85 6d         sta shroom_mod
d426  60            rts
                .audio_bend_up_or_down
d427  a5 6c         lda shroom_counter
d429  c9 3f         cmp #$3f
d42b  90 03         bcc .down
                .up
d42d  e6 6d         inc shroom_mod
d42f  60            rts
                .down
d430  c6 6d         dec shroom_mod
                .shroom_done
d432  60            rts
      
      
                powerup_pickup_plus_one: subroutine
d433  e6 0c         inc player_lives
d435  20 a3 f9      jsr sfx_powerup_1up
d438  60            rts
      
      
                powerup_pickup_bomb: subroutine
                    ; bomb_counter set inside sfx call
                    ; 1hp enemy damage every other frame
d439  20 44 f9      jsr sfx_powerup_bomb
d43c  60            rts
                powerup_bomb_update: subroutine
d43d  a5 6a         lda bomb_counter
d43f  f0 0a         beq .bomb_done
d441  c6 6a         dec bomb_counter
d443  a5 bf         lda sfx_noi_update_type
d445  d0 04         bne .bomb_done
d447  a9 04         lda #$04
d449  85 bf         sta sfx_noi_update_type
                .bomb_done
d44b  60            rts
      
      
                powerup_pickup_r_bag: subroutine
d44c  a9 ff         lda #$ff
d44e  85 6b         sta r_bag_counter
d450  18            clc
d451  a9 05         lda #$05
d453  65 20         adc player_autofire_s
d455  85 20         sta player_autofire_s
d457  a9 27         lda #$27
d459  c5 20         cmp player_autofire_s
d45b  90 02         bcc .not_maxed_out
d45d  a5 20         lda player_autofire_s
                .not_maxed_out
d45f  85 20         sta player_autofire_s
d461  60            rts
      
      
                powerup_pickup_health_25: subroutine
d462  20 ee f9      jsr sfx_powerup_battery_25
d465  a5 0b         lda player_health
d467  18            clc
d468  69 40         adc #$40
d46a  b0 13         bcs powerup_player_max_energy
d46c  85 0b         sta player_health
d46e  60            rts
      
                powerup_pickup_health_50: subroutine
d46f  20 f4 f9      jsr sfx_powerup_battery_50
d472  a5 0b         lda player_health
d474  18            clc
d475  69 80         adc #$80
d477  b0 06         bcs powerup_player_max_energy
d479  85 0b         sta player_health
d47b  60            rts
      
                powerup_pickup_health_100: subroutine
d47c  20 fa f9      jsr sfx_powerup_battery_100
                powerup_player_max_energy:
d47f  a9 ff         lda #$ff
d481  85 0b         sta player_health
d483  60            rts
      
      

sine.dasm
      
      
      04 80         sine_7bits equ $0480
      05 00         sine_6bits equ $0500
      05 80         sine_5bits equ $0580
      06 00         sine_4bits equ $0600
      06 80         sine_3bits equ $0680
      07 00         sine_2bits equ $0700
      07 80         sine_1bits equ $0780
      
      
      
      
                    ; 40 bytes
                sine_init: subroutine
d484  a2 00         ldx #$00
d486  a0 00         ldy #$00
                .loop
d488  b9 00 fe      lda sine_table,y
d48b  4a            lsr
d48c  9d 80 04      sta sine_7bits,x
d48f  4a            lsr
d490  9d 00 05      sta sine_6bits,x
d493  4a            lsr
d494  9d 80 05      sta sine_5bits,x
d497  4a            lsr
d498  9d 00 06      sta sine_4bits,x
d49b  4a            lsr
d49c  9d 80 06      sta sine_3bits,x
d49f  4a            lsr
d4a0  9d 00 07      sta sine_2bits,x
d4a3  4a            lsr
d4a4  9d 80 07      sta sine_1bits,x
d4a7  e8            inx
d4a8  c8            iny
d4a9  c8            iny
d4aa  d0 dc         bne .loop
d4ac  60            rts
      
      
                    ; 61 bytes
                sine_of_scale: subroutine
                    ; returns scaled value of sine table
                    ; a = sine max
                    ; x = sine pos
d4ad  49 ff         eor #$ff
d4af  85 86         sta temp00
                    ; half x for lookup table
d4b1  8a            txa
d4b2  4a            lsr
d4b3  aa            tax
d4b4  a9 00         lda #$00
d4b6  46 86         lsr temp00
d4b8  46 86         lsr temp00
d4ba  b0 03         bcs .not_1bits
d4bc  7d 80 07      adc sine_1bits,x
                .not_1bits
d4bf  46 86         lsr temp00
d4c1  b0 03         bcs .not_2bits
d4c3  7d 00 07      adc sine_2bits,x
                .not_2bits
d4c6  46 86         lsr temp00
d4c8  b0 03         bcs .not_3bits
d4ca  7d 80 06      adc sine_3bits,x
                .not_3bits
d4cd  46 86         lsr temp00
d4cf  b0 03         bcs .not_4bits
d4d1  7d 00 06      adc sine_4bits,x
                .not_4bits
d4d4  46 86         lsr temp00
d4d6  b0 03         bcs .not_5bits
d4d8  7d 80 05      adc sine_5bits,x
                .not_5bits
d4db  46 86         lsr temp00
d4dd  b0 03         bcs .not_6bits
d4df  7d 00 05      adc sine_6bits,x
                .not_6bits
d4e2  46 86         lsr temp00
d4e4  b0 03         bcs .not_7bits
d4e6  7d 80 04      adc sine_7bits,x
                .not_7bits
d4e9  60            rts
      
      
      

starfield_simple.asm
      
                    STARFIELD_MASK		EQM %00111111 ; star probability
                    STARFIELD_COL0_START	EQM $00
                    STARFIELD_COL1_START	EQM $07
      
                    starfield_sprite	EQM $6b
                    star_pattern		EQM $a0
      
      03 e0         starfield_msg_cache	= $03e0	; 32 bytes for string data
      
      00 d0         star_cache		= $d0
                    star_oam_start		EQM $40
                    star_oam_end		EQM <(star_oam_start+$80)
      
                    scroll_speed_lo_default	EQM #179
                    scroll_speed_hi_default	EQM #2
      
      
      
                    starfield_twinkle_reset: subroutine
d4ea  a9 00         lda #STARFIELD_COL0_START
d4ec  85 34         sta starfield_color0
d4ee  a9 07         lda #STARFIELD_COL1_START
d4f0  85 35         sta starfield_color1
d4f2  60            rts
      
      
                starfield_speed_increase
                    ; increase star speed
d4f3  a9 39         lda #57
d4f5  18            clc
d4f6  65 05         adc scroll_speed_lo
d4f8  85 05         sta scroll_speed_lo
d4fa  90 02         bcc .scroll_hi_done
d4fc  e6 06         inc scroll_speed_hi
                .scroll_hi_done
d4fe  60            rts
      
      
                starfield_init: subroutine
                    ; set starfield state
d4ff  a9 00         lda #$00
d501  85 31         sta starfield_state
                    ; set bg tile palette attributes / colors
                    ; $23c0 and $27c0
                    ; page 1 attributes
                    PPU_SETADDR $23c0
d503  a9 23         lda #>$23c0
d505  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
d508  a9 c0         lda #<$23c0
d50a  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
d50d  a9 14         lda #%00010100
d50f  a2 d8         ldx #$d8
                .23c0_loop
d511  8d 07 20      sta PPU_DATA
d514  e8            inx
d515  d0 fa         bne .23c0_loop
                    ; COLOR SPLIT
                    ; set split color attr for top row
                    PPU_SETADDR $23e8
d517  a9 23         lda #>$23e8
d519  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
d51c  a9 e8         lda #<$23e8
d51e  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
d521  a9 f1         lda #%11110001
d523  a2 f8         ldx #$f8
                .27e8_loop
d525  8d 07 20      sta PPU_DATA
d528  e8            inx
d529  d0 fa         bne .27e8_loop
                    ; page 2 attributes
                    PPU_SETADDR $27c0
d52b  a9 27         lda #>$27c0
d52d  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
d530  a9 c0         lda #<$27c0
d532  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
d535  a9 14         lda #%00010100
d537  a2 d8         ldx #$d8
                .27c0_loop
d539  8d 07 20      sta PPU_DATA
d53c  e8            inx
d53d  d0 fa         bne .27c0_loop
                    ; make sure sprite0 has something to collide with
d53f  20 dd d5      jsr starfield_draw_dash_top_bar_nametable0
                    ; draw stars on nametable 1
d542  a9 04         lda #CTRL_INC_32
d544  8d 00 20      sta PPU_CTRL
                    ;stx starfield_page
d547  a2 00         ldx #$00
                .draw_stars_loop
d549  86 32         stx starfield_column
d54b  20 52 c1      jsr get_next_random
d54e  20 9c d5      jsr starfield_cache_next_col
d551  a9 20         lda #$20
d553  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
d556  a5 32         lda starfield_column
d558  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
d55b  a0 15         ldy #$15
                .transfer_loop
d55d  b9 d0 00      lda star_cache,y
d560  8d 07 20      sta PPU_DATA
d563  88            dey
d564  d0 f7         bne .transfer_loop
d566  e8            inx
d567  e0 20         cpx #$20
d569  d0 de         bne .draw_stars_loop
d56b  a9 00         lda #0
d56d  8d 00 20      sta PPU_CTRL
d570  60            rts
      
      
      
                starfield_render: subroutine
                    ; copy tiles to PPU VRAM during vblank
                    ;update_starfield_column
d571  a9 04         lda #CTRL_INC_32
d573  8d 00 20      sta PPU_CTRL
                    ; set base PPU address
d576  a5 33         lda starfield_page
d578  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
d57b  a5 32         lda starfield_column
d57d  8d 06 20      sta PPU_ADDR
                    ; rip from cache to PPU
d580  a0 15         ldy #$15
                .col_loop
d582  b9 d0 00      lda star_cache,y
d585  8d 07 20      sta PPU_DATA
d588  88            dey
d589  d0 f7         bne .col_loop
                    ; put that shit back to sequential order
d58b  a9 00         lda #0
d58d  8d 00 20      sta PPU_CTRL
d590  20 75 d0      jsr dashboard_render
d593  4c 7d c0      jmp state_render_done
      
      
                starfield_update: subroutine
                    ; will not work with scroll speeds above 8
                    ; precalculate all starfield things post sprite
d596  20 f2 d5      jsr starfield_scroll
                    ; twinkle them stars with palette stuff
d599  20 1a d6      jsr starfield_twinkle_colors
                starfield_cache_next_col:
                    ; setup rng for star probability
d59c  a5 26         lda rng0
d59e  4a            lsr
d59f  29 3f         and #STARFIELD_MASK
d5a1  85 86         sta temp00
d5a3  a0 00         ldy #$00
                .col_assign_tile_loop
d5a5  c4 86         cpy temp00
d5a7  d0 11         bne .empty_tile
                .star_tile
d5a9  a5 7c         lda boss_heart_stars
d5ab  f0 04         beq .normal_stars
                .heart_stars
d5ad  a9 ff         lda #$ff
d5af  d0 0b         bne .cache_tile
                .normal_stars
d5b1  a5 32         lda starfield_column
d5b3  29 03         and #$03
d5b5  18            clc
d5b6  69 a0         adc #star_pattern
d5b8  d0 02         bne .cache_tile
                .empty_tile
d5ba  a9 03         lda #tile_empty
                .cache_tile
d5bc  99 d0 00      sta star_cache,y
d5bf  c8            iny
d5c0  c0 16         cpy #$16
d5c2  d0 e1         bne .col_assign_tile_loop
d5c4  60            rts
      
      
                death_scroll_speed: subroutine
d5c5  a5 06         lda scroll_speed_hi
d5c7  f0 13         beq .scroll_slow_done
d5c9  a5 05         lda scroll_speed_lo
d5cb  38            sec
d5cc  e9 0d         sbc #13
d5ce  85 05         sta scroll_speed_lo

                .scroll_slow_done

timer_global.dasm
      
                    ; counts in ranges that match numerical tiles
      
                timer_update: subroutine
                    ; UPDATE TIMER DIGITS
                    ; but not if game is over
d644  a5 63         lda phase_end_game
d646  d0 4c         bne .timer_done
                    ; frames 1s
d648  e6 44         inc timer_frames_1s
d64a  a9 6a         lda #char_set_offset+$a
d64c  c5 44         cmp timer_frames_1s
d64e  d0 44         bne .timer_done
d650  a9 60         lda #char_set_offset
d652  85 44         sta timer_frames_1s
                    ; frames 10s
d654  e6 45         inc timer_frames_10s
d656  a9 66         lda #char_set_offset+$6
d658  c5 45         cmp timer_frames_10s
d65a  d0 38         bne .timer_done
d65c  a9 60         lda #char_set_offset
d65e  85 45         sta timer_frames_10s
                    ; seconds 1s
d660  e6 46         inc timer_seconds_1s
d662  a9 6a         lda #char_set_offset+$a
d664  c5 46         cmp timer_seconds_1s
d666  d0 2c         bne .timer_done
d668  a9 60         lda #char_set_offset
d66a  85 46         sta timer_seconds_1s
                    ; seconds 10s
d66c  e6 47         inc timer_seconds_10s
d66e  a9 66         lda #char_set_offset+$6
d670  c5 47         cmp timer_seconds_10s
d672  d0 20         bne .timer_done
d674  a9 60         lda #char_set_offset
d676  85 47         sta timer_seconds_10s
                    ; minutes 1s
d678  e6 48         inc timer_minutes_1s
d67a  a9 6a         lda #char_set_offset+$a
d67c  c5 48         cmp timer_minutes_1s
d67e  d0 14         bne .timer_done
d680  a9 60         lda #char_set_offset
d682  85 48         sta timer_minutes_1s
                    ; minutes 10s
                    ; we really checking this?
                    ; let it go into tile limbo
                    ; GAME OVER at 99:59.59 !!
d684  e6 49         inc timer_minutes_10s
d686  a5 47         lda timer_seconds_10s
d688  c9 6a         cmp #char_set_offset+$a
d68a  d0 08         bne .timer_done
d68c  a9 00         lda #$00
d68e  85 0b         sta player_health
d690  a9 01         lda #$01
d692  85 0c         sta player_lives
                .timer_done
d694  60            rts
      
      
      
                    timer_reset: subroutine
d695  a9 60         lda #char_set_offset
d697  85 44         sta timer_frames_1s
d699  85 45         sta timer_frames_10s
d69b  85 46         sta timer_seconds_1s
d69d  85 47         sta timer_seconds_10s
d69f  85 48         sta timer_minutes_1s
d6a1  85 49         sta timer_minutes_10s
d6a3  60            rts

phase_handler.asm
      
      
                    ;phase_spawn_table EQM $0150
      
      
                    ; zp vars
      
                    ;phase_kill_count byte
                    ;phase_current  byte
                    ;phase_state  byte
                    ;phase_table_ptr byte
                    ;phase_spawn_type byte
                    ;phase_arctang_counter byte
                    ;phase_spawn_counter byte
                    ;phase_interval_counter byte
                    ;phase_end_game  byte
      
      
                    ; phase_state : 0 = still spawning
      
                phase_handling_stuff
      
      
      
      
                phase_handler: subroutine
                    ; only advance phase activity if player is alive
d6a4  a5 65         lda player_death_flag
d6a6  f0 01         beq .player_not_dead
d6a8  60            rts
                .player_not_dead
                    ; always check if its time for an interval spawn
d6a9  20 6c d9      jsr phase_interval_spawn
                    ; call the appropriate phase handler
d6ac  a5 5a         lda phase_current
d6ae  29 0f         and #$0f
d6b0  aa            tax
d6b1  bd d1 d9      lda phase_type_table,x
d6b4  18            clc
d6b5  69 4d         adc #phase_handlers_jump_table_offset
d6b7  4c f1 c4      jmp jump_to_subroutine
      
      
      
                    ; PHASE UTILITIES
      
                phase_check_next_phase: subroutine
                    ; if phase_state == 0 then
                    ; we don't know the kill count yet
d6ba  a5 59         lda phase_state
d6bc  f0 06         beq .not_next_phase
                    ; kill_counter > 0 means we need more killing
d6be  a5 5c         lda phase_kill_counter
d6c0  30 03         bmi .end_of_current_phase
d6c2  f0 01         beq .end_of_current_phase
                .not_next_phase
d6c4  60            rts
                .end_of_current_phase
                    ; use phase_state as a timer
d6c5  e6 59         inc phase_state
                    ; transition time
d6c7  a5 59         lda phase_state
d6c9  c9 20         cmp #$20
d6cb  d0 06         bne .dont_crossbones
d6cd  20 10 d7      jsr phase_clear_1_sprite_spawns
d6d0  20 04 f9      jsr sfx_enemy_death
                .dont_crossbones
d6d3  a5 59         lda phase_state
                    ; wait ~60 frames before trigger next phase sfx
d6d5  c9 40         cmp #$40
d6d7  d0 03         bne .dont_trigger_sfx
d6d9  20 ae fa      jsr sfx_phase_next
                .dont_trigger_sfx
d6dc  a5 59         lda phase_state
                    ; wait ~64 frames before starting next phase
                    ; this should be synced with sfx 2nd chime
d6de  c9 44         cmp #$44
d6e0  d0 e2         bne .not_next_phase
                    ; next phase process
      
                phase_next: subroutine
                    ; reset phase vars
d6e2  a9 00         lda #$00
d6e4  85 5c         sta phase_kill_counter
d6e6  85 61         sta phase_spawn_counter
d6e8  85 59         sta phase_state
d6ea  e6 5a         inc phase_current
                    ; check for starfield lumen reset
d6ec  a5 5a         lda phase_current
d6ee  29 01         and #$01
d6f0  f0 03         beq .no_starfield_lumen_reset
d6f2  20 ea d4      jsr starfield_twinkle_reset
                .no_starfield_lumen_reset
                    ; set phase level
d6f5  a5 5a         lda phase_current
d6f7  29 0f         and #$0f
d6f9  d0 02         bne .not_next_level
d6fb  e6 5b         inc phase_level
                .not_next_level
d6fd  20 f3 d4      jsr starfield_speed_increase
d700  20 39 d7      jsr phase_palette_load
d703  68            pla
d704  68            pla
d705  60            rts
      
                phase_check_spawn_frame: subroutine
d706  a5 24         lda wtf
d708  29 07         and #$07
d70a  f0 03         beq .spawn_frame_go
d70c  68            pla
d70d  68            pla
d70e  60            rts
                .spawn_frame_go
d70f  60            rts
      
                phase_clear_1_sprite_spawns: subroutine
                    ; all small enemies spawned by phase become crossbones
d710  38            sec
d711  a0 0f         ldy #$0f
d713  a2 78         ldx #$78
                .clear_spawn_loop
d715  b9 50 01      lda phase_spawn_table,y
d718  f0 0a         beq .prep_next_loop
d71a  a9 00         lda #0
d71c  99 50 01      sta phase_spawn_table,y
d71f  a9 01         lda #crossbones_id
d721  9d 00 03      sta enemy_ram_type,x
                .prep_next_loop
d724  8a            txa
d725  e9 08         sbc #$08
d727  aa            tax
d728  88            dey
d729  10 ea         bpl .clear_spawn_loop
d72b  60            rts
      
                phase_spawn_track: subroutine
                    ; x = enemy ram pos
d72c  86 86         stx temp00
d72e  8a            txa
d72f  4a            lsr
d730  4a            lsr
d731  4a            lsr
d732  aa            tax
d733  fe 50 01      inc phase_spawn_table,x
d736  a6 86         ldx temp00
d738  60            rts
      
                phase_palette_load: subroutine
                    ; each level has its own palette
d739  a4 5b         ldy phase_level
d73b  be c7 d1      ldx palette_level_offset_table,y
d73e  a0 0f         ldy #15
d740  20 cf d1      jsr palette_load
d743  20 cf d1      jsr palette_load
d746  20 cf d1      jsr palette_load
                    ; boss phases have palettes too!
d749  a5 5a         lda phase_current
d74b  29 0f         and #$0f
d74d  c9 0f         cmp #$0f
d74f  d0 0e         bne .done
d751  a6 5b         ldx phase_level
d753  bd cb d1      lda palette_boss_offset_table,x
d756  aa            tax
d757  a0 0f         ldy #15
d759  20 cf d1      jsr palette_load
d75c  20 cf d1      jsr palette_load
                .done
d75f  60            rts
      
      
      
      
                    ; PHASE TYPES
      
                phase_zero: subroutine
d760  20 ba d6      jsr phase_check_next_phase
d763  a5 59         lda phase_state
d765  d0 20         bne .done
d767  a5 5a         lda phase_current
d769  d0 04         bne .congration
d76b  a9 40         lda #$40
d76d  d0 02         bne .continue
                .congration
d76f  a9 20         lda #$20
                .continue
d771  85 43         sta dashboard_message
d773  e6 61         inc phase_spawn_counter
d775  a9 64         lda #100
d777  c5 61         cmp phase_spawn_counter
d779  d0 0c         bne .stay_zero
d77b  a9 ff         lda #$ff
d77d  85 43         sta dashboard_message
d77f  a5 5a         lda phase_current
                    ; phase #$40 == game done/end
d781  c9 40         cmp #$40
d783  f0 03         beq .end_game
d785  e6 59         inc phase_state
                .stay_zero
                .done
d787  60            rts
                .end_game
d788  a9 00         lda #$00
d78a  85 01         sta scroll_x_hi
d78c  85 04         sta scroll_page
d78e  a9 80         lda #state_init_jump_table_offset+4
d790  20 82 d2      jsr palette_fade_out_init
d793  60            rts
      
      
                phase_galger: subroutine
d794  20 ba d6      jsr phase_check_next_phase
d797  20 06 d7      jsr phase_check_spawn_frame
                    ; phase state > 0 == done initializing phase
d79a  a5 59         lda phase_state
d79c  d0 16         bne .phase_init_done
                    ; set kill counter
d79e  a5 5b         lda phase_level
d7a0  0a            asl
d7a1  18            clc
d7a2  69 06         adc #$06
d7a4  85 5c         sta phase_kill_counter
d7a6  85 61         sta phase_spawn_counter
                    ; set arctang sequence and advance
d7a8  a5 60         lda phase_arctang_counter
d7aa  29 0f         and #$0f
d7ac  aa            tax
d7ad  20 89 ea      jsr arc_sequence_set
d7b0  e6 60         inc phase_arctang_counter
                    ; set phase_state to init done
d7b2  e6 59         inc phase_state
                .phase_init_done
d7b4  a5 61         lda phase_spawn_counter
d7b6  f0 11         beq .dont_spawn
                .do_a_spawn
d7b8  20 86 dc      jsr get_enemy_slot_1_sprite
d7bb  b0 0c         bcs .dont_spawn
d7bd  20 2c d7      jsr phase_spawn_track
d7c0  a9 08         lda #galger_id
d7c2  85 5e         sta phase_spawn_type
d7c4  20 77 da      jsr enemy_spawn_delegator
d7c7  c6 61         dec phase_spawn_counter
                .dont_spawn
d7c9  60            rts
      
      
                phase_spawns: subroutine
d7ca  20 ba d6      jsr phase_check_next_phase
d7cd  20 06 d7      jsr phase_check_spawn_frame
                    ; phase state > 0 == done spawning
d7d0  a5 59         lda phase_state
d7d2  d0 2f         bne .dont_spawn
                    ; if downcounter > 0 then spawn
d7d4  a5 61         lda phase_spawn_counter
d7d6  d0 18         bne .do_spawn
                    ; check for next spawn type
d7d8  a4 5d         ldy phase_table_ptr
d7da  e6 5d         inc phase_table_ptr
d7dc  b9 19 da      lda phase_enemy_table,y
                    ; if type == 0 then stop spawning
d7df  d0 04         bne .next_spawn_type
d7e1  e6 59         inc phase_state
                    ; phase_state should be 1 now
d7e3  d0 1e         bne .dont_spawn
                .next_spawn_type
d7e5  85 5e         sta phase_spawn_type
                    ; load number to spawn
d7e7  a4 5d         ldy phase_table_ptr
d7e9  e6 5d         inc phase_table_ptr
d7eb  b9 19 da      lda phase_enemy_table,y
d7ee  85 61         sta phase_spawn_counter
                .do_spawn
d7f0  a4 5e         ldy phase_spawn_type
d7f2  20 72 dc      jsr enemy_slot_from_type
d7f5  b0 0c         bcs .dont_spawn
d7f7  20 2c d7      jsr phase_spawn_track
d7fa  c6 61         dec phase_spawn_counter
d7fc  e6 5c         inc phase_kill_counter
d7fe  a5 5e         lda phase_spawn_type
d800  20 77 da      jsr enemy_spawn_delegator
                .dont_spawn
d803  60            rts
      
      
                phase_spawn_long_table:
d804  byte          8, 16, 12, 24, 16, 32, 20, 40
      
                phase_spawn_long: subroutine
d80c  20 ba d6      jsr phase_check_next_phase
d80f  20 06 d7      jsr phase_check_spawn_frame
                    ; phase state > 0 == done spawning
d812  a5 59         lda phase_state
d814  d0 0b         bne .phase_init_done
                .init
                    ; oiriginal kill count calculation
                    ; 1 + level + difficulty * (4 or 8)
                    ; ---------------------------------;
                    ; difficulty    0  1    2    3
                    ; -------------|----|----|----|----;
                    ; phase 06    4  8   12   16
                    ; phase 0d    8 16   24   32
                    ; phase 16    8 12   16   20
                    ; phase 1d   16 24   32   40
                    ; phase 26   12 16   20   24
                    ; phase 2d   24 32   40   48
                    ; phase 36   16 20   24   28
                    ; phase 3d    32 40   48   56
d816  a6 5f         ldx phase_spawn_long_c
d818  bd 04 d8      lda phase_spawn_long_table,x
                    ; store kill count target and advance state
d81b  85 5c         sta phase_kill_counter
d81d  e6 5f         inc phase_spawn_long_c
d81f  e6 59         inc phase_state
                .phase_init_done
                    ; make sure state is 1 or dont spawn
d821  c9 01         cmp #$01
d823  d0 21         bne .dont_spawn
d825  a5 5b         lda phase_level
d827  c9 03         cmp #$03
d829  d0 0b         bne .not_final_level
                .final_level_spawn_rng
d82b  20 52 c1      jsr get_next_random
d82e  4a            lsr
d82f  29 03         and #$03
d831  f0 f8         beq .final_level_spawn_rng
d833  38            sec
d834  e9 01         sbc #$01
                .not_final_level
d836  18            clc
d837  69 05         adc #spark_id
d839  a8            tay
d83a  20 86 dc      jsr get_enemy_slot_1_sprite
d83d  b0 07         bcs .dont_spawn
                .set_and_jump
d83f  20 2c d7      jsr phase_spawn_track
d842  98            tya
d843  4c 77 da      jmp enemy_spawn_delegator
                .dont_spawn
d846  60            rts

                    ; boss spawning this frame
d895  20 21 c6      jsr state_clear

                phase_boss_dying_sfx_table:

                phase_boss_dying: subroutine
d8db  a5 61         lda phase_spawn_counter
d8dd  d0 0f         bne .dont_init
                    ; stop music
d8df  20 7c fb      jsr song_stop
                    ; calculate length of boss death
                    ; 8 frame increments
d8e2  a5 5b         lda phase_level
d8e4  18            clc
d8e5  69 02         adc #$02
d8e7  0a            asl ; ( phase level + 2 ) * 2 = 4, 6, 8, 10
d8e8  69 08         adc #$08 ; + 8 = 12, 14, 16, 18
d8ea  85 4a         sta state_v0
d8ec  e6 61         inc phase_spawn_counter
                .dont_init
d8ee  a0 08         ldy #8
                .rng_pal_loop
d8f0  20 52 c1      jsr get_next_random
d8f3  4a            lsr
d8f4  29 3c         and #%00111100
d8f6  99 a0 00      sta pal_spr_1_1,y
d8f9  88            dey
d8fa  10 f4         bpl .rng_pal_loop
                    ; spawn a sfx every 8th frame
d8fc  20 06 d7      jsr phase_check_spawn_frame
d8ff  a5 27         lda rng1
d901  29 03         and #$03
d903  a8            tay
d904  b9 d7 d8      lda phase_boss_dying_sfx_table,y
d907  20 46 f8      jsr sfx_test_delegator
d90a  c6 4a         dec state_v0
d90c  d0 3e         bne .done
      
                    ; kill everything on screen
d90e  a2 00         ldx #$00
d910  86 78         stx enemy_slot_id
d912  86 76         stx enemy_ram_offset
                .iteration_loop
d914  a6 78         ldx enemy_slot_id
d916  bc 39 db      ldy enemy_slot_offset_to_oam_offset,x
d919  84 77         sty enemy_oam_offset
d91b  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
d91d  bd 00 03      lda enemy_ram_type,x
d920  c9 03         cmp #$03
d922  90 03         bcc .skip_crossbones_transmogrification
d924  20 62 dd      jsr enemy_death_handler
                .skip_crossbones_transmogrification
                    ; setup next loop
d927  e6 78         inc enemy_slot_id
d929  a9 08         lda #$08
d92b  18            clc
d92c  65 76         adc enemy_ram_offset
d92e  85 76         sta enemy_ram_offset
d930  c9 e0         cmp #$e0
d932  d0 e0         bne .iteration_loop
                    ; setup starfield decceleration
d934  e6 5c         inc phase_kill_counter
d936  a9 40         lda #$40
d938  85 61         sta phase_spawn_counter
                    ; reset palettes
d93a  20 39 d7      jsr phase_palette_load
                    ; setup next scene
d93d  20 04 f9      jsr sfx_enemy_death
d940  20 33 d4      jsr powerup_pickup_plus_one
                    ; startup in game music
d943  a9 02         lda #song_in_game
d945  20 4b fb      jsr song_start
                    ; let crossbones animate
d948  a9 00         lda #$00
d94a  85 7b         sta boss_death_happening
                .done
d94c  60            rts
      
      
                phase_boss_fight_cooldown: subroutine
d94d  a5 61         lda phase_spawn_counter
d94f  29 0f         and #$0f
d951  d0 02         bne .dont_slow_stars
d953  c6 06         dec scroll_speed_hi
                .dont_slow_stars
d955  c6 61         dec phase_spawn_counter
d957  d0 12         bne .dont_advance
d959  a9 00         lda #$00
d95b  85 7b         sta boss_death_happening
d95d  85 7c         sta boss_heart_stars
d95f  85 7a         sta boss_dmg_handle_true
d961  85 5c         sta phase_kill_counter
d963  a9 02         lda #song_in_game
d965  20 4b fb      jsr song_start
d968  20 e2 d6      jsr phase_next
                .dont_advance
d96b  60            rts
      
      
      
      
      
                phase_interval_spawn: subroutine
                    ; dont spawn if boss is dying
d96c  a5 7b         lda boss_death_happening
d96e  d0 56         bne .no_enemy
                    ; check for ike's mom spawn
d970  a5 25         lda ftw
                    ;cmp #84 ; about 6 minutes
d972  c9 2a         cmp #42 ; about 3 minutes
d974  90 04         bcc .no_ikes_mom
d976  a0 15         ldy #ikes_mom_id
d978  d0 4d         bne phase_interval_spawn_special_4_sprite
                .no_ikes_mom
                    ; interval enemy spawn?
d97a  a5 44         lda timer_frames_1s
d97c  c9 60         cmp #char_set_offset
d97e  d0 46         bne .no_enemy
d980  a5 45         lda timer_frames_10s
d982  c9 60         cmp #char_set_offset
d984  d0 40         bne .no_enemy
                    ; look for 10 second increments
d986  a5 46         lda timer_seconds_1s
                    ; if seconds == 0 then starglasses possible
d988  c9 60         cmp #char_set_offset
d98a  f0 0a         beq .starglasses_possible
                    ; if seconds == 1 or 7 then spawn enemy
d98c  c9 61         cmp #char_set_offset+1
d98e  f0 14         beq .spawn_enemy
d990  c9 67         cmp #char_set_offset+7
d992  f0 10         beq .spawn_enemy
d994  d0 30         bne .no_enemy
                .starglasses_possible
d996  a5 47         lda timer_seconds_10s
d998  c9 63         cmp #char_set_offset+3
d99a  f0 04         beq .starglasses_happening
d99c  c9 65         cmp #char_set_offset+5
d99e  d0 26         bne .no_enemy
                .starglasses_happening
d9a0  a0 0f         ldy #starglasses_id
d9a2  d0 23         bne phase_interval_spawn_special_4_sprite
                .spawn_enemy
d9a4  a4 5b         ldy phase_level
d9a6  b9 e1 d9      lda level_intervals_lo,y
d9a9  85 88         sta temp02
d9ab  b9 e5 d9      lda level_intervals_hi,y
d9ae  85 89         sta temp03
d9b0  a4 62         ldy phase_interval_counter
d9b2  b1 88         lda (temp02),y
d9b4  d0 04         bne .dont_reset_counter
                .reset_counter
d9b6  85 62         sta phase_interval_counter
d9b8  b1 88         lda (temp02),y
                .dont_reset_counter
d9ba  a8            tay
d9bb  20 72 dc      jsr enemy_slot_from_type
d9be  b0 06         bcs .no_enemy
d9c0  e6 62         inc phase_interval_counter
d9c2  98            tya
d9c3  20 77 da      jsr enemy_spawn_delegator
                .no_enemy
d9c6  60            rts
      
                phase_interval_spawn_special_4_sprite: subroutine
d9c7  20 b0 dc      jsr get_enemy_slot_4_sprite
d9ca  b0 04         bcs .nope
d9cc  98            tya
d9cd  20 77 da      jsr enemy_spawn_delegator
                .nope
d9d0  60            rts
      
      
      
      

phase_table.asm
                    ; phase type constants
                    phase_zero_id		EQM	#$00	; "good luck" / "congration"
                    phase_galger_id		EQM	#$01
                    phase_spawns_id		EQM	#$02
                    phase_longspawn_id	EQM	#$03
                    phase_bossfight_id	EQM	#$04
      
                phase_type_table:
d9d1  byte          #phase_zero_id ; 0
d9d2  byte          #phase_galger_id ; 1
d9d3  byte          #phase_spawns_id ; 2
d9d4  byte          #phase_galger_id ; 3
d9d5  byte          #phase_spawns_id ; 4
d9d6  byte          #phase_galger_id ; 5
d9d7  byte          #phase_longspawn_id ; 6
d9d8  byte          #phase_galger_id ; 7
d9d9  byte          #phase_spawns_id ; 8
d9da  byte          #phase_galger_id ; 9
d9db  byte          #phase_galger_id ; a
d9dc  byte          #phase_spawns_id ; b
d9dd  byte          #phase_galger_id ; c
d9de  byte          #phase_longspawn_id ; d
d9df  byte          #phase_galger_id ; e
d9e0  byte          #phase_bossfight_id ; f
      
      
      
      
                    ; OLD DEMO PHASES
                    ; 1: 1 birb
                    ; 2: 2 maggs
                    ; 3: 2 starglasses
                    ; 4: 1 skully
                    ; 5: 2 starglasses, 2 maggs, 4 birbs
                    ; 6: 8 birbs, 4 maggs
                    ; 7: 12 birbs, 3 skullys
                    ; 8: 12 birbs, 1 skully, 2 maggs
                    ; 9: 16 birbs, 6 skullys, 4 maggs, 2 starglasses

                level_intervals_hi:

d9e6  byte          #>level_1_interval_spawn_table
d9e7  byte          #>level_2_interval_spawn_table
d9e8  byte          #>level_3_interval_spawn_table

                level_3_interval_spawn_table:

enemy_handler.dasm
      
      
                    ;;;; Constants
      
                    ENEMY_HIT_PALETTE_FRAMES	EQM $05
      
      02 03         oam_ram_x      = $0203
      02 00         oam_ram_y      = $0200
      02 01         oam_ram_spr    = $0201
      02 02         oam_ram_att    = $0202
      
      02 20         oam_enemy_sprites = $0220
      
      
      03 00         enemy_ram_type = $0300
      03 01         enemy_ram_hp   = $0301
      03 02         enemy_ram_x    = $0302
      03 03         enemy_ram_y    = $0303
      03 04         enemy_ram_pc   = $0304
      03 05         enemy_ram_ac   = $0305
      03 06         enemy_ram_hc   = $0306
      03 07         enemy_ram_ex   = $0307
      
      
      
                enemy_spawn_delegator: subroutine
                    ; a = enemy type id
                    ; x = enemy ram offset
da77  9d 00 03      sta enemy_ram_type,x
da7a  a8            tay
da7b  84 86         sty temp00 ; enemy type
                    ; do hit point maths
                    ; hp * (game difficulty + 1)
da7d  b9 c5 dc      lda enemy_hitpoints_table,y
da80  85 87         sta temp01 ; enemy hp
da82  a9 01         lda #$01
da84  18            clc
da85  65 23         adc game_difficulty
da87  a8            tay
da88  a9 00         lda #$00
                .enemy_hp_loop
da8a  65 87         adc temp01
da8c  88            dey
da8d  d0 fb         bne .enemy_hp_loop
                    ; save hp result
da8f  9d 01 03      sta enemy_ram_hp,x
                    ; jump to enemy type spawner
da92  a5 86         lda temp00 ; enemy type
da94  18            clc
da95  69 1b         adc #enemy_spawn_jump_table_offset
da97  4c f1 c4      jmp jump_to_subroutine
      
      
                enemy_do_nothing: subroutine
da9a  4c 92 db      jmp update_enemies_handler_next
      
      
                sprite_4_set_palette: subroutine
                    ; a = palette id
                    ; x = enemy ram offset
                    ; y = oam ram offset
da9d  20 39 dc      jsr enemy_set_palette
daa0  99 06 02      sta oam_ram_att+$04,y
daa3  99 0a 02      sta oam_ram_att+$08,y
daa6  99 0e 02      sta oam_ram_att+$0c,y
daa9  60            rts
      
                sprite_4_set_palette_no_process: subroutine
                    ; a = palette id
                    ; y = oam ram offset
daaa  99 02 02      sta oam_ram_att+$00,y
daad  99 06 02      sta oam_ram_att+$04,y
dab0  99 0a 02      sta oam_ram_att+$08,y
dab3  99 0e 02      sta oam_ram_att+$0c,y
dab6  60            rts
      
                sprite_4_set_sprite: subroutine
                    ; a = top left tile id
                    ; y = oam ram offset
dab7  99 01 02      sta oam_ram_spr,y
daba  18            clc
dabb  69 01         adc #$01
dabd  99 05 02      sta oam_ram_spr+$04,y
dac0  69 0f         adc #$0f
dac2  99 09 02      sta oam_ram_spr+$08,y
dac5  69 01         adc #$01
dac7  99 0d 02      sta oam_ram_spr+$0c,y
daca  60            rts
      
                sprite_4_set_sprite_flip: subroutine
                    ; a = top left tile id
                    ; y = oam ram offset
dacb  99 09 02      sta oam_ram_spr+$08,y
dace  18            clc
dacf  69 01         adc #$01
dad1  99 0d 02      sta oam_ram_spr+$0c,y
dad4  69 0f         adc #$0f
dad6  99 01 02      sta oam_ram_spr,y
dad9  69 01         adc #$01
dadb  99 05 02      sta oam_ram_spr+$04,y
dade  60            rts
      
                sprite_4_set_x: subroutine
                    ; a = x pos
                    ; y = oam ram offset
dadf  99 03 02      sta oam_ram_x,y
dae2  99 0b 02      sta oam_ram_x+$08,y
dae5  18            clc
dae6  69 08         adc #$08
dae8  99 07 02      sta oam_ram_x+$04,y
daeb  99 0f 02      sta oam_ram_x+$0c,y
daee  60            rts
      
                sprite_4_set_y: subroutine
                    ; a = y pos
                    ; y = oam ram offset
daef  99 00 02      sta oam_ram_y,y
daf2  99 04 02      sta oam_ram_y+$04,y
daf5  18            clc
daf6  69 08         adc #$08
daf8  99 08 02      sta oam_ram_y+$08,y
dafb  99 0c 02      sta oam_ram_y+$0c,y
dafe  60            rts
      
      
      
                enemy_fix_y_visible: subroutine
                    ; a = y position to check
                    ; returns new y position in a
daff  c9 f7         cmp #255-8
db01  90 0f         bcc .not_wrapped_from_top
db03  85 86         sta temp00
db05  a9 ff         lda #255
db07  38            sec
db08  e9 b6         sbc #sprite_0_y
db0a  85 87         sta temp01
db0c  a5 86         lda temp00
db0e  38            sec
db0f  e5 87         sbc temp01
db11  60            rts
                .not_wrapped_from_top
db12  c9 b6         cmp #sprite_0_y
db14  90 03         bcc .not_wrapped_from_bottom
db16  38            sec
db17  e9 b6         sbc #sprite_0_y
                .not_wrapped_from_bottom
db19  60            rts
      
      
                enemy_spawn_set_x_y_rng: subroutine
                    ; x = enemy_ram_offset
db1a  20 52 dc      jsr get_oam_offset_from_ram_offset
db1d  20 52 c1      jsr get_next_random
db20  30 0b         bmi .rng_y
                .rng_x
db22  a5 27         lda rng1
db24  99 03 02      sta oam_ram_x,y
db27  a9 00         lda #$00
db29  99 00 02      sta oam_ram_y,y
db2c  60            rts
                .rng_y
db2d  a9 00         lda #$00
db2f  99 03 02      sta oam_ram_x,y
db32  20 d1 db      jsr enemy_spawn_random_y_pos
db35  99 00 02      sta oam_ram_y,y
db38  60            rts
      
      
      
      
                enemy_slot_offset_to_oam_offset:
                    ; slots leftshift 3 times to get enemy_ram_offset
                    ; 1 sprite slots : start at $300
                    ; 16 slots using 1 sprite
db39  byte          $20,$24,$28,$2c,$30,$34,$38,$3c
db41  byte          $40,$44,$48,$4c,$50,$54,$58,$5c
                    ; 2 sprite slots : start at $380
                    ; 4 slots using 2 sprites
db49  byte          $60,$68,$70,$78
                    ; 4 sprite slots : start at $3a0
                    ; 8 slots using 4 sprites
db4d  byte          $80,$90,$a0,$b0,$c0,$d0,$e0,$f0
                    ; null terminater
db55  byte          $00
      
      
      
      
                enemies_update_all: subroutine
db56  a5 7b         lda boss_death_happening
db58  d0 4a         bne .done
                    ; set max darts launched per frame
db5a  a9 03         lda #$03
db5c  85 70         sta dart_frame_max
                    ; if player is dead despawn
db5e  a5 0b         lda player_health
db60  d0 02         bne .player_alive
db62  f0 41         beq player_dead_enemies_despawn
                .player_alive
                update_enemies_reset:
                    ; set vars for processing
db64  a2 00         ldx #$00
db66  86 78         stx enemy_slot_id
db68  86 76         stx enemy_ram_offset
db6a  bc 39 db      ldy enemy_slot_offset_to_oam_offset,x
db6d  84 77         sty enemy_oam_offset
                    ; cycle through all the slots
                .handler_loop
db6f  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
                    ; get enemy type
db71  bd 00 03      lda enemy_ram_type,x
                    ; not defined go next slot
db74  f0 1c         beq .handler_next
db76  aa            tax
db77  bd 03 c5      lda enemy_update_jump_table_lo,x
db7a  85 86         sta temp00
db7c  bd 92 c5      lda enemy_update_jump_table_hi,x
db7f  85 87         sta temp01
                    ; setup collision detection enemy origin pos
db81  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
db83  a4 77         ldy enemy_oam_offset
db85  b9 03 02      lda oam_ram_x,y
db88  85 3b         sta collision_0_x
db8a  b9 00 02      lda oam_ram_y,y
db8d  85 3c         sta collision_0_y
db8f  6c 86 00      jmp (temp00)
                update_enemies_handler_next:
                .handler_next
                    ; go to next enemy slot
db92  e6 78         inc enemy_slot_id
db94  a5 78         lda enemy_slot_id
db96  0a            asl
db97  0a            asl
db98  0a            asl
db99  85 76         sta enemy_ram_offset
db9b  a6 78         ldx enemy_slot_id
db9d  bc 39 db      ldy enemy_slot_offset_to_oam_offset,x
dba0  84 77         sty enemy_oam_offset
dba2  d0 cb         bne .handler_loop
                .done
dba4  60            rts
      
      
      
      
                player_dead_enemies_despawn: subroutine
                    ; enemies slide off left side of screen
                    ; after player dies
dba5  a2 df         ldx #$df
                .loop
dba7  bd 20 02      lda oam_enemy_sprites,x
dbaa  f0 14         beq .next
dbac  38            sec
dbad  e5 65         sbc player_death_flag
dbaf  9d 20 02      sta oam_enemy_sprites,x
dbb2  c9 f0         cmp #$f0
dbb4  90 0a         bcc .next
                .despawn
dbb6  a9 00         lda #$00
dbb8  9d 20 02      sta oam_enemy_sprites,x
dbbb  a9 ff         lda #$ff
dbbd  9d 1d 02      sta oam_enemy_sprites-3,x
                .next
dbc0  ca            dex
dbc1  ca            dex
dbc2  ca            dex
dbc3  ca            dex
dbc4  e0 ff         cpx #$ff
dbc6  d0 df         bne .loop
dbc8  a5 24         lda wtf
dbca  29 01         and #$01
dbcc  d0 02         bne .dont_increase_velocity
dbce  e6 65         inc player_death_flag
                .dont_increase_velocity
dbd0  60            rts
      
      
      
                enemy_spawn_random_y_pos: subroutine
dbd1  20 52 c1      jsr get_next_random
dbd4  4a            lsr
dbd5  18            clc
dbd6  69 10         adc #$10
dbd8  60            rts
      
      
      
      
      
      
                game_difficulty_damage_table:
                    ;hits to die  17   10    7   6
dbd9  byte          #15, #26, #37, #48
                    ;hits to die  13   10    7   5
                    ;byte  #20, #26, #37, #58
                    ;hits to die  23   17   11   5
                    ;byte  #11, #15, #25, #58
                    ;hits to die 5    4    3  2
                    ;byte  #58, #77, #109, #191
      
                enemy_gives_damage: subroutine
dbdd  a4 23         ldy game_difficulty
dbdf  b9 d9 db      lda game_difficulty_damage_table,y
dbe2  85 21         sta player_damage
dbe4  20 5e f2      jsr player_take_damage
dbe7  20 6f f8      jsr sfx_player_damage
dbea  60            rts
      
                enemy_handle_damage_and_death: subroutine
                    ; x = enemy ram offset
                    ; y = oam ram offset
dbeb  a9 00         lda #$00
dbed  85 79         sta enemy_dmg_accumulator
                    ; handle bullet collisions
dbef  20 04 f3      jsr player_bullet_collision_handler
dbf2  20 35 cf      jsr player_collision_detect
dbf5  f0 0a         beq .no_collision
                    ; handle player collision
                    ; player damage
dbf7  20 dd db      jsr enemy_gives_damage
                    ; enemy damage
dbfa  a5 79         lda enemy_dmg_accumulator
dbfc  18            clc
dbfd  69 02         adc #$02
dbff  85 79         sta enemy_dmg_accumulator
                .no_collision
                    ; handle bomb explosion
dc01  a5 6a         lda bomb_counter
dc03  f0 08         beq .no_bomb_damage
dc05  a5 24         lda wtf
dc07  29 01         and #1
dc09  f0 02         beq .no_bomb_damage
dc0b  e6 79         inc enemy_dmg_accumulator
                .no_bomb_damage
                    ; fix y register
dc0d  a4 77         ldy enemy_oam_offset
                    ; handle damage accumulated
dc0f  a5 79         lda enemy_dmg_accumulator
dc11  d0 01         bne .hit
dc13  60            rts
                .hit
dc14  bd 01 03      lda enemy_ram_hp,x
dc17  38            sec
dc18  e5 79         sbc enemy_dmg_accumulator
dc1a  9d 01 03      sta enemy_ram_hp,x
dc1d  90 09         bcc .dead
      
dc1f  a9 05         lda #ENEMY_HIT_PALETTE_FRAMES
dc21  9d 06 03      sta enemy_ram_hc,x
dc24  20 e3 f8      jsr sfx_enemy_damage
dc27  60            rts
                .dead
                    ; boss death check
dc28  a5 7a         lda boss_dmg_handle_true
dc2a  f0 05         beq .not_boss_death
dc2c  e6 7b         inc boss_death_happening
dc2e  e6 7c         inc boss_heart_stars
dc30  60            rts
                .not_boss_death
dc31  20 62 dd      jsr enemy_death_handler
                    ; return to enemy handler not cycle
dc34  68            pla
dc35  68            pla
dc36  4c 92 db      jmp update_enemies_handler_next
      
      
      
                enemy_set_palette: subroutine
                    ; checks/handles hit palette counter
                    ; a = enemy normal palette
                    ; x = enemy ram offset
                    ; y = oam ram offset
                    ; leaves correct palette id in a
                    ; can be applied to extra sprites of larger enemy
dc39  18            clc ; set carry for branching on return
dc3a  85 86         sta temp00
dc3c  bd 06 03      lda enemy_ram_hc,x
dc3f  f0 0b         beq .use_enemy_palette
                .use_hit_palette
dc41  de 06 03      dec enemy_ram_hc,x
dc44  a5 86         lda temp00
dc46  29 fc         and #%11111100
dc48  99 02 02      sta oam_ram_att,y
dc4b  60            rts
                .use_enemy_palette
dc4c  a5 86         lda temp00
dc4e  99 02 02      sta oam_ram_att,y
dc51  60            rts
      
      
      
      
      
                    ; 16 1 sprite enemies
                    ; 4  2 sprite enemies
                    ; 8  4 sprite enemies
                    ; 28 total on screen enemies / entities
                    ; 1 sprite can occupy all slots
                    ; 2 sprite can occupy 12 slots
                    ; 4 sprite restricted to their 8 slots
      
      
                    get_oam_offset_from_ram_offset: subroutine
                    ; x = slot offset
                    ; returns oam offset in y
dc52  86 86         stx temp00
dc54  8a            txa
dc55  4a            lsr
dc56  4a            lsr
dc57  4a            lsr
dc58  aa            tax
dc59  bc 39 db      ldy enemy_slot_offset_to_oam_offset,x
dc5c  a6 86         ldx temp00
dc5e  60            rts
      
      
      
      
                get_enemy_slot_next: subroutine
                    ; returns enemy_ram_offset in x
                    ; works from the top down
dc5f  a9 d8         lda #$d8
dc61  38            sec
                .slot_loop
dc62  aa            tax
dc63  86 86         stx temp00
dc65  bd 00 03      lda $0300,x
dc68  f0 07         beq .slot_found
dc6a  a5 86         lda temp00
dc6c  e9 08         sbc #$08
dc6e  b0 f2         bcs .slot_loop
                .no_slot
dc70  18            clc
                .slot_found
dc71  60            rts
      
      
                enemy_slot_from_type: subroutine
                    ; y = enemy type id
                    ; returns slot offset in x
dc72  be e0 dc      ldx enemy_size_table,y
dc75  e0 01         cpx #1
dc77  d0 03         bne .not_size_1
dc79  4c 86 dc      jmp get_enemy_slot_1_sprite
                .not_size_1
dc7c  e0 02         cpx #2
dc7e  d0 03         bne .not_size_2
dc80  4c 9b dc      jmp get_enemy_slot_2_sprite
                .not_size_2
dc83  4c b0 dc      jmp get_enemy_slot_4_sprite
      
      
                get_enemy_slot_1_sprite: subroutine
                    ; returns enemy_ram offset in x
                    ; carry cleared if found
                    ; carry set if unfound
dc86  a9 00         lda #$00
dc88  18            clc
                .slot_loop
dc89  aa            tax
dc8a  86 86         stx temp00
dc8c  bd 00 03      lda $0300,x
dc8f  f0 09         beq .slot_found
dc91  a9 08         lda #$08
dc93  65 86         adc temp00
dc95  c9 80         cmp #$80
dc97  d0 f0         bne .slot_loop
                .no_available_slot
dc99  38            sec
                .slot_found
dc9a  60            rts
      
                get_enemy_slot_2_sprite: subroutine
                    ; returns enemy_ram offset in x
dc9b  a9 80         lda #$80
dc9d  18            clc
                .slot_loop
dc9e  aa            tax
dc9f  86 86         stx temp00
dca1  bd 00 03      lda $0300,x
dca4  f0 09         beq .slot_found
dca6  a9 08         lda #$08
dca8  65 86         adc temp00
dcaa  c9 a0         cmp #$a0
dcac  d0 f0         bne .slot_loop
                .no_available_slot
dcae  38            sec
                .slot_found
dcaf  60            rts
      
                get_enemy_slot_4_sprite: subroutine
                    ; returns enemy_ram offset in x
dcb0  a9 a0         lda #$a0
dcb2  18            clc
                .slot_loop
dcb3  aa            tax
dcb4  86 86         stx temp00
dcb6  bd 00 03      lda $0300,x
dcb9  f0 09         beq .slot_found
dcbb  a9 08         lda #$08
dcbd  65 86         adc temp00
dcbf  c9 e0         cmp #$e0
dcc1  d0 f0         bne .slot_loop
                .no_available_slot
dcc3  38            sec
                .slot_found
dcc4  60            rts
