enemy_death.asm
      
      
                enemy_accelerates_upwards: subroutine
dcfb  fe 05 03      inc enemy_ram_ac,x
dcfe  bd 05 03      lda enemy_ram_ac,x
dd01  4a            lsr
dd02  4a            lsr
dd03  4a            lsr
dd04  9d 03 03      sta enemy_ram_y,x
dd07  b9 00 02      lda oam_ram_y,y
dd0a  38            sec
dd0b  fd 03 03      sbc enemy_ram_y,x
dd0e  c9 b6         cmp #sprite_0_y
dd10  60            rts
      
      
                crossbones_cycle: subroutine
dd11  20 fb dc      jsr enemy_accelerates_upwards
dd14  b0 0f         bcs .crossbones_death
dd16  99 00 02      sta oam_ram_y,y
dd19  a9 c1         lda #$c1
dd1b  99 01 02      sta oam_ram_spr,y
dd1e  a9 03         lda #$03
dd20  99 02 02      sta oam_ram_att,y
dd23  d0 0a         bne .crossbones_done
                .crossbones_death
dd25  a5 bd         lda sfx_noi_counter
dd27  d0 03         bne .die_already
dd29  20 2c fb      jsr sfx_ghost_snare
                .die_already
dd2c  20 47 dd      jsr enemy_clear
                .crossbones_done
dd2f  4c 92 db      jmp update_enemies_handler_next
      
      
      
      
      
      
      
                clear_all_enemies: subroutine
                    ; clears all enemy ram
dd32  a2 f0         ldx #$f0
dd34  a9 00         lda #$00
                .enemy_loop
dd36  ca            dex
dd37  9d 00 03      sta enemy_ram_type,x
dd3a  d0 fa         bne .enemy_loop
                    ; clears all enemy sprites
dd3c  a9 ff         lda #$ff
dd3e  a2 20         ldx #$20
                .sprite_loop
dd40  9d 00 02      sta $0200,x
dd43  e8            inx
dd44  d0 fa         bne .sprite_loop
dd46  60            rts
      
      
      
                enemy_clear: subroutine
                    ; clear OAM data
dd47  a4 77         ldy enemy_oam_offset
dd49  a9 ff         lda #$ff
dd4b  a2 03         ldx #$03
                .enemy_oam_loop
dd4d  99 00 02      sta oam_ram_y,y
dd50  c8            iny
dd51  ca            dex
dd52  d0 f9         bne .enemy_oam_loop
                    ; clear enemy data
dd54  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
dd56  a9 00         lda #$00
dd58  a0 06         ldy #$06 ; 7 would clear _ex
                .enemy_ram_loop
dd5a  9d 00 03      sta enemy_ram_type,x
dd5d  e8            inx
dd5e  88            dey
dd5f  d0 f9         bne .enemy_ram_loop
                    ; enemy_ram_ex is allowed to carry over
                    ; entities using _ex should set it on spawn
dd61  60            rts
      
      
      
                enemy_death_handler: subroutine
                    ; requires
                    ; enemy_ram_offset
                    ; enemy_oam_offset
      
                    ; don't handle a non entity
dd62  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
dd64  bd 00 03      lda enemy_ram_type,x
dd67  f0 59         beq .done
      
dd69  e6 64         inc player_frag_counter
      
                    ; track deaths of phase spawns
dd6b  a4 78         ldy enemy_slot_id
dd6d  b9 50 01      lda phase_spawn_table,y
dd70  f0 0b         beq .not_phase_spawn
dd72  a9 00         lda #$00
dd74  99 50 01      sta phase_spawn_table,y
dd77  c6 5c         dec phase_kill_counter
                    ; if less than 0 set to 0
dd79  10 02         bpl .not_phase_spawn
dd7b  85 5c         sta phase_kill_counter
                .not_phase_spawn
      
dd7d  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
dd7f  a4 77         ldy enemy_oam_offset
                    ; 4 sprite cleanup
dd81  e0 a0         cpx #$a0
dd83  90 1f         bcc .dont_cleanup_4_sprite
dd85  a9 04         lda #$04 ; move first sprite over 4 pixels
dd87  18            clc
dd88  79 03 02      adc oam_ram_x,y
dd8b  99 03 02      sta oam_ram_x,y
dd8e  a9 04         lda #$04 ; move first sprite down 4 pixels
dd90  18            clc
dd91  79 00 02      adc oam_ram_y,y
dd94  99 00 02      sta oam_ram_y,y
                    ; move other sprites off screen
dd97  a9 ff         lda #$ff
dd99  99 04 02      sta oam_ram_y+4,y
dd9c  99 08 02      sta oam_ram_y+8,y
dd9f  99 0c 02      sta oam_ram_y+12,y
dda2  d0 09         bne .check_for_powerup_drop
                .dont_cleanup_4_sprite
      
                    ; 2 sprite cleanup
dda4  e0 80         cpx #$80
dda6  90 05         bcc .dont_cleanup_2_sprite
dda8  a9 ff         lda #$ff
ddaa  99 04 02      sta oam_ram_y+4,y
                .dont_cleanup_2_sprite
      
                .check_for_powerup_drop
                    ; starglasses
ddad  bd 00 03      lda enemy_ram_type,x
ddb0  c9 0f         cmp #starglasses_id
ddb2  d0 03         bne .not_starglasses
                    ; spawn powerup
ddb4  4c 18 d3      jmp powerup_from_starglasses
                .not_starglasses
      
ddb7  20 04 f9      jsr sfx_enemy_death
                    ; change it into crossbones!
ddba  a9 01         lda #crossbones_id
ddbc  9d 00 03      sta enemy_ram_type,x
ddbf  9d 05 03      sta enemy_ram_ac,x
                .done
ddc2  60            rts

enemy_boss_moufs.asm
      
      
                    ; boss_x : x origin of sine pattern
                    ; boss_y : y origin of sine pattern
                    ; state_v0 : bottom lip position
                    ; state_v1 : bottom lip animation counter
                    ; state_v2 :
                    ; state_v3 : y bounce sine direction
                    ; state_v4 : sprite x position
                    ; state_v5 : sprite y position
                    ; state_v6 : upper lip up sprite offset
      
      
                boss_moufs_spawn: subroutine
ddc3  a9 03         lda #boss_assist_id
                    ; eyeballs
ddc5  8d 40 03      sta $0340
ddc8  8d 48 03      sta $0348
                    ; lip rows
ddcb  8d c0 03      sta $03c0
ddce  8d c8 03      sta $03c8
ddd1  8d d0 03      sta $03d0
ddd4  a9 40         lda #$40
ddd6  9d 02 03      sta enemy_ram_x,x
ddd9  85 4e         sta state_v4
dddb  9d 03 03      sta enemy_ram_y,x
ddde  85 4f         sta state_v5
dde0  a9 08         lda #$08
dde2  85 7d         sta boss_x
dde4  a9 20         lda #$20
dde6  85 7e         sta boss_y
dde8  60            rts
      
      
      
                moufs_oam_offset_table:
dde9  byte          $b0, $c0, $e0, $d0
                moufs_sprite_offset_table:
dded  byte          $00, $10, $20, $30
                moufs_y_offset_table:
ddf1  byte          $00, $08, $0d, $10
      
      
                boss_moufs_cycle: subroutine
ddf5  a5 4e         lda state_v4
ddf7  85 3b         sta collision_0_x
ddf9  a5 4f         lda state_v5
ddfb  85 3c         sta collision_0_y
ddfd  a9 14         lda #$14
ddff  85 3d         sta collision_0_w
de01  a9 20         lda #$20
de03  85 3e         sta collision_0_h
      
de05  e6 7a         inc boss_dmg_handle_true
de07  20 eb db      jsr enemy_handle_damage_and_death
de0a  c6 7a         dec boss_dmg_handle_true
      
                    ; set upper lip default sprite
de0c  a9 00         lda #$00
de0e  85 50         sta state_v6
      
                    ; sine bounce movement
                    ; ac does x axis
de10  fe 05 03      inc enemy_ram_ac,x
                    ; pc does y axis
de13  a5 4d         lda state_v3
de15  29 01         and #$01
de17  d0 0f         bne .y_increase
                .y_decrease
                    ; arc backwards
de19  de 04 03      dec enemy_ram_pc,x
de1c  bd 04 03      lda enemy_ram_pc,x
de1f  c9 b3         cmp #$b3
de21  b0 11         bcs .y_change_done
de23  e6 50         inc state_v6
de25  4c 34 de      jmp .y_change_done
                .y_increase
                    ; arc forwards
de28  fe 04 03      inc enemy_ram_pc,x
de2b  bd 04 03      lda enemy_ram_pc,x
de2e  c9 ce         cmp #$ce
de30  90 02         bcc .y_change_done
de32  e6 50         inc state_v6
                .y_change_done
de34  bc 04 03      ldy enemy_ram_pc,x
de37  b9 00 fe      lda sine_table,y
de3a  a4 77         ldy enemy_oam_offset
de3c  c9 80         cmp #$80
de3e  d0 02         bne .dont_change_dir
de40  e6 4d         inc state_v3
                .dont_change_dir
                    ; x
de42  bd 05 03      lda enemy_ram_ac,x
de45  18            clc
de46  69 40         adc #$40
de48  4a            lsr
de49  aa            tax
de4a  bd 00 05      lda sine_6bits,x
de4d  18            clc
de4e  65 7d         adc boss_x
de50  85 4e         sta state_v4
de52  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
                    ; y
de54  bd 04 03      lda enemy_ram_pc,x
de57  aa            tax
de58  bd 00 fe      lda sine_table,x
de5b  18            clc
de5c  65 7e         adc boss_y
de5e  85 4f         sta state_v5
de60  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
      
                    ; palette
de62  a9 01         lda #$01
de64  20 9d da      jsr sprite_4_set_palette
de67  85 87         sta temp01
      
      
                    ; EYEBALLLZ
de69  a5 15         lda player_y_hi
de6b  c5 4f         cmp state_v5
de6d  b0 04         bcs .look_down
                .look_up
de6f  a9 c6         lda #$c6
de71  d0 02         bne .look_set_sprites
                .look_down
de73  a9 d6         lda #$d6
                .look_set_sprites
de75  8d 41 02      sta oam_ram_spr+$40
de78  8d 45 02      sta oam_ram_spr+$44
de7b  ee 45 02      inc oam_ram_spr+$44
      
de7e  a5 4e         lda state_v4
de80  8d 43 02      sta oam_ram_x+$40
de83  18            clc
de84  69 11         adc #$11
de86  8d 47 02      sta oam_ram_x+$44
de89  a5 4f         lda state_v5
de8b  e9 04         sbc #$04
de8d  8d 40 02      sta oam_ram_y+$40
de90  8d 44 02      sta oam_ram_y+$44
de93  a5 87         lda temp01
de95  f0 02         beq .hit
de97  a9 02         lda #$02
                .hit
de99  8d 42 02      sta oam_ram_att+$40
de9c  8d 46 02      sta oam_ram_att+$44
      
      
      
                    ; LIPPPPS
      
      
      
                    ; y row 3
de9f  a5 4b         lda state_v1
dea1  4a            lsr
dea2  aa            tax
dea3  bd 00 07      lda sine_2bits,x
dea6  85 88         sta temp02
      
dea8  e6 4b         inc state_v1
deaa  e6 4b         inc state_v1
deac  e6 4b         inc state_v1
      
                    ; y row 4
deae  a5 4b         lda state_v1
deb0  4a            lsr
deb1  aa            tax
deb2  bd 80 06      lda sine_3bits,x
deb5  85 4a         sta state_v0
deb7  85 89         sta temp03
      
deb9  e6 4b         inc state_v1
debb  e6 4b         inc state_v1
debd  e6 4b         inc state_v1
      
                    ; row 1 sprite
debf  a0 b0         ldy #$b0
dec1  a5 50         lda state_v6
dec3  f0 06         beq .lip_up
                .lip_down
dec5  a9 65         lda #$65
dec7  85 50         sta state_v6
dec9  d0 04         bne .upper_lip_set_sprite
                .lip_up
decb  a9 86         lda #$86
decd  85 50         sta state_v6
                .upper_lip_set_sprite
      
      
      
decf  a2 00         ldx #$00
                .mouf_sprites_loop
ded1  bc e9 dd      ldy moufs_oam_offset_table,x
                    ; sprite
                    ; a = left tile id
                    ; y = oam ram offset
ded4  e0 02         cpx #$02
ded6  b0 04         bcs .sprite_normal
                .sprite_custom
ded8  a5 50         lda state_v6
deda  d0 02         bne .sprite_chosen
                .sprite_normal
dedc  a9 86         lda #$86
                .sprite_chosen
dede  18            clc
dedf  7d ed dd      adc moufs_sprite_offset_table,x
dee2  99 01 02      sta oam_ram_spr,y
dee5  18            clc
dee6  69 01         adc #$01
dee8  99 05 02      sta oam_ram_spr+$04,y
deeb  69 01         adc #$01
deed  99 09 02      sta oam_ram_spr+$08,y
                    ; x
                    ; a = x pos
                    ; y = oam ram offset
def0  a5 4e         lda state_v4
def2  99 03 02      sta oam_ram_x,y
def5  18            clc
def6  69 08         adc #$08
def8  99 07 02      sta oam_ram_x+$04,y
defb  69 08         adc #$08
defd  99 0b 02      sta oam_ram_x+$08,y
df00  69 08         adc #$08
df02  99 0f 02      sta oam_ram_x+$0c,y
                    ; y
                    ; a = y pos
                    ; y = oam ram offset
df05  a5 4f         lda state_v5
df07  18            clc
df08  7d f1 dd      adc moufs_y_offset_table,x
df0b  e0 02         cpx #$02
df0d  90 0a         bcc .no_y_adjustments
df0f  e0 03         cpx #$03
df11  f0 04         beq .bottom_sprite
                .almost_bottom_sprite
df13  65 88         adc temp02
df15  d0 02         bne .no_y_adjustments
                .bottom_sprite
df17  65 89         adc temp03
                .no_y_adjustments
df19  99 00 02      sta oam_ram_y,y
df1c  99 04 02      sta oam_ram_y+$04,y
df1f  99 08 02      sta oam_ram_y+$08,y
                    ; pal / attr
df22  a5 87         lda temp01
df24  20 aa da      jsr sprite_4_set_palette_no_process
                    ; check next
df27  e8            inx
df28  e0 04         cpx #$04
df2a  d0 a5         bne .mouf_sprites_loop
      
      
df2c  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
      
      
      
                    ; fire time?
df2e  a5 4a         lda state_v0
df30  c9 07         cmp #$07
df32  d0 38         bne .done
df34  a5 24         lda wtf
df36  29 07         and #$07
df38  d0 32         bne .done
      
                .dart_fire
                    ; x
df3a  a5 4e         lda state_v4
df3c  18            clc
df3d  69 0a         adc #$0a
df3f  85 71         sta dart_x_origin
                    ; y
df41  a5 4f         lda state_v5
df43  18            clc
df44  69 0f         adc #$0f
df46  85 72         sta dart_y_origin
                    ; velocity
df48  bd 04 03      lda enemy_ram_pc,x
df4b  e9 80         sbc #$80
df4d  4a            lsr
df4e  4a            lsr
df4f  4a            lsr
df50  4a            lsr

                    ; sprite
df53  a9 fe         lda #$fe
                    ;lda #$00

                    ; dir adjustor
df57  a9 00         lda #$00
df59  85 75         sta dart_dir_adjust
df5b  20 a8 e7      jsr dart_spawn

df62  20 a8 e7      jsr dart_spawn
df65  a9 01         lda #$01
df67  85 75         sta dart_dir_adjust
df69  20 a8 e7      jsr dart_spawn
      
                .done

enemy_boss_vamp.asm
      
      
                    ; things the bat entities need to know
                    ; boss_x  : center offset
                    ; boss_y  : center offset
                    ; state_v0 : state
                    ; state_v1 : spawn x to target
                    ; state_v2 : x sine position
                    ; state_v3 : mouth position ( 0 = closed ; < 8 midframe ; > 8 open )
                    ; state_v4 : bat circle size / other states counter
                    ; state_v5 : bat visibility
                    ; state_v6 : rng target
                    ; state_v7 : mouth frame current
                    ; enemy_ram_pc : mouth frame target
                    ; enemy_ram_ac : idle_update counter
                    ; enemy_ram_ex : arctang direction
      
      
      
                boss_vamp_bat_spawn: subroutine
                    ; x = bat slot in enemy ram
df6f  a9 0b         lda #boss_vamp_bat_id
df71  9d 00 03      sta enemy_ram_type,x
df74  60            rts
      
      
                boss_vamp_bat_cycle: subroutine
                    ; bats visible?
df75  a5 4f         lda state_v5
df77  f0 69         beq .bats_not_visible
                    ; collision detection
df79  a9 08         lda #$08
df7b  85 3d         sta collision_0_w
df7d  85 3e         sta collision_0_h
df7f  20 eb db      jsr enemy_handle_damage_and_death
      
                    ; update x pos

df85  85 87         sta temp01 ; position on circle

df8c  90 0c         bcc .use_vamp_circle_size
df8e  bd 05 03      lda enemy_ram_ac,x
df91  fe 05 03      inc enemy_ram_ac,x
df94  fe 05 03      inc enemy_ram_ac,x
df97  fe 05 03      inc enemy_ram_ac,x
                .use_vamp_circle_size
df9a  85 88         sta temp02 ; size of circle
df9c  4a            lsr
df9d  85 89         sta temp03 ; half of circle size
df9f  a5 88         lda temp02
dfa1  a6 87         ldx temp01
dfa3  20 ad d4      jsr sine_of_scale
dfa6  18            clc
dfa7  65 7d         adc boss_x
dfa9  38            sec
dfaa  e5 89         sbc temp03
dfac  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
dfae  99 03 02      sta oam_ram_x,y
                    ; update y pos
dfb1  a5 87         lda temp01
dfb3  18            clc
dfb4  69 40         adc #$40
dfb6  aa            tax
dfb7  a5 88         lda temp02
dfb9  20 ad d4      jsr sine_of_scale
dfbc  18            clc

dfbf  38            sec

                    ; interpret animation frame
dfc7  bd 07 03      lda enemy_ram_ex,x
dfca  4a            lsr
dfcb  4a            lsr
dfcc  29 03         and #%00000011
dfce  18            clc
dfcf  69 38         adc #$38
dfd1  99 01 02      sta oam_ram_spr,y
                    ; palette
dfd4  ad be 03      lda $03be ; use vamp hc
dfd7  9d 06 03      sta enemy_ram_hc,x
dfda  a9 02         lda #$02
dfdc  20 39 dc      jsr enemy_set_palette
                .done
dfdf  4c 92 db      jmp update_enemies_handler_next
      
                .bats_not_visible
dfe2  a9 ff         lda #$ff
dfe4  99 00 02      sta oam_ram_y,y
dfe7  d0 f6         bne .done
      
      
      
                    ; I VANT TO
                    ; SUCK YOUR
                    ; BLEEDS !!
                boss_vamp_spawn: subroutine
                    ; claim eyeball slot
dfe9  a9 03         lda #boss_assist_id
dfeb  8d d8 03      sta $03d8
                    ; clear eyeball slot sprites
dfee  a9 f0         lda #$f0 ; y pos of metasprite
dff0  a8            tay ; happens to match oam offset

                    ; set eye color

dff6  85 7f         sta boss_eyes_pal
                    ; set spawn x

                    ; set spawn y
dffc  a9 15         lda #$15
dffe  9d 03 03      sta enemy_ram_y,x
                    ; bats are visible
e001  a9 01         lda #$01
e003  85 4f         sta state_v5
                    ; minimum bat circle size
e005  a9 40         lda #$40
e007  85 4e         sta state_v4
                    ; rando target
e009  a9 80         lda #$80
e00b  85 50         sta state_v6
                    ; target count of bat underlings;
                    ; #$0d = 13 but we `bpl loopin`
e00d  a0 0c         ldy #$0c
e00f  85 86         sta temp00
                    ; set temp counters to 0
e011  a9 00         lda #$00
e013  85 87         sta temp01
e015  85 88         sta temp02
                .bat_spawn_loop
                    ; a = animation counter / v1
                    ; x = slot in enemy ram
e017  a5 87         lda temp01
e019  a6 88         ldx temp02
e01b  9d 07 03      sta enemy_ram_ex,x
e01e  18            clc
e01f  69 14         adc #$14
e021  85 87         sta temp01
e023  a9 08         lda #$08
e025  65 88         adc temp02
e027  85 88         sta temp02
e029  88            dey
e02a  10 eb         bpl .bat_spawn_loop
                .done
e02c  60            rts
      
      
      
                boss_vamp_cycle: subroutine
e02d  a9 0d         lda #$0d
e02f  85 3d         sta collision_0_w
e031  a9 10         lda #$10
e033  85 3e         sta collision_0_h
      
e035  e6 7a         inc boss_dmg_handle_true
e037  20 eb db      jsr enemy_handle_damage_and_death
e03a  c6 7a         dec boss_dmg_handle_true
                    ; IS DEAD?
e03c  a5 7b         lda boss_death_happening
e03e  f0 1b         beq .not_dead
e040  c9 02         cmp #$02
e042  f0 17         beq .bats_spawned
e044  e6 4f         inc state_v5 ; bats are visible
e046  a9 56         lda #$56
e048  85 4e         sta state_v4 ; bat circle size
e04a  85 51         sta state_v7 ; mouf open
                    ; track bats are gonna spawn

e051  85 7d         sta boss_x
e053  b9 00 02      lda oam_ram_y,y
e056  85 7e         sta boss_y
                    ; reset enemy handler so bats show
e058  4c 64 db      jmp update_enemies_reset
                .bats_spawned
                .not_dead
      
                    ; MOUTH HANDLER
                    ; state_v7 : mouth frame current
                    ; enemy_ram_pc : mouth frame target
e05b  a5 51         lda state_v7

                .mouth_open

e066  d0 02         bne .mouth_is_fine
                .mouth_close
e068  c6 51         dec state_v7
                .mouth_is_fine
      
                    ; STATE behavior
e06a  20 aa e0      jsr boss_vamp_update_state_delegator
e06d  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
      
                    ; SPRITE tiles
e06f  a5 51         lda state_v7
e071  4a            lsr
e072  4a            lsr
e073  4a            lsr

e076  c9 03         cmp #$03
e078  d0 02         bne .good_frame
e07a  a9 02         lda #$02
                .good_frame
e07c  0a            asl
e07d  18            clc
e07e  69 9a         adc #$9a
e080  20 b7 da      jsr sprite_4_set_sprite
      
                    ; palette
e083  a9 01         lda #$01
e085  20 9d da      jsr sprite_4_set_palette
e088  d0 04         bne .not_hit
e08a  a9 80         lda #$80
e08c  85 51         sta state_v7
                .not_hit
      
                    ; eyeballs
e08e  b9 03 02      lda oam_ram_x,y
e091  18            clc
e092  69 03         adc #$03
e094  85 86         sta temp00
e096  b9 00 02      lda oam_ram_y,y
e099  85 87         sta temp01
e09b  b9 01 02      lda oam_ram_spr,y

e0a0  d0 02         bne .dont_adjust_for_open_mouth

                .dont_adjust_for_open_mouth
e0a4  20 b6 e5      jsr enemy_boss_eyes
      
e0a7  4c 92 db      jmp update_enemies_handler_next

                .next_state_check

                .shrink_bat_circle

                .bat_circle_adjust_done
                    ; BAT UPDATE LOOP

                .bat_update_loop

                    ; update circle position

                    ; respawn if slot is empty

e12f  a9 20         lda #$20
e131  85 51         sta state_v7
                .dont_respawn_bat

e135  18            clc

e138  85 86         sta temp00

e13c  d0 e4         bne .bat_update_loop
e13e  60            rts

                .setup_next_state

e201  85 4a         sta state_v0
                .done
e203  60            rts
      
      

enemy_boss_scarab.asm
      
                    ; things the bat entities need to know
                    ; boss_x  : topleft offset
                    ; boss_y  : topleft offset
                    ; state_v2 : x sine position
                    ; state_v3 : y sine position
                    ; state_v4 : x body position
                    ; state_v5 : y body position
                    ; state_v6 : x wing position
                    ; state_v7 : y wing position
      
                    ; scarab palette
                    ; $0c, $27, $38
      
                boss_scarab_spawn: subroutine
                    ; claim highest 4 sprite slot for beetle body
                    ;ldx #boss_scarab_id
                    ;stx $03b8
                    ; claim 4 more slots for the wings
e204  a9 03         lda #boss_assist_id
e206  8d c0 03      sta $03c0
e209  8d c8 03      sta $03c8
e20c  8d d0 03      sta $03d0
e20f  8d d8 03      sta $03d8
                    ; setup initial state
e212  a9 10         lda #$10
e214  85 7d         sta boss_x
e216  a9 1c         lda #$1c
e218  85 7e         sta boss_y
e21a  a9 00         lda #$00
e21c  85 4d         sta state_v3
e21e  85 50         sta state_v6
e220  85 51         sta state_v7
e222  a9 c0         lda #$c0
e224  85 4c         sta state_v2
e226  60            rts
      
      
                    ;   KNOW
                    ;  THYSELF
                    ; DEATHLESS
      
                scarab_oam_offset_table:
e227  byte          $c0, $d0, $e0, $f0
                scarab_wing_y_offset_table:
e22b  byte          $f4, $e4, $0c, $1c
                scarab_wing_sprite_table:
e22f  byte          $80, $60, $80, $60
      
      
                boss_scarab_cycle: subroutine
e233  a9 10         lda #$10
e235  85 3d         sta collision_0_w
e237  85 3e         sta collision_0_h
      
e239  e6 7a         inc boss_dmg_handle_true
e23b  20 eb db      jsr enemy_handle_damage_and_death
e23e  c6 7a         dec boss_dmg_handle_true
      
                    ; MAIN BODY
      
                    ; x pos
e240  e6 4c         inc state_v2
e242  a6 4c         ldx state_v2
e244  bd 00 fe      lda sine_table,x
e247  4a            lsr
e248  4a            lsr
e249  18            clc
e24a  65 7d         adc boss_x
e24c  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
e24e  9d 02 03      sta enemy_ram_x,x
e251  85 4e         sta state_v4
e253  20 df da      jsr sprite_4_set_x
      
                    ; y pos
e256  e6 4d         inc state_v3
e258  e6 4d         inc state_v3
e25a  a6 4d         ldx state_v3
e25c  bd 00 fe      lda sine_table,x
e25f  4a            lsr
e260  18            clc
e261  65 7e         adc boss_y
e263  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
e265  9d 03 03      sta enemy_ram_y,x
e268  85 4f         sta state_v5
e26a  20 ef da      jsr sprite_4_set_y
      
                    ; sprite
e26d  a9 a0         lda #$a0
e26f  20 b7 da      jsr sprite_4_set_sprite
      
                    ; WINGS
                    ; twitch offset
e272  e6 51         inc state_v7
e274  a5 51         lda state_v7
e276  c9 03         cmp #3
e278  d0 04         bne .state_v7_dont_reset
e27a  a9 00         lda #0
e27c  85 51         sta state_v7
                .state_v7_dont_reset
e27e  a5 24         lda wtf
e280  29 01         and #1
e282  85 50         sta state_v6
      
      
      
      
                    ; palette
e284  a9 02         lda #$02
e286  20 9d da      jsr sprite_4_set_palette
e289  f0 03         beq .hit
e28b  38            sec
e28c  e9 01         sbc #$01
                .hit
e28e  85 87         sta temp01
      
                    ; calc x pos of wing set
e290  a5 4e         lda state_v4
e292  38            sec
e293  e9 06         sbc #$6
e295  18            clc
e296  65 50         adc state_v6
e298  85 86         sta temp00
      
      
e29a  a2 03         ldx #$03
                .wing_sprites_loop
e29c  bc 27 e2      ldy scarab_oam_offset_table,x
                    ; x
e29f  a5 86         lda temp00
e2a1  20 df da      jsr sprite_4_set_x
                    ; y
e2a4  a5 4f         lda state_v5
e2a6  18            clc
e2a7  7d 2b e2      adc scarab_wing_y_offset_table,x
e2aa  20 ef da      jsr sprite_4_set_y
                    ; sprite
e2ad  bd 2f e2      lda scarab_wing_sprite_table,x
                    ; flipped wing?
e2b0  e0 02         cpx #$02
e2b2  90 09         bcc .no_flip
e2b4  20 cb da      jsr sprite_4_set_sprite_flip
                    ; att flipped
e2b7  a5 87         lda temp01
e2b9  09 80         ora #$80
e2bb  d0 05         bne .sprite_done
                .no_flip
e2bd  20 b7 da      jsr sprite_4_set_sprite
                    ; att
e2c0  a5 87         lda temp01
                .sprite_done
e2c2  20 aa da      jsr sprite_4_set_palette_no_process
e2c5  ca            dex
e2c6  10 d4         bpl .wing_sprites_loop
      
      
                    ; SHOOT
e2c8  a5 24         lda wtf
e2ca  29 03         and #$03
e2cc  d0 23         bne .done
e2ce  a5 24         lda wtf
e2d0  29 20         and #$20
e2d2  d0 1d         bne .done
      
                .dart_fire
e2d4  a5 4e         lda state_v4
e2d6  18            clc
e2d7  69 10         adc #$10
e2d9  85 71         sta dart_x_origin
e2db  a5 4f         lda state_v5
e2dd  18            clc
e2de  69 04         adc #$04
e2e0  85 72         sta dart_y_origin
e2e2  a9 02         lda #$02
e2e4  85 73         sta dart_velocity
e2e6  a9 fe         lda #$fe
e2e8  85 74         sta dart_sprite
e2ea  a9 00         lda #$00
e2ec  85 75         sta dart_dir_adjust
e2ee  20 a8 e7      jsr dart_spawn
      
      
                .done
e2f1  a5 24         lda wtf
e2f3  29 0f         and #$0f
e2f5  d0 08         bne .done_x_entrance
e2f7  a5 7d         lda boss_x
e2f9  c9 40         cmp #$40
e2fb  f0 02         beq .done_x_entrance
e2fd  e6 7d         inc boss_x
                .done_x_entrance
e2ff  4c 92 db      jmp update_enemies_handler_next

enemy_boss_swordtner.asm
      
                    ; things the bat entities need to know
                    ; boss_x  : x body position
                    ; boss_y  :  y body position
      
                    ; state_v0 : current mode
                    ; 0 - bounce around and fire
                    ; 1 - lunge at player and around to box
                    ; 2 - aim and shake
                    ; state_v1 : cycle counter
                    ; state_v2 : velocity
                    ; state_v4 : emiter type
                    ; state_v5 : scratch for spawn loop
                    ; state_v6 : alt face countdowner
                    ; state_v7 : 2nd form true
      
      
                    ; swordtner palette
                    ; $07, $17, $3d
                    ; $0c, $2d, $31
      
                boss_swordtner_spawn: subroutine
                    ; claim 4 more slots for rest of body
e302  a9 03         lda #boss_assist_id
e304  8d c0 03      sta $03c0
e307  8d c8 03      sta $03c8
e30a  8d d0 03      sta $03d0
e30d  8d d8 03      sta $03d8
                    ; claim 1 more slot for eyeballs
e310  8d 40 03      sta $0340
                    ; setup initial state
e313  20 52 dc      jsr get_oam_offset_from_ram_offset
e316  a9 20         lda #$20
e318  99 03 02      sta oam_ram_x,y
e31b  99 00 02      sta oam_ram_y,y
e31e  a9 15         lda #$15
e320  9d 07 03      sta enemy_ram_ex,x
                    ; set eye color
e323  a9 01         lda #$01
e325  85 7f         sta boss_eyes_pal
                    ; offset song position
e327  a9 0e         lda #$0e
e329  85 c3         sta audio_pattern_pos
e32b  60            rts
      
                    ; SWORDTNER
                    ; PROVERB
                    ; HERE
      
                arc_bounce_x:
                    ; if ex < $0c then ex = $0c - ex
e32c  hex           0b 0b 0a 09 08 07 05 05 04 03 02 01
                    ; if ex == $0c then ex = 0
                    ; if ex > $0c then ex = $17 - (ex - $0d)
e338  hex           01 17 16 15 14 13 13 11 10 0f 0e 0d
                arc_bounce_y:
                    ; if ex == $00 then ex = 0
                    ; if ex < $0c then ex = $18 - ex
e344  hex           01 17 16 15 14 13 13 11 10 0f 0e 0d
                    ; if ex > $0c then ex = $24 - ex
e350  hex           0b 0b 0a 09 08 07 05 05 04 03 02 01
      
                swordtner_metasprite_offset:
e35c  byte          #$10, #$20, #$30, #$40
                swordtner_metasprite_id:
e360  byte          #$82, #$a2, #$a2, #$c2
      
                swordtner_rng_bullet:
e364  20 52 c1      jsr get_next_random
e367  29 07         and #$07
e369  18            clc
e36a  69 24         adc #$24
e36c  85 74         sta dart_sprite
e36e  60            rts
      
      
      
                boss_swordtner_cycle: subroutine
                    ; shrink width of hit box
e36f  a9 04         lda #$04
e371  85 3d         sta collision_0_w
e373  65 3b         adc collision_0_x
e375  85 3b         sta collision_0_x
                    ; check for player collision with blade
e377  a9 50         lda #$50
e379  85 3e         sta collision_0_h
e37b  20 35 cf      jsr player_collision_detect
e37e  f0 0f         beq .no_collision
e380  20 dd db      jsr enemy_gives_damage
e383  a9 00         lda #$00
e385  85 4b         sta state_v1
e387  a9 01         lda #$01
e389  85 4a         sta state_v0
                    ; change facial experession
e38b  a9 20         lda #$20
e38d  85 50         sta state_v6
                .no_collision
      
      
                    ; hitbox is face of swordtner
e38f  a9 0f         lda #$0f
e391  85 3e         sta collision_0_h
e393  a9 12         lda #$12
e395  65 3c         adc collision_0_y
e397  85 3c         sta collision_0_y
e399  e6 7a         inc boss_dmg_handle_true
e39b  20 eb db      jsr enemy_handle_damage_and_death
e39e  c9 00         cmp #$00
e3a0  f0 04         beq .dont_change_face
e3a2  a9 10         lda #$10
e3a4  85 50         sta state_v6
                .dont_change_face
e3a6  c6 7a         dec boss_dmg_handle_true
      
      
                    ; check for 2nd Form
e3a8  a5 51         lda state_v7
e3aa  d0 11         bne .2nd_form_done
e3ac  bd 01 03      lda enemy_ram_hp,x
e3af  c9 19         cmp #25
e3b1  b0 0a         bcs .2nd_form_done
e3b3  a9 64         lda #100
e3b5  7d 01 03      adc enemy_ram_hp,x
e3b8  9d 01 03      sta enemy_ram_hp,x
e3bb  e6 51         inc state_v7
                .2nd_form_done
      
      
                    ; both modes use this
                    ; x
e3bd  a5 7d         lda boss_x
e3bf  18            clc
e3c0  69 04         adc #$04
e3c2  85 71         sta dart_x_origin
                    ; y
e3c4  a5 7e         lda boss_y
e3c6  18            clc
e3c7  69 0c         adc #$0c
e3c9  85 72         sta dart_y_origin
      
      
                    ; act on current mode
e3cb  a5 4a         lda state_v0
e3cd  18            clc
e3ce  69 3c         adc #boss_swordtner_state_jump_table_offset
e3d0  20 f1 c4      jsr jump_to_subroutine
      
      
                    ; y
e3d3  b9 00 02      lda oam_ram_y,y
                    ; y check for poor range
                .check_too_low
e3d6  c9 88         cmp #$88
e3d8  90 05         bcc .check_too_high
e3da  a9 8a         lda #$8a
e3dc  99 00 02      sta oam_ram_y,y
                .check_too_high
e3df  c9 08         cmp #$08
e3e1  b0 05         bcs .check_y_softer_bounce
e3e3  a9 06         lda #$06
e3e5  99 00 02      sta oam_ram_y,y
                .check_y_softer_bounce
                    ; y check for bounce
e3e8  c9 08         cmp #$08
e3ea  90 06         bcc .bounce_y
e3ec  c9 88         cmp #$88
e3ee  b0 02         bcs .bounce_y
e3f0  90 0b         bcc .dont_bounce_y
                .bounce_y
e3f2  bc 07 03      ldy enemy_ram_ex,x
e3f5  b9 44 e3      lda arc_bounce_y,y
e3f8  9d 07 03      sta enemy_ram_ex,x
e3fb  a4 77         ldy enemy_oam_offset
                .dont_bounce_y
      
      
                .set_boss_x_y
                    ; set boxx x,y
e3fd  b9 03 02      lda oam_ram_x,y
e400  85 7d         sta boss_x
e402  20 df da      jsr sprite_4_set_x
e405  b9 00 02      lda oam_ram_y,y
e408  85 7e         sta boss_y
e40a  20 ef da      jsr sprite_4_set_y
      
      
      
      
                    ; SWORDTNER
                    ; MAIN BODY
      
                    ; handle sprite
e40d  a9 62         lda #$62
e40f  20 b7 da      jsr sprite_4_set_sprite
      
      
e412  a2 03         ldx #$03
                .next_meta_sprite
e414  bd 5c e3      lda swordtner_metasprite_offset,x
e417  18            clc
e418  65 77         adc enemy_oam_offset
e41a  a8            tay
e41b  a5 7d         lda boss_x
e41d  20 df da      jsr sprite_4_set_x
e420  a5 7e         lda boss_y
e422  7d 5c e3      adc swordtner_metasprite_offset,x
e425  20 ef da      jsr sprite_4_set_y
                    ; check for face animation
e428  e0 00         cpx #$00
e42a  d0 0a         bne .not_face_sprite
e42c  a5 50         lda state_v6
e42e  f0 12         beq .normal_sprite
e430  c6 50         dec state_v6
e432  a9 84         lda #$84
e434  d0 0f         bne .plot_sprite
                .not_face_sprite
                    ; check for 2nd form
e436  a5 51         lda state_v7
e438  f0 08         beq .normal_sprite
e43a  bd 60 e3      lda swordtner_metasprite_id,x
e43d  18            clc
e43e  69 02         adc #$02
e440  d0 03         bne .plot_sprite
                .normal_sprite
e442  bd 60 e3      lda swordtner_metasprite_id,x
                .plot_sprite
e445  20 b7 da      jsr sprite_4_set_sprite
e448  ca            dex
e449  10 c9         bpl .next_meta_sprite
      
e44b  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
e44d  a4 77         ldy enemy_oam_offset
      
                    ; palette
e44f  a9 01         lda #$01
e451  20 9d da      jsr sprite_4_set_palette
e454  f0 03         beq .hit
e456  18            clc
e457  69 01         adc #$01
                .hit
e459  a0 c0         ldy #$c0
e45b  20 aa da      jsr sprite_4_set_palette_no_process
e45e  a0 d0         ldy #$d0
e460  20 aa da      jsr sprite_4_set_palette_no_process
e463  a0 e0         ldy #$e0
e465  20 aa da      jsr sprite_4_set_palette_no_process
e468  a0 f0         ldy #$f0
e46a  20 aa da      jsr sprite_4_set_palette_no_process
      
      
                    ; move last sprite to higher spot
                    ; make room for eyeballs
e46d  a2 03         ldx #$03
                .migrate_sprite_loop
e46f  bd fc 02      lda $02fc,x
e472  9d 40 02      sta $0240,x
e475  ca            dex
e476  10 f7         bpl .migrate_sprite_loop
      
                    ;ldx enemy_ram_offset
                    ;ldy enemy_oam_offset
      
                    ; eyeballs setup
e478  a4 77         ldy enemy_oam_offset
e47a  b9 03 02      lda oam_ram_x,y
e47d  18            clc
e47e  69 04         adc #$04
e480  85 86         sta temp00
e482  b9 00 02      lda oam_ram_y,y
e485  18            clc
e486  69 10         adc #$10
e488  85 87         sta temp01
e48a  20 b6 e5      jsr enemy_boss_eyes
      
      
                .done
e48d  4c 92 db      jmp update_enemies_handler_next
      
      
      
      
      
                swordtner_inside_bouncy_box_x: subroutine
                    ; returns 0 in a if false
e490  b9 03 02      lda oam_ram_x,y
e493  c9 10         cmp #$10
e495  90 08         bcc .outside_bouncy_box_x
e497  c9 60         cmp #$60
e499  b0 04         bcs .outside_bouncy_box_x
e49b  a9 00         lda #$00
e49d  90 02         bcc .inside_bouncy_box_x
                .outside_bouncy_box_x
e49f  a9 ff         lda #$ff
                .inside_bouncy_box_x
e4a1  60            rts
      
      
      
      
      
                swordtner_emit_enemies: subroutine
e4a2  a9 02         lda #$02
e4a4  85 4f         sta state_v5
                .spawn_loop
e4a6  20 86 dc      jsr get_enemy_slot_1_sprite
e4a9  b0 19         bcs .done
e4ab  18            clc
e4ac  a9 05         lda #spark_id
e4ae  65 4f         adc state_v5
e4b0  20 77 da      jsr enemy_spawn_delegator
e4b3  20 52 dc      jsr get_oam_offset_from_ram_offset
e4b6  a5 71         lda dart_x_origin
e4b8  99 03 02      sta oam_ram_x,y
e4bb  a5 72         lda dart_y_origin
e4bd  99 00 02      sta oam_ram_y,y

                .done
e4c4  20 5e fa      jsr sfx_shoot_bullet
e4c7  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
e4c9  a4 77         ldy enemy_oam_offset
e4cb  60            rts
      
      
      
                swordtner_emit_projectiles: subroutine
                    ; velocity
e4cc  a9 02         lda #$02
e4ce  85 73         sta dart_velocity
                    ; sprite

                    ;lda #$00
e4d2  85 74         sta dart_sprite
                    ; dir adjustor

e4d6  85 75         sta dart_dir_adjust
e4d8  20 64 e3      jsr swordtner_rng_bullet
e4db  20 a8 e7      jsr dart_spawn
e4de  a9 ff         lda #$ff
e4e0  85 75         sta dart_dir_adjust
e4e2  20 64 e3      jsr swordtner_rng_bullet
e4e5  20 a8 e7      jsr dart_spawn
e4e8  a9 01         lda #$01
e4ea  85 75         sta dart_dir_adjust
e4ec  20 64 e3      jsr swordtner_rng_bullet
e4ef  20 a8 e7      jsr dart_spawn
e4f2  60            rts
      
      
      
      
                    ; mode 0 - bounce around and fire
                boss_swordtner_mode_0: subroutine
      
                    ; fix x position after player death
e4f3  b9 03 02      lda oam_ram_x,y
e4f6  d0 08         bne .dont_fix_x
e4f8  a9 20         lda #$20
e4fa  99 03 02      sta oam_ram_x,y
e4fd  99 00 02      sta oam_ram_y,y
                .dont_fix_x
      
                .bounce_and_fire
                    ; change face before firing
e500  a5 c3         lda audio_pattern_pos
e502  c9 09         cmp #$09
e504  d0 04         bne .no_face_change
e506  a9 05         lda #$05
e508  85 50         sta state_v6
                .no_face_change
e50a  a5 c3         lda audio_pattern_pos
e50c  c9 08         cmp #8
e50e  90 07         bcc .before_snare
e510  c9 0c         cmp #12
e512  b0 0b         bcs .after_snare
e514  4c 3a e5      jmp .movement_skip
      
                .before_snare
e517  aa            tax
e518  bd f0 ff      lda arctang_velocities_lo,x
e51b  85 4c         sta state_v2
e51d  d0 04         bne .bounce_movement
      
                .after_snare
e51f  a9 80         lda #<arctang_velocity_6.66
e521  85 4c         sta state_v2
      
      
                    ; MOVEMENT
                .bounce_movement
e523  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
e525  85 84         sta arctang_velocity_lo
e527  20 f6 ce      jsr arctang_enemy_update
                    ; x
e52a  20 90 e4      jsr swordtner_inside_bouncy_box_x
e52d  f0 0b         beq .dont_bounce_x
                .bounce_x
e52f  bc 07 03      ldy enemy_ram_ex,x
e532  b9 2c e3      lda arc_bounce_x,y
e535  9d 07 03      sta enemy_ram_ex,x
e538  a4 77         ldy enemy_oam_offset
                .dont_bounce_x
      
                .movement_skip
      
      
      
                    ; spawn/fire on boss fight music main snare
e53a  a5 c1         lda audio_frame_counter
e53c  c9 05         cmp #5
e53e  d0 24         bne .dont_emit
e540  a5 c3         lda audio_pattern_pos
e542  c9 0a         cmp #10
e544  d0 1e         bne .dont_emit
e546  a5 4b         lda state_v1
e548  c9 02         cmp #$02
e54a  d0 07         bne .dont_next_state
e54c  e6 4a         inc state_v0
e54e  a9 00         lda #$00
e550  85 4b         sta state_v1
e552  60            rts
                .dont_next_state
e553  a5 4e         lda state_v4
e555  29 01         and #$01
e557  d0 06         bne .emit_enemies
                .emit_projectiles
e559  20 cc e4      jsr swordtner_emit_projectiles
e55c  4c 62 e5      jmp .inc_state
                .emit_enemies
e55f  20 a2 e4      jsr swordtner_emit_enemies
                .inc_state
e562  e6 4b         inc state_v1
                .dont_emit
      
e564  60            rts
      
      
      
                    ; mode 1 - aim for player and warn of attack
                boss_swordtner_mode_shake: subroutine
e565  a5 4b         lda state_v1
e567  d0 13         bne .init_done
                .init
e569  e6 4b         inc state_v1
e56b  20 12 ce      jsr enemy_get_direction_of_player
e56e  18            clc
e56f  69 01         adc #$01
e571  20 ae ce      jsr arctang_bound_dir
e574  9d 07 03      sta enemy_ram_ex,x
e577  ad f1 ff      lda arctang_velocities_lo+1
e57a  85 4c         sta state_v2
                .init_done
                    ; x shake
e57c  20 66 c1      jsr shake_8
e57f  65 7d         adc boss_x
e581  20 df da      jsr sprite_4_set_x
                    ; y shake
e584  20 66 c1      jsr shake_8
e587  65 7e         adc boss_y
e589  20 ef da      jsr sprite_4_set_y
                    ; check if done
e58c  e6 4b         inc state_v1
e58e  a5 4b         lda state_v1
e590  c9 10         cmp #$10
e592  d0 04         bne .done
e594  a9 02         lda #$02
e596  85 4a         sta state_v0
                .done
e598  60            rts
      
      
      
                    ; mode 2 - lunges at player
                boss_swordtner_mode_2: subroutine
e599  e6 4b         inc state_v1
e59b  a5 4b         lda state_v1
e59d  c9 40         cmp #$40
e59f  90 0d         bcc .lunge_keep_going
e5a1  20 90 e4      jsr swordtner_inside_bouncy_box_x
e5a4  d0 08         bne .lunge_keep_going
                    ; next state
e5a6  e6 4e         inc state_v4
e5a8  a9 00         lda #$00
e5aa  85 4a         sta state_v0
e5ac  85 4b         sta state_v1
                .lunge_keep_going
e5ae  a5 4c         lda state_v2
e5b0  85 84         sta arctang_velocity_lo
e5b2  20 f6 ce      jsr arctang_enemy_update
                .lunge_done
e5b5  60            rts
      
      
      
      

enemy_boss_eyes.asm
      
                    ; temp00 = eye sprite x origin
                    ; temp01 = eye sprite y origin
      
                enemy_boss_eyes: subroutine
                    ; EYE SPRITE
                .now_lets_add_eyes
e5b6  a2 fc         ldx #$fc
e5b8  a9 a9         lda #$a9
e5ba  9d 01 02      sta oam_ram_spr,x
e5bd  a5 7f         lda boss_eyes_pal
e5bf  9d 02 02      sta oam_ram_att,x
                    ; find x
e5c2  a5 86         lda temp00
e5c4  9d 03 02      sta oam_ram_x,x
                    ; check if rudy is left of vampire
e5c7  c5 13         cmp player_x_hi
e5c9  90 05         bcc .looking_right
                .looking_left
e5cb  a9 99         lda #$99
e5cd  9d 01 02      sta oam_ram_spr,x
                    ;inc oam_ram_x,x
                .looking_right
                    ; find y
e5d0  a4 87         ldy temp01
e5d2  c8            iny
e5d3  98            tya
e5d4  a4 77         ldy enemy_oam_offset
e5d6  9d 00 02      sta oam_ram_y,x
e5d9  18            clc
e5da  69 1c         adc #$1c
e5dc  38            sec
e5dd  e5 15         sbc player_y_hi
e5df  90 14         bcc .looking_down
e5e1  18            clc
e5e2  69 cc         adc #$cc
e5e4  b0 0b         bcs .looking_up
                .looking_across
e5e6  bd 01 02      lda oam_ram_spr,x
e5e9  c9 99         cmp #$99
e5eb  f0 03         beq .adjust_for_left_looking
e5ed  4c f8 e5      jmp .done
                .adjust_for_left_looking
e5f0  60            rts
                .looking_up
e5f1  de 00 02      dec oam_ram_y,x
e5f4  60            rts
                .looking_down
e5f5  fe 00 02      inc oam_ram_y,x
                .done
e5f8  60            rts

enemy_ant.asm
      
                ant_spawn: subroutine
                    ; x is set by enemy spawner
e5f9  a9 fc         lda #$fc
e5fb  9d 02 03      sta enemy_ram_x,x ; x pos
e5fe  a9 00         lda #$00
e600  9d 05 03      sta enemy_ram_ac,x ; animation counter
e603  8a            txa
e604  4a            lsr
e605  4a            lsr
e606  4a            lsr

e609  f0 0e         beq .dash_ant
                .ceiling_ant

                .dash_ant

                ant_cycle: subroutine
e625  a9 10         lda #$10
e627  85 3d         sta collision_0_w
e629  a9 10         lda #$10
e62b  85 3e         sta collision_0_h
e62d  20 eb db      jsr enemy_handle_damage_and_death
      
e630  a5 24         lda wtf
e632  4a            lsr
e633  4a            lsr
e634  29 3f         and #$3f
e636  c9 37         cmp #$37
e638  90 41         bcc .normal_walking
                .stop_and_shoot
                    ; frames default to spazzing
                    ; frames f0-f8 butt up
                    ; frame f8 shoot dart
                    ; frames f9-ff butt down
e63a  a5 24         lda wtf
e63c  c9 f9         cmp #$f9
e63e  b0 34         bcs .butt_down
e640  c9 f8         cmp #$f8
e642  b0 17         bcs .dart_fire
e644  c9 f0         cmp #$f0
e646  b0 0c         bcs .butt_up
                .butt_shake
e648  4a            lsr
e649  29 01         and #$01
e64b  0a            asl
e64c  18            clc
e64d  69 e8         adc #$e8
e64f  9d 05 03      sta enemy_ram_ac,x
e652  d0 39         bne .dont_advance
                .butt_up
e654  a9 ea         lda #$ea
e656  9d 05 03      sta enemy_ram_ac,x
e659  d0 32         bne .dont_advance
                .dart_fire
e65b  bd 02 03      lda enemy_ram_x,x
e65e  85 71         sta dart_x_origin
e660  bd 04 03      lda enemy_ram_pc,x
e663  85 72         sta dart_y_origin
e665  a9 03         lda #$03
e667  85 73         sta dart_velocity
e669  a9 00         lda #$00
e66b  85 74         sta dart_sprite
e66d  85 75         sta dart_dir_adjust
e66f  20 a8 e7      jsr dart_spawn
e672  d0 19         bne .dont_advance
                .butt_down
e674  a9 e8         lda #$e8
e676  9d 05 03      sta enemy_ram_ac,x
e679  d0 12         bne .dont_advance
      
                .normal_walking
e67b  29 03         and #$03
e67d  0a            asl
e67e  18            clc
e67f  69 e0         adc #$e0
e681  9d 05 03      sta enemy_ram_ac,x
e684  a5 24         lda wtf
e686  29 03         and #$03
e688  d0 03         bne .dont_advance
e68a  fe 02 03      inc enemy_ram_x,x
                .dont_advance
e68d  bd 02 03      lda enemy_ram_x,x
e690  20 df da      jsr sprite_4_set_x
e693  bd 03 03      lda enemy_ram_y,x
e696  20 ef da      jsr sprite_4_set_y
e699  bd 07 03      lda enemy_ram_ex,x
e69c  d0 0a         bne .ceiling_ant
                .dash_ant

                .ceiling_ant

e6ab  20 cb da      jsr sprite_4_set_sprite_flip
e6ae  a9 81         lda #$81
                .ant_pos_done

                .done

e6e3  4a            lsr

e6f9  bd 00 fe      lda sine_table,x
e6fc  4a            lsr

e6fe  4a            lsr

e701  18            clc

e715  4a            lsr

enemy_dart.asm
      
                    ; set velocity after calling spawn with :
                    ; lda #<arctang_velocity_3.33
                    ; sta enemy_ram_pc,x
      
                    ; spawn settings go in:
                    ;   dart_velocity
                    ;   dart_x_origin
                    ;   dart_y_origin
                    ;   dart_dir_adjust
                    ;   dart_sprite
      
                dart_spawn: subroutine
                    ; put x,y origin in:
                    ; collision_0_x
                    ; collision_0_y
                .checks
                    ; dart_frame_max is set at top of enemy_handler
e7a8  a5 70         lda dart_frame_max
e7aa  f0 5f         beq .done
e7ac  c6 70         dec dart_frame_max
e7ae  20 5f dc      jsr get_enemy_slot_next
e7b1  90 58         bcc .done
                .spawn
e7b3  a9 0a         lda #dart_id
e7b5  9d 00 03      sta enemy_ram_type,x
e7b8  a9 00         lda #0
e7ba  9d 02 03      sta enemy_ram_x,x ; sub pixel pos
e7bd  9d 03 03      sta enemy_ram_y,x ; sub pixel pos
                    ; velocity
e7c0  a4 73         ldy dart_velocity
e7c2  b9 f0 ff      lda arctang_velocities_lo,y
e7c5  9d 04 03      sta enemy_ram_pc,x
                    ; get y register and set origin
e7c8  20 52 dc      jsr get_oam_offset_from_ram_offset
e7cb  a5 71         lda dart_x_origin
e7cd  99 03 02      sta oam_ram_x,y
e7d0  a5 72         lda dart_y_origin
e7d2  99 00 02      sta oam_ram_y,y
                    ; direction
e7d5  20 12 ce      jsr enemy_get_direction_of_player
e7d8  18            clc
e7d9  65 75         adc dart_dir_adjust
e7db  20 ae ce      jsr arctang_bound_dir
e7de  9d 07 03      sta enemy_ram_ex,x
                    ; sprite
e7e1  a5 74         lda dart_sprite
e7e3  d0 17         bne .sprite_custom
e7e5  bd 07 03      lda enemy_ram_ex,x
e7e8  aa            tax
e7e9  bd 0c e8      lda dart_direction_to_sprite_table,x
e7ec  18            clc
e7ed  69 b9         adc #$b9
e7ef  99 01 02      sta oam_ram_spr,y
                    ; attributes
e7f2  bd 24 e8      lda dart_direction_to_attribute_table,x
e7f5  09 01         ora #$01
e7f7  99 02 02      sta oam_ram_att,y
e7fa  d0 08         bne .sprite_done
                .sprite_custom
e7fc  99 01 02      sta oam_ram_spr,y
e7ff  a9 01         lda #$01
e801  99 02 02      sta oam_ram_att,y
                .sprite_done
                    ; sfx
e804  20 41 fa      jsr sfx_shoot_dart
                    ; register happy town
e807  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
e809  a4 77         ldy enemy_oam_offset
                .done
e80b  60            rts
      
      
                dart_direction_to_sprite_table:
e80c  byte          0,1,2,3,4,5,6,5,4,3,2,1
e818  byte          0,1,2,3,4,5,6,5,4,3,2,1
                dart_direction_to_attribute_table:
e824  byte          $00,$00,$00,$00,$00,$00
e82a  byte          $40,$40,$40,$40,$40,$40
e830  byte          $c0,$c0,$c0,$c0,$c0,$c0
e836  byte          $80,$80,$80,$80,$80,$80
      
      
                dart_cycle: subroutine
                    ; check for x despawn
e83c  b9 03 02      lda oam_ram_x,y
e83f  c9 03         cmp #$03
e841  90 21         bcc .despawn
e843  c9 fc         cmp #$fc
e845  b0 1d         bcs .despawn
                    ; check for player collision
e847  a9 04         lda #$04
e849  85 3d         sta collision_0_w
e84b  85 3e         sta collision_0_h
e84d  b9 03 02      lda oam_ram_x,y
e850  18            clc
e851  69 02         adc #$02
e853  85 3b         sta collision_0_x
e855  b9 00 02      lda oam_ram_y,y
e858  69 02         adc #$02
e85a  85 3c         sta collision_0_y
e85c  20 35 cf      jsr player_collision_detect
e85f  f0 09         beq .no_collision
e861  20 dd db      jsr enemy_gives_damage
                .despawn
e864  20 47 dd      jsr enemy_clear
e867  4c 79 e8      jmp .done
                .no_collision
                    ; handle direction movement
e86a  bd 04 03      lda enemy_ram_pc,x
e86d  85 84         sta arctang_velocity_lo
e86f  20 f6 ce      jsr arctang_enemy_update
                    ; check for y despawn
e872  b9 00 02      lda oam_ram_y,y
e875  c9 06         cmp #$06
e877  90 eb         bcc .despawn
                .done
e879  4c 92 db      jmp update_enemies_handler_next

enemy_galger.asm
      
      
                    ; flight path built on sine table circles
      
                    ; quadrants clockwise : x val , y val
                    ; 0 or 12-3 : $00-$40 , $c0-$00
                    ; 1 or 3-6  : $40-$80 , $00-$40
                    ; 2 or 6-9  : $80-$c0 , $40-$80
                    ; 3 or 9-12 : $c0-$00 , $80-$c0
      
                    ; state sequence -- comes in from top
                    ;   ; x,y origin ; quandrant(s) ; diameter x,y ; x,y origin diff
                    ; state1 ;  88,-48    ;       1      ; 96, 96      ;
                    ; state2 ; 124, 32    ;     2-3      ; 24, 16      ; +38, +80
                    ; state3 ; 120, 32    ;     0-1      ; 32, 32      ; -4, 0
                    ; state4 ; 104, 16    ;     2-3      ; 64, 48      ; -16, -16
                    ; state5 ; 104, 16    ;     0-1      ; 64, 60      ;  0, 0
                    ; state6 ;  88, 76    ; 3 in reverse ; 96, 52      ; -16, +60
                    ; state7 resets the pattern...   -8, 56      ; -96, -20
      
                    ; enemy_ram_pc = current arc position
                    ; enemy_ram_ac = arc position high byte
                    ; enemy_ram_ex = current arc sequence leg
      
                    ; before 1st leg translation === saves stuff in enemy ram
                    ; x, y origin: 184, 228  <-- saved in x, y
                    ; how many legs : 6  <-- saved in ex
                    ; initial arc position: $c0 <-- saved in pattern counter
      
      
      04 00         arc_leg_x_offset	EQU	$400
      04 01         arc_leg_y_offset	EQU	$401
      04 02         arc_leg_x_scale EQU $402
      04 03         arc_leg_y_scale EQU $403
      04 04         arc_leg_arc_start EQU $404
      04 05         arc_leg_arc_target EQU $405
      04 06         arc_leg_speed_inc EQU $406
      04 07         arc_leg_speed_dec EQU $407
      
                    ; height of playfield = 182px
      
                    ; 16 sequences with 46 steps total
                arc_sequence_begin:
e8d9  byte          $00, $05, $0b, $0f, $11, $13, $15, $1a, $1e, $21, $22, $23, $25, $27, $29, $2b
                arc_sequence_lengths:
e8e9  byte          $05, $06, $04, $02, $02, $02, $05, $04, $03, $01, $01, $02, $02, $02, $02, $03
                arc_sequence_x_origin:
e8f9  byte          $00, $a0, $80, $cd, $98, $35, $be, $d8, $22, $f8, $00, $cf, $ba, $e2, $c4, $80
                arc_sequence_y_origin:
e909  byte          $96, $ec, $2a, $10, $ac, $76, $9e, $a0, $a8, $5e, $32, $84, $78, $70, $8c, $34
                arc_sequences:
                    ; sequence 0 : Spiral Young Buck
e919  byte          $d2, $a0, $60, $60, $80, $40, $00, $02
e921  byte          $60, $10, $40, $40, $c0, $78, $04, $00
e929  byte          $10, $10, $30, $30, $80, $f8, $05, $00
e931  byte          $00, $00, $20, $20, $00, $78, $07, $00
e939  byte          $10, $10, $08, $08, $80, $f0, $10, $00
                    ; sequence 1 : Spiral O.G.
e941  byte          $a0, $ec, $60, $60, $40, $80, $03, $00
e949  byte          $26, $50, $18, $10, $80, $00, $07, $00
e951  byte          $fc, $00, $20, $20, $00, $80, $06, $00
e959  byte          $f0, $f0, $40, $30, $80, $00, $05, $00
e961  byte          $00, $00, $40, $3c, $00, $80, $04, $00
e969  byte          $f0, $3c, $60, $34, $00, $c0, $00, $03
                    ; sequence 2 : Big S
e971  byte          $80, $60, $70, $14, $bc, $80, $00, $04
e979  byte          $1c, $d2, $3c, $40, $7d, $03, $00, $04
e981  byte          $00, $c0, $3c, $40, $80, $fa, $04, $00
e989  byte          $dc, $00, $80, $c8, $00, $40, $02, $00
                    ; sequence 3 : Arc and Loop from Behind
e991  byte          $a8, $00, $ff, $eb, $00, $c0, $00, $01
e999  byte          $00, $5a, $40, $40, $c0, $06, $00, $04
                    ; sequence 4 : Butt Planet
e9a1  byte          $28, $b6, $de, $a4, $40, $05, $00, $01
e9a9  byte          $60, $f0, $20, $12, $80, $c0, $05, $00
                    ; sequence 5 : Loop Up from Below
e9b1  byte          $00, $c0, $40, $80, $80, $fa, $02, $00
e9b9  byte          $00, $00, $40, $40, $00, $7d, $03, $00
                    ; sequence 6 : Loop de Loop
e9c1  byte          $00, $00, $80, $10, $80, $c0, $03, $00
e9c9  byte          $80, $fc, $80, $10, $40, $02, $00, $03
e9d1  byte          $00, $81, $80, $80, $80, $7e, $02, $00
e9d9  byte          $00, $81, $80, $10, $00, $c0, $00, $03

                arc_leg: subroutine
                    ; set x position
      
eb0e  bd 04 03      lda enemy_ram_pc,x

eb1d  18            clc
eb1e  7d 02 03      adc enemy_ram_x,x
eb21  99 03 02      sta oam_ram_x,y
      
                    ; set y position
eb24  bd 04 03      lda enemy_ram_pc,x
eb27  18            clc
eb28  69 c0         adc #$c0
eb2a  85 86         sta temp00
eb2c  bd 03 04      lda arc_leg_y_scale,x
eb2f  a6 86         ldx temp00
eb31  20 ad d4      jsr sine_of_scale
      
eb34  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
eb36  18            clc
eb37  7d 03 03      adc enemy_ram_y,x
eb3a  99 00 02      sta oam_ram_y,y
      
eb3d  c9 f0         cmp #240
eb3f  90 11         bcc .y_under_240
eb41  38            sec
eb42  e9 4a         sbc #sprite_0_y_diff
eb44  99 00 02      sta oam_ram_y,y
eb47  bd 03 03      lda enemy_ram_y,x
eb4a  38            sec
eb4b  e9 4a         sbc #sprite_0_y_diff
eb4d  9d 03 03      sta enemy_ram_y,x
eb50  b0 13         bcs .y_check_done
                .y_under_240
eb52  c9 b6         cmp #sprite_0_y
eb54  90 0f         bcc .y_check_done
eb56  18            clc
eb57  69 4a         adc #sprite_0_y_diff
eb59  99 00 02      sta oam_ram_y,y
eb5c  bd 03 03      lda enemy_ram_y,x
eb5f  18            clc
eb60  69 4a         adc #sprite_0_y_diff
eb62  9d 03 03      sta enemy_ram_y,x
                .y_check_done
      
                    ; forward arc position
eb65  bd 05 03      lda enemy_ram_ac,x
eb68  f0 22         beq .speed_handle_normal
                .speed_handle_abnormal
eb6a  bd 06 04      lda arc_leg_speed_inc,x
eb6d  c9 00         cmp #$00
eb6f  f0 0b         beq .speed_ab_dec
                .speed_ab_inc
eb71  18            clc
eb72  7d 04 03      adc enemy_ram_pc,x
eb75  9d 04 03      sta enemy_ram_pc,x
eb78  90 11         bcc .speed_ab_done
eb7a  b0 0c         bcs .speed_dec_ac
                .speed_ab_dec
eb7c  bd 04 03      lda enemy_ram_pc,x
eb7f  38            sec
eb80  fd 07 04      sbc arc_leg_speed_dec,x
eb83  9d 04 03      sta enemy_ram_pc,x
eb86  b0 03         bcs .speed_ab_done
                .speed_dec_ac
eb88  de 05 03      dec enemy_ram_ac,x
                .speed_ab_done
eb8b  60            rts
      
      
                .speed_handle_normal
eb8c  bd 06 04      lda arc_leg_speed_inc,x
eb8f  c9 00         cmp #$00
eb91  f0 0d         beq .speed_dec
                .speed_inc
eb93  18            clc
eb94  7d 04 03      adc enemy_ram_pc,x
eb97  9d 04 03      sta enemy_ram_pc,x
eb9a  dd 05 04      cmp arc_leg_arc_target,x
eb9d  b0 11         bcs .next_leg
eb9f  60            rts
                .speed_dec
eba0  bd 04 03      lda enemy_ram_pc,x
eba3  38            sec
eba4  fd 07 04      sbc arc_leg_speed_dec,x
eba7  9d 04 03      sta enemy_ram_pc,x
ebaa  dd 05 04      cmp arc_leg_arc_target,x
ebad  90 01         bcc .next_leg
ebaf  60            rts
      
                .next_leg
                arc_leg_init:
ebb0  bd 07 03      lda enemy_ram_ex,x
                    ; update arc data in enemy slot
ebb3  0a            asl
ebb4  0a            asl
ebb5  0a            asl
ebb6  a8            tay
ebb7  b1 81         lda (arc_sequence_lo),y
ebb9  9d 00 04      sta arc_leg_x_offset,x
ebbc  c8            iny
ebbd  b1 81         lda (arc_sequence_lo),y
ebbf  9d 01 04      sta arc_leg_y_offset,x
ebc2  c8            iny
ebc3  b1 81         lda (arc_sequence_lo),y
ebc5  9d 02 04      sta arc_leg_x_scale,x
ebc8  c8            iny
ebc9  b1 81         lda (arc_sequence_lo),y
ebcb  9d 03 04      sta arc_leg_y_scale,x
ebce  c8            iny
ebcf  b1 81         lda (arc_sequence_lo),y
ebd1  9d 04 04      sta arc_leg_arc_start,x
ebd4  9d 04 03      sta enemy_ram_pc,x
ebd7  c8            iny
ebd8  b1 81         lda (arc_sequence_lo),y
ebda  9d 05 04      sta arc_leg_arc_target,x
ebdd  c8            iny
ebde  b1 81         lda (arc_sequence_lo),y
ebe0  9d 06 04      sta arc_leg_speed_inc,x
ebe3  c8            iny
ebe4  b1 81         lda (arc_sequence_lo),y
ebe6  9d 07 04      sta arc_leg_speed_dec,x
ebe9  a4 77         ldy enemy_oam_offset
                    ; update x origin
ebeb  bd 02 03      lda enemy_ram_x,x
ebee  18            clc
ebef  7d 00 04      adc arc_leg_x_offset,x
ebf2  9d 02 03      sta enemy_ram_x,x
                    ; update y origin
ebf5  bd 03 03      lda enemy_ram_y,x
ebf8  18            clc
ebf9  7d 01 04      adc arc_leg_y_offset,x
ebfc  9d 03 03      sta enemy_ram_y,x
                    ; update arc origin
ebff  bd 04 04      lda arc_leg_arc_start,x
ec02  9d 04 03      sta enemy_ram_pc,x
ec05  bd 06 04      lda arc_leg_speed_inc,x
ec08  c9 00         cmp #$00
ec0a  f0 0d         beq .check_speed_dec_count
                .check_speed_inc_count
ec0c  bd 04 04      lda arc_leg_arc_start,x
ec0f  dd 05 04      cmp arc_leg_arc_target,x
ec12  90 10         bcc .arc_leg_done
ec14  fe 05 03      inc enemy_ram_ac,x
ec17  d0 0b         bne .arc_leg_done
                .check_speed_dec_count
ec19  bd 04 04      lda arc_leg_arc_start,x
ec1c  dd 05 04      cmp arc_leg_arc_target,x
ec1f  b0 03         bcs .arc_leg_done
ec21  fe 05 03      inc enemy_ram_ac,x
                .arc_leg_done
ec24  fe 07 03      inc enemy_ram_ex,x
ec27  a5 83         lda arc_sequence_length
ec29  dd 07 03      cmp enemy_ram_ex,x
ec2c  d0 05         bne .dont_wrap_sequence
ec2e  a9 00         lda #0
ec30  9d 07 03      sta enemy_ram_ex,x
                .dont_wrap_sequence
ec33  60            rts

ee0c  20 eb db      jsr enemy_handle_damage_and_death
      
                    ; x pos
ee0f  b9 03 02      lda oam_ram_x,y
ee12  99 07 02      sta oam_ram_x+4,y
      
                    ; y pos
ee15  20 fb dc      jsr enemy_accelerates_upwards
ee18  b0 0b         bcs .reset_x_and_y_pos
ee1a  99 00 02      sta oam_ram_y,y
ee1d  18            clc
ee1e  69 08         adc #$08
ee20  99 04 02      sta oam_ram_y+4,y
ee23  d0 13         bne .reset_skip
                .reset_x_and_y_pos
                    ; x
ee25  20 52 c1      jsr get_next_random
ee28  4a            lsr
ee29  69 5c         adc #$5c
ee2b  99 03 02      sta oam_ram_x,y
                    ; y
ee2e  a9 b4         lda #muya_spawn_y
ee30  99 00 02      sta oam_ram_y,y
                    ; reset animation counter
ee33  a9 00         lda #$00
ee35  9d 05 03      sta enemy_ram_ac,x
                .reset_skip
      
                    ; sprite
ee38  a9 64         lda #$64
ee3a  99 01 02      sta oam_ram_spr,y
ee3d  a9 74         lda #$74
ee3f  99 05 02      sta oam_ram_spr+4,y
      
                    ; palette
ee42  a9 02         lda #$02
ee44  20 39 dc      jsr enemy_set_palette
ee47  99 06 02      sta oam_ram_att+4,y
      
ee4a  4c 92 db      jmp update_enemies_handler_next
      
      

enemy_skeet.asm
      
                    ; skeet is a buggar that moves in curves
                    ;
                    ; ac used for both tiles and cycle counter
                    ; pc 2bits x velocity / 2bits y velocity
                    ; ex holds 4-way direction
      
                skeet_att_table:
                    ;     r/u   l/u   l/d  r/d
ee4d  byte          #$a2, #$d2, #$62, #$22
                skeet_vel_table:
ee51  byte          #0, #5, #6, #1, #6, #5
      
                skeet_spawn: subroutine
                    ; set direction
ee57  a5 27         lda rng1
ee59  29 02         and #$02
ee5b  d0 04         bne .downward
                .upward
ee5d  a9 06         lda #$06
ee5f  d0 02         bne .set_updown
                .downward
ee61  a9 12         lda #18
                .set_updown
ee63  9d 07 03      sta enemy_ram_ex,x
                    ; set position
ee66  4c 1a db      jmp enemy_spawn_set_x_y_rng
      
      
                skeet_cycle: subroutine
ee69  a9 08         lda #$08
ee6b  85 3d         sta collision_0_w
ee6d  85 3e         sta collision_0_h
ee6f  20 eb db      jsr enemy_handle_damage_and_death
      
                    ; reset direction?
ee72  bd 05 03      lda enemy_ram_ac,x
ee75  d0 16         bne .skip_reset
ee77  a9 40         lda #$40
ee79  9d 05 03      sta enemy_ram_ac,x
ee7c  20 52 c1      jsr get_next_random
ee7f  10 07         bpl .dir_inc
                .dir_dec
ee81  a9 ff         lda #$ff
ee83  9d 04 03      sta enemy_ram_pc,x
ee86  d0 05         bne .skip_reset
                .dir_inc
ee88  a9 01         lda #$01
ee8a  9d 04 03      sta enemy_ram_pc,x
                .skip_reset
ee8d  de 05 03      dec enemy_ram_ac,x
ee90  bd 05 03      lda enemy_ram_ac,x
ee93  29 03         and #$03
ee95  d0 0d         bne .skip_dir_change
ee97  bd 07 03      lda enemy_ram_ex,x
ee9a  18            clc
ee9b  7d 04 03      adc enemy_ram_pc,x
ee9e  20 ae ce      jsr arctang_bound_dir
eea1  9d 07 03      sta enemy_ram_ex,x
                .skip_dir_change
                    ; velocity
eea4  bd 07 03      lda enemy_ram_ex,x
eea7  4a            lsr
eea8  4a            lsr
eea9  aa            tax
eeaa  bd 51 ee      lda skeet_vel_table,x
eead  aa            tax
eeae  bd f0 ff      lda arctang_velocities_lo,x
eeb1  85 84         sta arctang_velocity_lo
eeb3  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
eeb5  20 f6 ce      jsr arctang_enemy_update
                    ; sprite
eeb8  bd 05 03      lda enemy_ram_ac,x
eebb  4a            lsr
eebc  aa            tax
eebd  bd 00 fe      lda sine_table,x
eec0  29 01         and #1
eec2  18            clc
eec3  69 4e         adc #$4e ; base sprite tile
eec5  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
eec7  99 01 02      sta oam_ram_spr,y
                    ; attr
eeca  bd 07 03      lda enemy_ram_ex,x
eecd  69 04         adc #$04
eecf  4a            lsr
eed0  4a            lsr
eed1  4a            lsr
eed2  aa            tax
eed3  bd 4d ee      lda skeet_att_table,x
eed6  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
eed8  20 39 dc      jsr enemy_set_palette
      
eedb  4c 92 db      jmp update_enemies_handler_next
      
      

enemy_skully.asm
      
      
                skully_spawn: subroutine
                    ; x is set by enemy spawner
eede  a5 26         lda rng0
eee0  9d 05 03      sta enemy_ram_ac,x ; animation counter
eee3  20 d1 db      jsr enemy_spawn_random_y_pos
eee6  9d 03 03      sta enemy_ram_y,x ; y pos
eee9  60            rts
      
      
      
                    ;;;; HANDLING SKULLY
                skully_cycle: subroutine
eeea  a9 10         lda #$10
eeec  85 3d         sta collision_0_w
eeee  85 3e         sta collision_0_h
eef0  20 eb db      jsr enemy_handle_damage_and_death
      
eef3  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
eef5  a4 77         ldy enemy_oam_offset
eef7  a9 01         lda #$01 ; set mirror flag
eef9  9d 07 03      sta enemy_ram_ex,x
                    ; let's find what frame we're on
eefc  bd 05 03      lda enemy_ram_ac,x
eeff  4a            lsr
ef00  4a            lsr
ef01  4a            lsr
ef02  4a            lsr
ef03  4a            lsr
ef04  0a            asl
                    ; accumulator is now in 0..7 range except x2
                    ; sprite
ef05  20 b7 da      jsr sprite_4_set_sprite
                    ; x pos
ef08  bd 02 03      lda enemy_ram_x,x
ef0b  20 df da      jsr sprite_4_set_x
                    ; y pos
ef0e  bd 03 03      lda enemy_ram_y,x
ef11  20 ef da      jsr sprite_4_set_y
                    ; update spinning counter
ef14  a9 07         lda #$07
ef16  18            clc
ef17  7d 05 03      adc enemy_ram_ac,x
ef1a  9d 05 03      sta enemy_ram_ac,x
                    ; move skully
ef1d  20 28 ef      jsr skully_handle_movement
                    ; palette
ef20  a9 03         lda #$03
ef22  20 9d da      jsr sprite_4_set_palette
                .skully_done
ef25  4c 92 db      jmp update_enemies_handler_next
      
      
                skully_handle_movement: subroutine
ef28  a5 0e         lda attract_true
ef2a  d0 3e         bne .demoshit
ef2c  a9 69         lda #$69
ef2e  bd 04 03      lda enemy_ram_pc,x
ef31  c9 40         cmp #$40
ef33  d0 1d         bne .not_chasing
                    ; CHASE MODE
ef35  de 02 03      dec enemy_ram_x,x
ef38  a5 24         lda wtf
ef3a  29 03         and #$03
ef3c  d0 2f         bne .done
ef3e  bd 03 03      lda enemy_ram_y,x
ef41  18            clc
ef42  69 09         adc #$09
ef44  c5 15         cmp player_y_hi
ef46  f0 25         beq .done
ef48  90 04         bcc .down
                .up
ef4a  de 03 03      dec enemy_ram_y,x
ef4d  60            rts
                .down
ef4e  fe 03 03      inc enemy_ram_y,x
ef51  60            rts
                .not_chasing
                    ; ZOOM MODE
ef52  a9 20         lda #$20
ef54  9d 04 03      sta enemy_ram_pc,x
ef57  a9 04         lda #$04
ef59  18            clc
ef5a  7d 02 03      adc enemy_ram_x,x
ef5d  9d 02 03      sta enemy_ram_x,x
ef60  c9 f0         cmp #240
ef62  90 06         bcc .demoshit
ef64  a9 40         lda #$40
ef66  9d 04 03      sta enemy_ram_pc,x
ef69  60            rts
                .demoshit
                    ; DEMO MODE
ef6a  fe 02 03      inc enemy_ram_x,x
                .done
ef6d  60            rts
      

enemy_spark.asm
      
                    ;  U     R     D    L
                spark_dir_x_table:
ef6e  byte          #$00, #$01, #$00, #$ff
                spark_dir_y_table:
ef72  byte          #$ff, #$00, #$01, #$00
      
                spark_spawn: subroutine
                    ; x = slot in enemy ram
                    ; y = boss slot in enemy ram
ef76  20 52 c1      jsr get_next_random
ef79  9d 02 03      sta enemy_ram_x,x
ef7c  a9 04         lda #4
ef7e  9d 03 03      sta enemy_ram_y,x
ef81  a9 00         lda #0
ef83  9d 07 03      sta enemy_ram_ex,x
ef86  60            rts
      
                spark_cycle: subroutine
ef87  a9 08         lda #$08
ef89  85 3d         sta collision_0_w
ef8b  85 3e         sta collision_0_h
ef8d  20 eb db      jsr enemy_handle_damage_and_death
                    ; change direction?
ef90  a5 24         lda wtf
ef92  29 07         and #$07
ef94  d0 12         bne .dont_reset_dir
                    ; 50% chance of downward movement
ef96  20 52 c1      jsr get_next_random
ef99  10 04         bpl .not_hard_down
ef9b  a9 02         lda #$02
ef9d  d0 06         bne .set_dir
                .not_hard_down
                    ; otherwise random 4 directions
ef9f  20 52 c1      jsr get_next_random
efa2  4a            lsr
efa3  29 03         and #%00000011
                .set_dir
efa5  9d 07 03      sta enemy_ram_ex,x
                .reset_done
                .dont_reset_dir
                    ; apply direction
efa8  bc 07 03      ldy enemy_ram_ex,x
efab  b9 6e ef      lda spark_dir_x_table,y
efae  85 86         sta temp00
efb0  b9 72 ef      lda spark_dir_y_table,y
efb3  85 87         sta temp01
efb5  a4 77         ldy enemy_oam_offset
efb7  bd 02 03      lda enemy_ram_x,x
efba  18            clc
efbb  65 86         adc temp00
efbd  9d 02 03      sta enemy_ram_x,x
efc0  99 03 02      sta oam_ram_x,y
efc3  bd 03 03      lda enemy_ram_y,x
efc6  18            clc
efc7  65 87         adc temp01
efc9  20 ff da      jsr enemy_fix_y_visible
efcc  9d 03 03      sta enemy_ram_y,x
efcf  99 00 02      sta oam_ram_y,y
                    ; attributes
efd2  20 52 c1      jsr get_next_random
efd5  99 02 02      sta oam_ram_att,y
                    ; sprite
efd8  20 52 c1      jsr get_next_random
efdb  29 03         and #$03
efdd  18            clc
efde  69 6c         adc #$6c
efe0  99 01 02      sta oam_ram_spr,y
                .done
efe3  4c 92 db      jmp update_enemies_handler_next

                .x_decide_action
                    ; calc x
f00b  bd 02 03      lda enemy_ram_x,x
f00e  c9 10         cmp #$10

f012  fe 02 03      inc enemy_ram_x,x
f015  bd 02 03      lda enemy_ram_x,x

                .x_sine_pattern
                    ; using pattern_counter for x sin
f01e  fe 04 03      inc enemy_ram_pc,x

f024  aa            tax

f028  4a            lsr
f029  4a            lsr
f02a  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
f02c  18            clc

                .y_calc_pos
                    ; using anim_counter for y sin

f036  fe 05 03      inc enemy_ram_ac,x

f039  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
f03b  bd 05 03      lda enemy_ram_ac,x
f03e  aa            tax
f03f  bd 00 fe      lda sine_table,x
f042  4a            lsr
f043  4a            lsr
f044  18            clc
f045  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
f047  7d 03 03      adc enemy_ram_y,x
f04a  20 ef da      jsr sprite_4_set_y
                    ; tiles
f04d  a6 76         ldx enemy_ram_offset
f04f  bd 05 03      lda enemy_ram_ac,x
f052  29 40         and #$40
f054  f0 04         beq .frame1
                .frame0
f056  a9 7a         lda #$7a
f058  d0 02         bne .frame_done
                .frame1
f05a  a9 7c         lda #$7c
                .frame_done
f05c  20 b7 da      jsr sprite_4_set_sprite
f05f  a9 03         lda #$03
f061  20 9d da      jsr sprite_4_set_palette
                .done
